# Patch 1 — Add consolidated hub

## New file: `frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterToolsHub.tsx`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterToolsHub.tsx b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterToolsHub.tsx
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterToolsHub.tsx
@@
+import React from 'react';
+import { WriterLayersPanel } from './WriterLayersPanel';
+import { WriterQuickTemplatesPanel } from './WriterQuickTemplatesPanel';
+import { WriterObjectSearchPanel } from './WriterObjectSearchPanel';
+
+type WriterToolsTab = 'objects' | 'templates' | 'search';
+
+const STORAGE_KEY = 'writer-v2-tools-hub';
+
+export const WriterToolsHub: React.FC = () => {
+  const initial = React.useMemo(() => {
+    try {
+      const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
+
+      if (!raw) {
+        return {
+          collapsed: false,
+          tab: 'objects' as WriterToolsTab,
+        };
+      }
+
+      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as {
+        collapsed?: boolean;
+        tab?: WriterToolsTab;
+      };
+
+      return {
+        collapsed: parsed.collapsed ?? false,
+        tab: parsed.tab ?? 'objects',
+      };
+    } catch {
+      return {
+        collapsed: false,
+        tab: 'objects' as WriterToolsTab,
+      };
+    }
+  }, []);
+
+  const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = React.useState(initial.collapsed);
+  const [tab, setTab] = React.useState<WriterToolsTab>(initial.tab);
+
+  React.useEffect(() => {
+    localStorage.setItem(
+      STORAGE_KEY,
+      JSON.stringify({
+        collapsed,
+        tab,
+      }),
+    );
+  }, [collapsed, tab]);
+
+  if (collapsed) {
+    return (
+      <button
+        type="button"
+        data-writer-tools-hub-collapsed="true"
+        aria-label="Expand Writer tools"
+        onClick={() => setCollapsed(false)}
+        style={{
+          position: 'fixed',
+          right: 18,
+          top: 96,
+          zIndex: 230,
+          height: 34,
+          padding: '0 12px',
+          borderRadius: 999,
+          border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
+          background: '#ffffff',
+          color: '#0f172a',
+          fontSize: 12,
+          fontWeight: 800,
+          boxShadow: '0 8px 24px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.18)',
+          cursor: 'pointer',
+        }}
+      >
+        Writer Tools
+      </button>
+    );
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <aside
+      data-writer-tools-hub="true"
+      aria-label="Writer tools"
+      style={{
+        position: 'fixed',
+        right: 18,
+        top: 92,
+        zIndex: 230,
+        width: 340,
+        maxHeight: 'calc(100vh - 120px)',
+        display: 'flex',
+        flexDirection: 'column',
+        background: '#ffffff',
+        border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
+        borderRadius: 12,
+        boxShadow: '0 16px 44px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.22)',
+        overflow: 'hidden',
+      }}
+    >
+      <header
+        style={{
+          height: 40,
+          padding: '0 10px',
+          display: 'flex',
+          alignItems: 'center',
+          gap: 8,
+          borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
+          background: '#f8fafc',
+        }}
+      >
+        <strong
+          style={{
+            flex: 1,
+            minWidth: 0,
+            fontSize: 13,
+            color: '#0f172a',
+          }}
+        >
+          Writer Tools
+        </strong>
+
+        <button
+          type="button"
+          onClick={() => setCollapsed(true)}
+          aria-label="Collapse Writer tools"
+          title="Collapse"
+          style={headerButtonStyle}
+        >
+          −
+        </button>
+      </header>
+
+      <nav
+        aria-label="Writer tool tabs"
+        style={{
+          display: 'grid',
+          gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr 1fr',
+          gap: 4,
+          padding: 8,
+          borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
+          background: '#ffffff',
+        }}
+      >
+        <TabButton active={tab === 'objects'} onClick={() => setTab('objects')}>
+          Objects
+        </TabButton>
+        <TabButton active={tab === 'templates'} onClick={() => setTab('templates')}>
+          Templates
+        </TabButton>
+        <TabButton active={tab === 'search'} onClick={() => setTab('search')}>
+          Search
+        </TabButton>
+      </nav>
+
+      <section
+        style={{
+          flex: 1,
+          minHeight: 0,
+          overflow: 'auto',
+          background: '#ffffff',
+        }}
+      >
+        {tab === 'objects' ? <WriterLayersPanel embedded /> : null}
+        {tab === 'templates' ? <WriterQuickTemplatesPanel embedded /> : null}
+        {tab === 'search' ? <WriterObjectSearchPanel embedded /> : null}
+      </section>
+    </aside>
+  );
+};
+
+function TabButton({
+  active,
+  onClick,
+  children,
+}: {
+  active: boolean;
+  onClick: () => void;
+  children: React.ReactNode;
+}) {
+  return (
+    <button
+      type="button"
+      onClick={onClick}
+      aria-pressed={active}
+      style={{
+        height: 28,
+        borderRadius: 7,
+        border: active ? '1px solid #2563eb' : '1px solid #cbd5e1',
+        background: active ? '#eff6ff' : '#ffffff',
+        color: active ? '#1d4ed8' : '#334155',
+        fontSize: 11,
+        fontWeight: 800,
+        cursor: 'pointer',
+      }}
+    >
+      {children}
+    </button>
+  );
+}
+
+const headerButtonStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
+  width: 26,
+  height: 26,
+  borderRadius: 7,
+  border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
+  background: '#ffffff',
+  color: '#334155',
+  cursor: 'pointer',
+  fontSize: 16,
+  lineHeight: 1,
+};
```

# Patch 6 — Add hub test

## New file: `frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterToolsHub.test.tsx`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterToolsHub.test.tsx b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterToolsHub.test.tsx
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterToolsHub.test.tsx
@@
+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import { fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
+import type { WriterElementV2 } from '@/core/writer-v2/types';
+import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';
+import { useWriterV2Store } from '@/core/writer-v2/writerV2Store';
+import { WriterToolsHub } from './WriterToolsHub';
+
+function element(
+  id: string,
+  patch: Partial<WriterElementV2> = {},
+): WriterElementV2 {
+  return {
+    id,
+    documentFingerprint: 'doc-1',
+    pageIndex: 0,
+    type: 'richTextBox',
+    rect: { x: 10, y: 20, width: 100, height: 40 },
+    plainText: 'Writer object',
+    editorStateJson: '{"root":{"children":[]}}',
+    style: {
+      zIndex: 1,
+      backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
+      borderColor: '#111827',
+      borderWidth: 1,
+      padding: 8,
+      opacity: 1,
+    },
+    createdAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
+    updatedAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
+    locked: false,
+    hidden: false,
+    ...patch,
+  };
+}
+
+describe('WriterToolsHub', () => {
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    localStorage.clear();
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().clearWriterV2();
+    useSessionStore.getState().openDocument({
+      documentKey: 'doc-1',
+      fileName: 'sample.pdf',
+      bytes: new Uint8Array([37, 80, 68, 70]),
+      pageCount: 1,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('renders one consolidated Writer tools hub', () => {
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().hydrateWriterSession({
+      documentFingerprint: 'doc-1',
+      elements: [element('writer-1')],
+    });
+
+    render(<WriterToolsHub />);
+
+    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Writer tools')).toBeTruthy();
+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Objects' })).toBeTruthy();
+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Templates' })).toBeTruthy();
+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Search' })).toBeTruthy();
+  });
+
+  it('switches tabs', () => {
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().hydrateWriterSession({
+      documentFingerprint: 'doc-1',
+      elements: [element('writer-1')],
+    });
+
+    render(<WriterToolsHub />);
+
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Templates' }));
+    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Writer V2 quick templates')).toBeTruthy();
+
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Search' }));
+    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Writer V2 object search')).toBeTruthy();
+  });
+
+  it('collapses and expands', () => {
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().hydrateWriterSession({
+      documentFingerprint: 'doc-1',
+      elements: [element('writer-1')],
+    });
+
+    render(<WriterToolsHub />);
+
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('Collapse Writer tools'));
+    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Expand Writer tools')).toBeTruthy();
+
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('Expand Writer tools'));
+    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Writer tools')).toBeTruthy();
+  });
+});
```

-------------------------------------------
Below is the **next coding slice**: make the existing Writer ribbon **actually functional when no rich text box is active**.

Root problem from repo check:

```txt
WriterRibbon already has table/image/heading logic, but it depends on activeEditor.
activeEditor only exists after the user double-clicks into a Writer object.
So ribbon icons look available, but many actions silently do nothing.
```

The repo confirms:

* `WriterRibbon.tsx` already imports/uses `activeEditor`, table insertion, and image node creation. 
* `WriterInlineEditor.tsx` already registers `TableNode`, `TableRowNode`, `TableCellNode`, `ImageNode`, `HeadingNode`, list/link/code nodes. 
* `ImageNode` helper is currently `$createImageNode(src, altText, width?, height?, caption?)`. 
* `insertTable(editor, rows, cols)` already exists and inserts a Lexical table at cursor. 

I cannot honestly claim local tests passed from this chat runtime because I do not have a runnable checkout with npm dependencies here. The patch is test-gated below.

# WR2-RICH.2 — Smart Rich Text Activation

## What this patch does

```txt
Click H1/H2/H3/Table/Image without active editor
→ find selected rich Writer object
→ begin editing it
→ queue command
→ inline editor opens
→ queued command runs

If no rich Writer object exists
→ create one on current page
→ begin editing it
→ queued command runs
```

This directly fixes the “icons are just icons” issue.

---

# Patch 1 — Add command queue

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.ts
@@
+import { create } from 'zustand';
+
+export type PendingRichEditorCommand =
+  | {
+      type: 'insertTable';
+      rows: number;
+      cols: number;
+    }
+  | {
+      type: 'insertImage';
+      src: string;
+      altText: string;
+      width?: number | 'inherit';
+      height?: number | 'inherit';
+      caption?: string;
+    }
+  | {
+      type: 'applyHeading';
+      level: 1 | 2 | 3;
+    }
+  | {
+      type: 'focus';
+    };
+
+interface RichEditorCommandQueueState {
+  pending: PendingRichEditorCommand[];
+  enqueue: (command: PendingRichEditorCommand) => void;
+  drain: () => PendingRichEditorCommand[];
+  clear: () => void;
+}
+
+export const useRichEditorCommandQueue = create<RichEditorCommandQueueState>((set, get) => ({
+  pending: [],
+  enqueue: (command) =>
+    set((state) => ({
+      pending: [...state.pending, command],
+    })),
+  drain: () => {
+    const pending = get().pending;
+    set({ pending: [] });
+    return pending;
+  },
+  clear: () => set({ pending: [] }),
+}));
```

# Patch 2 — Add smart rich box activation

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/ensureRichTextEditing.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/ensureRichTextEditing.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/ensureRichTextEditing.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/ensureRichTextEditing.ts
@@
+import type { WriterElementV2 } from './types';
+import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';
+import { useWriterV2Store } from './writerV2Store';
+import { createRichTextBoxElement } from './writerElementFactory';
+
+const RICH_EDITABLE_TYPES = new Set<WriterElementV2['type']>([
+  'richTextBox',
+  'textCallout',
+  'richNote',
+]);
+
+export async function ensureRichTextEditing(): Promise<string | null> {
+  const writer = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+  const session = useSessionStore.getState();
+
+  const selected = writer.elements.find((element) =>
+    (writer.activeId === element.id || writer.selectedIds.includes(element.id)) &&
+    RICH_EDITABLE_TYPES.has(element.type) &&
+    !element.locked &&
+    !element.hidden,
+  );
+
+  if (selected) {
+    writer.beginEditingWriterElement(selected.id);
+    return selected.id;
+  }
+
+  const sourceBytes = session.workingBytes ?? session.originalBytes;
+
+  if (!sourceBytes || session.pageCount <= 0) {
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  const { PDFDocument } = await import('pdf-lib');
+  const pdf = await PDFDocument.load(sourceBytes);
+  const pageIndex = Math.max(
+    0,
+    Math.min(session.viewState.currentPage - 1, pdf.getPageCount() - 1),
+  );
+  const page = pdf.getPage(pageIndex);
+  const pageSize = page.getSize();
+
+  const maxZIndex = writer.elements.reduce(
+    (max, element) => Math.max(max, element.style.zIndex),
+    0,
+  );
+
+  const element = createRichTextBoxElement({
+    documentFingerprint:
+      writer.currentDocumentFingerprint ??
+      session.documentKey ??
+      session.fileName ??
+      'writer-v2-unsaved-document',
+    pageIndex,
+    rect: {
+      x: 54,
+      y: Math.max(54, pageSize.height - 260),
+      width: Math.min(520, pageSize.width - 108),
+      height: 190,
+    },
+    text: '',
+    zIndex: maxZIndex + 1,
+  });
+
+  writer.dispatchWriterCommand({
+    type: 'ADD_WRITER_ELEMENT',
+    element,
+  });
+
+  useWriterV2Store.getState().beginEditingWriterElement(element.id);
+
+  return element.id;
+}
```

# Patch 3 — Drain queued commands inside inline editor

## File

`frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterInlineEditor.tsx`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterInlineEditor.tsx b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterInlineEditor.tsx
--- a/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterInlineEditor.tsx
+++ b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterInlineEditor.tsx
@@
-import type { EditorState, LexicalEditor } from 'lexical';
-import { $getRoot } from 'lexical';
+import type { EditorState, LexicalEditor } from 'lexical';
+import { $getRoot, $insertNodes } from 'lexical';
@@
-import { ImageNode } from '@/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/ImageNode';
-import { HeadingNode } from '@/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/headingHelpers';
+import {
+  $createImageNode,
+  ImageNode,
+} from '@/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/ImageNode';
+import {
+  HeadingNode,
+  toggleHeading,
+} from '@/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/headingHelpers';
+import { insertTable } from '@/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableHelpers';
+import { useRichEditorCommandQueue } from '@/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue';
@@
         <ActiveEditorRegisterPlugin
           onEditor={(editor) => {
             editorRef.current = editor;
             // Focus the editor after registration so the user can type immediately
             // without needing an extra click.
             requestAnimationFrame(() => {
               editor.focus();
             });
           }}

+        <QueuedRichEditorCommandPlugin />
 
         <OnChangePlugin
           onChange={(editorState) => {
             latestStateRef.current = editorState;
@@
 function ActiveEditorRegisterPlugin({
@@
 
   return null;
 }
+
+function QueuedRichEditorCommandPlugin(): null {
+  const [editor] = useLexicalComposerContext();
+
+  React.useEffect(() => {
+    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
+      const commands = useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().drain();
+
+      if (commands.length === 0) {
+        return;
+      }
+
+      editor.focus(() => {
+        for (const command of commands) {
+          switch (command.type) {
+            case 'insertTable':
+              insertTable(editor, command.rows, command.cols);
+              break;
+
+            case 'insertImage':
+              editor.update(() => {
+                $insertNodes([
+                  $createImageNode(
+                    command.src,
+                    command.altText,
+                    command.width,
+                    command.height,
+                    command.caption,
+                  ),
+                ]);
+              });
+              break;
+
+            case 'applyHeading':
+              toggleHeading(editor, command.level);
+              break;
+
+            case 'focus':
+              break;
+          }
+        }
+      });
+    }, 0);
+
+    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
+  }, [editor]);
+
+  return null;
+}
```

```diff
   const runWithFocusedEditor = React.useCallback((
     operation: (editor: LexicalEditor) => void,
   ): void => {
     const editor = activeEditor;
     if (!editor) return;

       operation(editor);

+
+  const runOrQueueRichCommand = React.useCallback(async (
+    command: PendingRichEditorCommand,
+    immediate: (editor: LexicalEditor) => void,
+  ): Promise<void> => {
+    if (activeEditor) {
+      runWithFocusedEditor(immediate);
+      return;
+    }
+
+    useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().enqueue(command);
+    await ensureRichTextEditing();
+  }, [activeEditor, runWithFocusedEditor]);
```

# Patch 5 — Tests for queue

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.test.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.test.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.test.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.test.ts
@@
+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import { useRichEditorCommandQueue } from './richEditorCommandQueue';
+
+describe('richEditorCommandQueue', () => {
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().clear();
+  });
+
+  it('enqueues and drains commands in order', () => {
+    useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().enqueue({
+      type: 'applyHeading',
+      level: 1,
+    });
+    useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().enqueue({
+      type: 'insertTable',
+      rows: 2,
+      cols: 3,
+    });
+
+    expect(useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().pending).toHaveLength(2);
+
+    const drained = useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().drain();
+
+    expect(drained).toEqual([
+      {
+        type: 'applyHeading',
+        level: 1,
+      },
+      {
+        type: 'insertTable',
+        rows: 2,
+        cols: 3,
+      },
+    ]);
+    expect(useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().pending).toEqual([]);
+  });
+
+  it('clears queued commands', () => {
+    useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().enqueue({
+      type: 'focus',
+    });
+
+    useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().clear();
+
+    expect(useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().pending).toEqual([]);
+  });
+});
```

```diff
+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

+import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';
+import { useWriterV2Store } from './writerV2Store';
+import { ensureRichTextEditing } from './ensureRichTextEditing';
+
+async function samplePdf(): Promise<Uint8Array> {
+  const pdf = await PDFDocument.create();
+  pdf.addPage([612, 792]);
+  return pdf.save();
+}
+
+function element(
+  id: string,
+  patch: Partial<WriterElementV2> = {},
+): WriterElementV2 {
+  return {
+    id,
+    documentFingerprint: 'doc-1',
+    pageIndex: 0,
+    type: 'richTextBox',
+    rect: { x: 10, y: 20, width: 100, height: 40 },
+    plainText: 'Existing box',
+    editorStateJson: '{"root":{"children":[]}}',
+    style: {
+      zIndex: 1,
+      backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
+      borderColor: '#111827',
+      borderWidth: 1,
+      padding: 8,
+      opacity: 1,
+    },
+    createdAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
+    updatedAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
+    locked: false,
+    hidden: false,
+    ...patch,
+  };
+}
+
+describe('ensureRichTextEditing', () => {
+  beforeEach(async () => {
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().clearWriterV2();
+    useSessionStore.getState().openDocument({
+      documentKey: 'doc-1',
+      fileName: 'sample.pdf',
+      bytes: await samplePdf(),
+      pageCount: 1,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('opens selected rich text object for editing', async () => {
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().hydrateWriterSession({
+      documentFingerprint: 'doc-1',
+      elements: [element('writer-1')],
+    });
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().dispatchWriterCommand({
+      type: 'SET_ACTIVE_WRITER_ELEMENT',
+      id: 'writer-1',
+    });
+
+    const id = await ensureRichTextEditing();
+
+    expect(id).toBe('writer-1');
+    expect(useWriterV2Store.getState().editingId).toBe('writer-1');
+  });
+
+  it('creates a rich text object when none is selected', async () => {
+    const id = await ensureRichTextEditing();
+    const state = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+
+    expect(id).toBeTruthy();
+    expect(state.elements).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(state.elements[0].type).toBe('richTextBox');
+    expect(state.editingId).toBe(id);
+  });
+
+  it('does not use locked selected rich text object', async () => {
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().hydrateWriterSession({
+      documentFingerprint: 'doc-1',
+      elements: [
+        element('locked-writer', {
+          locked: true,
+        }),
+      ],
+    });
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().dispatchWriterCommand({
+      type: 'SET_ACTIVE_WRITER_ELEMENT',
+      id: 'locked-writer',
+    });
+
+    const id = await ensureRichTextEditing();
+
+    expect(id).not.toBe('locked-writer');
+    expect(useWriterV2Store.getState().elements).toHaveLength(2);
+  });
+});
```

# Expected user-visible improvement

```txt
Before:
- H1/H2/H3/Table/Image require existing active inline editor.
- User sees icons but clicks often do nothing.

After:
- H1/H2/H3/Table/Image create/open a Rich Text Box automatically.
- Command runs inside the same Rich Text Box.
- Table/image/heading become part of one rich content overlay, matching your target UX.
```

Next slice should make **all formatting controls** use the same smart activation path, not just H1/H2/H3/Table/Image.

-----------------------------------------------------

Proceeding with the **next grounded coding slice**:

# WR2-RICH.3 — Make remaining WriterRibbon controls smart-active

This continues the previous patch. The repo already has:

* `WriterRibbon.tsx` with rich formatting, image, table, heading, list, link, font, color controls. 
* `WriterInlineEditor.tsx` already registers `TableNode`, `ImageNode`, `HeadingNode`, list/link/code nodes. 
* `ImageNode` already supports inline/block images inside a rich text box. 
* `insertTable(editor, rows, cols)` already exists. 

So the correct next patch is to make **font, color, highlight, alignment, list, indent, spacing, clear formatting, quote/code/hr** work even when the user has not double-clicked into a Writer object.

# Patch 2 — Expand queued command drain in inline editor

## `frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterInlineEditor.tsx`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterInlineEditor.tsx b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterInlineEditor.tsx

 function QueuedRichEditorCommandPlugin(): null {
@@
           switch (command.type) {
@@
             case 'applyHeading':
               toggleHeading(editor, command.level);
               break;
+
+            case 'formatText':
+              editor.dispatchCommand(FORMAT_TEXT_COMMAND, command.format);
+              break;
+
+            case 'formatElement':
+              editor.dispatchCommand(FORMAT_ELEMENT_COMMAND, command.format);
+              break;
+
+            case 'indent':
+              editor.dispatchCommand(INDENT_CONTENT_COMMAND, undefined);
+              break;
+
+            case 'outdent':
+              editor.dispatchCommand(OUTDENT_CONTENT_COMMAND, undefined);
+              break;

+            case 'list':
+              if (command.command === 'bullet') {
+                editor.dispatchCommand(INSERT_UNORDERED_LIST_COMMAND, undefined);
+              } else if (command.command === 'number') {
+                editor.dispatchCommand(INSERT_ORDERED_LIST_COMMAND, undefined);
+              } else if (command.command === 'check') {
+                editor.dispatchCommand(INSERT_CHECK_LIST_COMMAND, undefined);
+              } else {
+                editor.dispatchCommand(REMOVE_LIST_COMMAND, undefined);
+              }
+              break;
+
+            case 'horizontalRule':
+              editor.dispatchCommand(INSERT_HORIZONTAL_RULE_COMMAND, undefined);
+              break;
+
+            case 'applyTextStyle':
+              editor.update(() => {
+                const selection = $getSelection();
+                if ($isRangeSelection(selection)) {
+                  $patchStyleText(selection, {
+                    [command.property]: command.value,
+                  });
+                }
+              });
+              break;
+
+            case 'applyBlockStyle':
+              applyBlockStyle(editor, command.style);
+              break;
+
+            case 'toggleBlockType':
+              toggleBlockType(editor, command.blockType);
+              break;
+
+            case 'clearFormatting':
+              clearEditorFormatting(editor);
+              break;
 
             case 'focus':
               break;
           }
@@
 
   return null;
 }
+
+function clearEditorFormatting(editor: LexicalEditor): void {
+  editor.update(() => {
+    const selection = $getSelection();
+    if (!$isRangeSelection(selection)) return;
+
+    ([
+      'bold',
+      'italic',
+      'underline',
+      'strikethrough',
+      'superscript',
+      'subscript',
+      'code',
+    ] as TextFormatType[]).forEach((format) => {
+      if (selection.hasFormat(format)) {
+        selection.formatText(format);
+      }
+    });
+
+    $patchStyleText(selection, {
+      color: '',
+      'background-color': '',
+      'font-size': '',
+      'font-family': '',
+      'letter-spacing': '',
+      'line-height': '',
+      'text-transform': '',
+    });
+
+    $setBlocksType(selection, () => $createParagraphNode());
+  });
+
+  editor.dispatchCommand(REMOVE_LIST_COMMAND, undefined);
+}
```

# Patch 3 — Add smart helper functions in WriterRibbon

## `frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterRibbon.tsx`

### 3A. Import queue types

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterRibbon.tsx b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterRibbon.tsx

   type PendingRichEditorCommand,
+  type PendingElementFormat,
+  type PendingListCommand,
 } from '@/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue';
```

### 3B. Add smart helpers after `runOrQueueRichCommand`

```diff
@@
   const runOrQueueRichCommand = React.useCallback(async (
     command: PendingRichEditorCommand,
     immediate: (editor: LexicalEditor) => void,
   ): Promise<void> => {
@@
     useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().enqueue(command);
     await ensureRichTextEditing();
   }, [activeEditor, runWithFocusedEditor]);
+
+  const runSmartTextFormat = React.useCallback((format: TextFormatType) => {
+    void runOrQueueRichCommand(
+      {
+        type: 'formatText',
+        format,
+      },
+      (editor) => {
+        editor.dispatchCommand(FORMAT_TEXT_COMMAND, format);
+      },
+    );
+  }, [runOrQueueRichCommand]);
+
+  const runSmartElementFormat = React.useCallback((format: PendingElementFormat) => {
+    void runOrQueueRichCommand(
+      {
+        type: 'formatElement',
+        format,
+      },
+      (editor) => {
+        editor.dispatchCommand(FORMAT_ELEMENT_COMMAND, format);
+      },
+    );
+  }, [runOrQueueRichCommand]);
+
+  const runSmartListCommand = React.useCallback((command: PendingListCommand) => {
+    void runOrQueueRichCommand(
+      {
+        type: 'list',
+        command,
+      },
+      (editor) => {
+        if (command === 'bullet') {
+          editor.dispatchCommand(INSERT_UNORDERED_LIST_COMMAND, undefined);
+        } else if (command === 'number') {
+          editor.dispatchCommand(INSERT_ORDERED_LIST_COMMAND, undefined);
+        } else if (command === 'check') {
+          editor.dispatchCommand(INSERT_CHECK_LIST_COMMAND, undefined);
+        } else {
+          editor.dispatchCommand(REMOVE_LIST_COMMAND, undefined);
+        }
+      },
+    );
+  }, [runOrQueueRichCommand]);
+
+  const runSmartIndent = React.useCallback((direction: 'in' | 'out') => {
+    void runOrQueueRichCommand(
+      {
+        type: direction === 'in' ? 'indent' : 'outdent',
+      },
+      (editor) => {
+        editor.dispatchCommand(
+          direction === 'in' ? INDENT_CONTENT_COMMAND : OUTDENT_CONTENT_COMMAND,
+          undefined,
+        );
+      },
+    );
+  }, [runOrQueueRichCommand]);
+
+  const runSmartTextStyle = React.useCallback((
+    property: string,
+    value: string,
+  ) => {
+    void runOrQueueRichCommand(
+      {
+        type: 'applyTextStyle',
+        property,
+        value,
+      },
+      (editor) => {
+        editor.update(() => {
+          const selection = $getSelection();
+          if ($isRangeSelection(selection)) {
+            $patchStyleText(selection, {
+              [property]: value,
+            });
+          }
+        });
+      },
+    );
+  }, [runOrQueueRichCommand]);
+
+  const runSmartBlockStyle = React.useCallback((style: Record<string, string>) => {
+    void runOrQueueRichCommand(
+      {
+        type: 'applyBlockStyle',
+        style,
+      },
+      (editor) => applyBlockStyle(editor, style),
+    );
+  }, [runOrQueueRichCommand]);
+
+  const runSmartBlockType = React.useCallback((blockType: WriterBlockType) => {
+    void runOrQueueRichCommand(
+      {
+        type: 'toggleBlockType',
+        blockType,
+      },
+      (editor) => toggleBlockType(editor, blockType),
+    );
+  }, [runOrQueueRichCommand]);
```

```tsx
onClick={() => runSmartTextFormat('subscript')}
onClick={() => runSmartTextFormat('code')}
```

```tsx
onClick={() => runSmartElementFormat('justify')}
```

# Expected effect

```txt
Before:
- Some ribbon icons work only after double-click.
- Font/color/list/alignment controls silently no-op when no active editor.

After:
- Clicking font/color/list/alignment/etc. auto-creates or opens a rich text box.
- The command is queued and executed once the inline editor mounts.
- Writer starts behaving like a single rich authoring box, not scattered overlay silos.
```

Next coding slice should add a **visible “Writer Ready / Editing Active” state** in the ribbon so the user can see whether commands will apply immediately or will create/open a rich box.
--------------------------------------------------

Proceeding with the next coding slice.

# WR2-RICH.4 — Writer Authoring Status + Start/Finish Rich Editing

This adds a visible state chip so users can understand what the Writer ribbon will do:

```txt
No PDF open        → Open a PDF to use Writer
No active editor   → Smart authoring ready
Opening editor     → Opening rich editor...
Active editor      → Editing active
```

It also adds a clear **Start Rich Box** action and **Finish Editing** action.

This is grounded to the current repo:

* `WriterRibbon.tsx` depends on `activeEditor` from `useActiveEditorStore`; when it is null, many ribbon controls previously had no target. 
* `writerV2Store.ts` already exposes `editingId`, `beginEditingWriterElement(id)`, and `endEditingWriterElement()`. 
* `beginEditingWriterElement` safely rejects missing/locked/hidden targets and sets `editingId`, `selectedIds`, and `activeId`. 
* `endEditingWriterElement()` already exists and clears `editingId`. 

I still cannot honestly say tests passed from this chat runtime because the repo is not runnable here. I’m providing the compile-oriented patch and the required test gates.

---

# Patch 1 — Add Writer authoring status component

## New file

`frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterRichAuthoringStatus.tsx`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterRichAuthoringStatus.tsx b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterRichAuthoringStatus.tsx
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterRichAuthoringStatus.tsx
@@
+import React from 'react';
+import { useActiveEditorStore } from '@/core/writer-v2/activeEditorStore';
+import { ensureRichTextEditing } from '@/core/writer-v2/ensureRichTextEditing';
+import { useWriterV2Store } from '@/core/writer-v2/writerV2Store';
+import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';
+
+export const WriterRichAuthoringStatus: React.FC = () => {
+  const activeEditor = useActiveEditorStore((state) => state.activeEditor);
+  const editingId = useWriterV2Store((state) => state.editingId);
+  const selectedIds = useWriterV2Store((state) => state.selectedIds);
+  const activeId = useWriterV2Store((state) => state.activeId);
+  const elements = useWriterV2Store((state) => state.elements);
+  const endEditingWriterElement = useWriterV2Store((state) => state.endEditingWriterElement);
+  const pageCount = useSessionStore((state) => state.pageCount);
+  const sourceBytes = useSessionStore((state) => state.workingBytes ?? state.originalBytes);
+
+  const [busy, setBusy] = React.useState(false);
+  const hasOpenPdf = Boolean(sourceBytes && pageCount > 0);
+  const selectedElement = elements.find((element) =>
+    element.id === activeId || selectedIds.includes(element.id),
+  );
+
+  const startRichBox = async () => {
+    if (!hasOpenPdf || busy) return;
+
+    setBusy(true);
+    try {
+      await ensureRichTextEditing();
+    } finally {
+      setBusy(false);
+    }
+  };
+
+  const status = getStatus({
+    hasOpenPdf,
+    busy,
+    editingId,
+    hasActiveEditor: Boolean(activeEditor),
+  });
+
+  return (
+    <div
+      data-writer-v2-rich-authoring-status="true"
+      aria-label="Writer rich authoring status"
+      style={{
+        display: 'flex',
+        alignItems: 'center',
+        gap: 8,
+        minHeight: 32,
+        padding: '4px 8px',
+        borderRadius: 9,
+        border: `1px solid ${status.border}`,
+        background: status.background,
+        color: status.color,
+        boxSizing: 'border-box',
+        whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
+      }}
+    >
+      <span
+        aria-hidden="true"
+        style={{
+          width: 8,
+          height: 8,
+          borderRadius: 99,
+          background: status.dot,
+          flexShrink: 0,
+        }}
+      />
+
+      <span
+        style={{
+          display: 'grid',
+          gap: 1,
+          minWidth: 0,
+        }}
+      >
+        <strong
+          style={{
+            fontSize: 11,
+            lineHeight: 1.05,
+          }}
+        >
+          {status.label}
+        </strong>
+        <span
+          title={selectedElement ? `${selectedElement.type} / ${selectedElement.id}` : undefined}
+          style={{
+            maxWidth: 220,
+            overflow: 'hidden',
+            textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
+            fontSize: 10,
+            color: status.subtle,
+          }}
+        >
+          {status.detail}
+        </span>
+      </span>
+
+      {!activeEditor ? (
+        <button
+          type="button"
+          onClick={() => {
+            void startRichBox();
+          }}
+          disabled={!hasOpenPdf || busy}
+          title="Create or open a rich Writer text box"
+          style={{
+            ...buttonStyle,
+            opacity: !hasOpenPdf || busy ? 0.5 : 1,
+            cursor: !hasOpenPdf || busy ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
+          }}
+        >
+          {busy ? 'Opening...' : 'Start Rich Box'}
+        </button>
+      ) : (
+        <button
+          type="button"
+          onClick={endEditingWriterElement}
+          title="Finish the active rich text edit session"
+          style={buttonStyle}
+        >
+          Finish Editing
+        </button>
+      )}
+    </div>
+  );
+};
+
+function getStatus({
+  hasOpenPdf,
+  busy,
+  editingId,
+  hasActiveEditor,
+}: {
+  hasOpenPdf: boolean;
+  busy: boolean;
+  editingId: string | null;
+  hasActiveEditor: boolean;
+}) {
+  if (!hasOpenPdf) {
+    return {
+      label: 'Writer unavailable',
+      detail: 'Open a PDF to use rich authoring.',
+      dot: '#94a3b8',
+      color: '#334155',
+      subtle: '#64748b',
+      border: '#cbd5e1',
+      background: '#f8fafc',
+    };
+  }
+
+  if (busy || (editingId && !hasActiveEditor)) {
+    return {
+      label: 'Opening editor',
+      detail: 'Preparing the active rich text box...',
+      dot: '#f59e0b',
+      color: '#78350f',
+      subtle: '#92400e',
+      border: '#fcd34d',
+      background: '#fffbeb',
+    };
+  }
+
+  if (hasActiveEditor) {
+    return {
+      label: 'Editing active',
+      detail: 'Ribbon commands apply to the current rich box.',
+      dot: '#16a34a',
+      color: '#064e3b',
+      subtle: '#047857',
+      border: '#86efac',
+      background: '#ecfdf5',
+    };
+  }
+
+  return {
+    label: 'Smart authoring ready',
+    detail: 'Ribbon commands will create/open a rich box.',
+    dot: '#2563eb',
+    color: '#1e3a8a',
+    subtle: '#2563eb',
+    border: '#93c5fd',
+    background: '#eff6ff',
+  };
+}
+
+const buttonStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
+  height: 24,
+  padding: '0 8px',
+  borderRadius: 6,
+  border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
+  background: '#ffffff',
+  color: '#0f172a',
+  fontSize: 10,
+  fontWeight: 800,
+};
```

# Patch 3 — Add component tests

## New file

`frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterRichAuthoringStatus.test.tsx`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterRichAuthoringStatus.test.tsx b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterRichAuthoringStatus.test.tsx
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterRichAuthoringStatus.test.tsx
@@
+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import { fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
+import { PDFDocument } from 'pdf-lib';
+import type { WriterElementV2 } from '@/core/writer-v2/types';
+import { useActiveEditorStore } from '@/core/writer-v2/activeEditorStore';
+import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';
+import { useWriterV2Store } from '@/core/writer-v2/writerV2Store';
+import { WriterRichAuthoringStatus } from './WriterRichAuthoringStatus';
+
+async function samplePdf(): Promise<Uint8Array> {
+  const pdf = await PDFDocument.create();
+  pdf.addPage([612, 792]);
+  return pdf.save();
+}
+
+function element(
+  id: string,
+  patch: Partial<WriterElementV2> = {},
+): WriterElementV2 {
+  return {
+    id,
+    documentFingerprint: 'doc-1',
+    pageIndex: 0,
+    type: 'richTextBox',
+    rect: { x: 10, y: 20, width: 160, height: 80 },
+    plainText: 'Existing rich box',
+    editorStateJson: '{"root":{"children":[]}}',
+    style: {
+      zIndex: 1,
+      backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
+      borderColor: '#111827',
+      borderWidth: 1,
+      padding: 8,
+      opacity: 1,
+    },
+    createdAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
+    updatedAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
+    locked: false,
+    hidden: false,
+    ...patch,
+  };
+}
+
+describe('WriterRichAuthoringStatus', () => {
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    useActiveEditorStore.getState().setActiveEditor(null);
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().clearWriterV2();
+    useSessionStore.getState().clearDocument();
+  });
+
+  it('shows unavailable state when no PDF is open', () => {
+    render(<WriterRichAuthoringStatus />);
+
+    expect(screen.getByText('Writer unavailable')).toBeTruthy();
+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start Rich Box' })).toBeDisabled();
+  });
+
+  it('creates a rich text box when Start Rich Box is clicked', async () => {
+    useSessionStore.getState().openDocument({
+      documentKey: 'doc-1',
+      fileName: 'sample.pdf',
+      bytes: await samplePdf(),
+      pageCount: 1,
+    });
+
+    render(<WriterRichAuthoringStatus />);
+
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start Rich Box' }));
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(useWriterV2Store.getState().elements).toHaveLength(1);
+    });
+
+    const state = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+    expect(state.elements[0].type).toBe('richTextBox');
+    expect(state.editingId).toBe(state.elements[0].id);
+  });
+
+  it('opens selected rich text box instead of creating a new one', async () => {
+    useSessionStore.getState().openDocument({
+      documentKey: 'doc-1',
+      fileName: 'sample.pdf',
+      bytes: await samplePdf(),
+      pageCount: 1,
+    });
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().hydrateWriterSession({
+      documentFingerprint: 'doc-1',
+      elements: [element('writer-1')],
+    });
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().dispatchWriterCommand({
+      type: 'SET_ACTIVE_WRITER_ELEMENT',
+      id: 'writer-1',
+    });
+
+    render(<WriterRichAuthoringStatus />);
+
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Start Rich Box' }));
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(useWriterV2Store.getState().editingId).toBe('writer-1');
+    });
+
+    expect(useWriterV2Store.getState().elements).toHaveLength(1);
+  });
+
+  it('shows opening editor state when editingId exists but active editor is not registered yet', async () => {
+    useSessionStore.getState().openDocument({
+      documentKey: 'doc-1',
+      fileName: 'sample.pdf',
+      bytes: await samplePdf(),
+      pageCount: 1,
+    });
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().hydrateWriterSession({
+      documentFingerprint: 'doc-1',
+      elements: [element('writer-1')],
+    });
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().beginEditingWriterElement('writer-1');
+
+    render(<WriterRichAuthoringStatus />);
+
+    expect(screen.getByText('Opening editor')).toBeTruthy();
+  });
+});
```

# WR2-RICH.5 — Smart Rich Box Starter Templates

Current repo creates a rich text box with only plain text state: `createRichTextBoxElement()` always uses `createPlainTextEditorStateJson(input.text ?? '')`.  The editor-state helper only has empty/plain-text creation today. 

This patch adds a **professional starter rich box** so “Start Rich Box” creates a useful Writer canvas instead of a tiny blank overlay:

```txt
Title
Body paragraph
Action / comment paragraph
Optional table inserted after editor opens
```

It stays compatible with the previous command-queue patch, because table insertion should use the existing `insertTable(editor, rows, cols)` helper rather than hand-building Lexical table JSON. 

---

# Patch 1 — Add rich starter editor-state builder

## `frontend/src/core/writer-v2/editor/writerEditorState.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/editor/writerEditorState.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/editor/writerEditorState.ts
--- a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/editor/writerEditorState.ts
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/editor/writerEditorState.ts
@@
 export function createPlainTextEditorStateJson(text: string): string {
   const safeText = text ?? '';
@@
   });
 }
+
+export type WriterStarterTemplateId =
+  | 'blank'
+  | 'authoringCard'
+  | 'reviewComment'
+  | 'engineeringNote';
+
+export function createWriterStarterEditorStateJson(
+  templateId: WriterStarterTemplateId,
+): string {
+  switch (templateId) {
+    case 'authoringCard':
+      return createStructuredEditorStateJson([
+        headingNode('Document Heading', 'h1'),
+        paragraphNode('Write your content here. Use the ribbon to add tables, images, lists, colors, and review comments.'),
+        paragraphNode('Action / comment: '),
+      ]);
+
+    case 'reviewComment':
+      return createStructuredEditorStateJson([
+        headingNode('Review Comment', 'h2'),
+        paragraphNode('Observation: '),
+        paragraphNode('Required action: '),
+        paragraphNode('Status: Open'),
+      ]);
+
+    case 'engineeringNote':
+      return createStructuredEditorStateJson([
+        headingNode('Engineering Note', 'h2'),
+        paragraphNode('Reference: '),
+        paragraphNode('Finding: '),
+        paragraphNode('Recommendation: '),
+      ]);
+
+    case 'blank':
+    default:
+      return createPlainTextEditorStateJson('');
+  }
+}
+
+function createStructuredEditorStateJson(
+  children: Array<Record<string, unknown>>,
+): string {
+  return JSON.stringify({
+    root: {
+      children,
+      direction: null,
+      format: '',
+      indent: 0,
+      type: 'root',
+      version: 1,
+    },
+  });
+}
+
+function headingNode(
+  text: string,
+  tag: 'h1' | 'h2' | 'h3',
+): Record<string, unknown> {
+  return {
+    children: [textNode(text)],
+    direction: null,
+    format: '',
+    indent: 0,
+    type: 'heading',
+    version: 1,
+    tag,
+  };
+}
+
+function paragraphNode(text: string): Record<string, unknown> {
+  return {
+    children: text ? [textNode(text)] : [],
+    direction: null,
+    format: '',
+    indent: 0,
+    type: 'paragraph',
+    version: 1,
+    textFormat: 0,
+    textStyle: '',
+  };
+}
+
+function textNode(text: string): Record<string, unknown> {
+  return {
+    detail: 0,
+    format: 0,
+    mode: 'normal',
+    style: '',
+    text,
+    type: 'text',
+    version: 1,
+  };
+}
 
 export function normalizeEditorStateJson(
```

# Patch 5 — Keep ribbon smart-creation blank by default

In the previous patch, ribbon commands call:

```ts
await ensureRichTextEditing();
```

Keep that default. It means:

```txt
Click H1/Table/Image/Font command → create/open a clean rich box
Click Start Writer Box → create structured authoring card with starter table
```

That avoids injecting template text when the user simply clicks Bold or Color.

```diff
+import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

+

+    expect(text).toContain('Required action');
+  });
+
+  it('creates engineering note state', () => {
+    const json = createWriterStarterEditorStateJson('engineeringNote');
+    const text = plainTextFromSerializedEditorState(json);
+
+    expect(text).toContain('Engineering Note');
+    expect(text).toContain('Recommendation');
+  });
+});
```

# WR2-RICH.6 — Box Style in Ribbon: Background / Border / Padding / Opacity for the single Rich Text Box

This directly targets your requirement:

```txt
Writer V2 should feel like one rich authoring box:
text + heading + table + image + font + background
```

Repo-grounding:

* Current `WriterV2Inspector.tsx` already has Fill & Stroke controls, but they are isolated in the inspector. 
* Current `WriterElementV2.style` already supports `backgroundColor`, `borderColor`, `borderWidth`, `padding`, `opacity`, `zIndex`, `fontSize`, `fontFamily`, `textColor`. 
* Current `writerV2Store` already supports `dispatchWriterCommand`, `editingId`, `activeId`, and `beginEditingWriterElement`. 
* `beginEditingWriterElement` already safely selects/activates the object. 

I still cannot truthfully claim local tests passed from this chat runtime. Below is the code and the required gates.

# Patch 1 — Add rich box style actions

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichBoxStyleActions.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichBoxStyleActions.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichBoxStyleActions.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichBoxStyleActions.ts
@@
+import type { WriterElementV2 } from './types';
+import { ensureRichTextEditing } from './ensureRichTextEditing';
+import { useWriterV2Store } from './writerV2Store';
+
+export type WriterRichBoxStylePresetId =
+  | 'plainWhite'
+  | 'reviewCard'
+  | 'engineeringNote'
+  | 'warningBox'
+  | 'transparentOverlay'
+  | 'noBorder';
+
+export interface WriterRichBoxStylePreset {
+  id: WriterRichBoxStylePresetId;
+  label: string;
+  style: Partial<WriterElementV2['style']>;
+}
+
+export const WRITER_RICH_BOX_STYLE_PRESETS: WriterRichBoxStylePreset[] = [
+  {
+    id: 'plainWhite',
+    label: 'Plain White',
+    style: {
+      backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
+      borderColor: '#cbd5e1',
+      borderWidth: 1,
+      padding: 10,
+      opacity: 1,
+    },
+  },
+  {
+    id: 'reviewCard',
+    label: 'Review Card',
+    style: {
+      backgroundColor: '#fff7ed',
+      borderColor: '#f97316',
+      borderWidth: 1,
+      padding: 12,
+      opacity: 1,
+    },
+  },
+  {
+    id: 'engineeringNote',
+    label: 'Engineering Note',
+    style: {
+      backgroundColor: '#eff6ff',
+      borderColor: '#2563eb',
+      borderWidth: 1,
+      padding: 12,
+      opacity: 1,
+    },
+  },
+  {
+    id: 'warningBox',
+    label: 'Warning Box',
+    style: {
+      backgroundColor: '#fef2f2',
+      borderColor: '#dc2626',
+      borderWidth: 2,
+      padding: 12,
+      opacity: 1,
+    },
+  },
+  {
+    id: 'transparentOverlay',
+    label: 'Transparent Overlay',
+    style: {
+      backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
+      borderColor: '#94a3b8',
+      borderWidth: 1,
+      padding: 8,
+      opacity: 0.65,
+    },
+  },
+  {
+    id: 'noBorder',
+    label: 'No Border',
+    style: {
+      backgroundColor: 'transparent',
+      borderColor: 'transparent',
+      borderWidth: 0,
+      padding: 8,
+      opacity: 1,
+    },
+  },
+];
+
+const RICH_STYLE_TARGET_TYPES = new Set<WriterElementV2['type']>([
+  'richTextBox',
+  'textCallout',
+  'richNote',
+]);
+
+export interface WriterRichBoxStyleResult {
+  changed: boolean;
+  id: string | null;
+  message: string;
+}
+
+export async function patchActiveRichBoxStyle(
+  stylePatch: Partial<WriterElementV2['style']>,
+): Promise<WriterRichBoxStyleResult> {
+  const id = await ensureRichBoxStyleTarget();
+
+  if (!id) {
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      id: null,
+      message: 'Open a PDF before styling a Writer rich box.',
+    };
+  }
+
+  const store = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+  const element = store.elements.find((item) => item.id === id);
+
+  if (!element) {
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      id,
+      message: 'Writer rich box target was not found.',
+    };
+  }
+
+  const result = store.dispatchWriterCommand({
+    type: 'UPDATE_WRITER_ELEMENT',
+    id,
+    patch: {
+      style: {
+        ...element.style,
+        ...stylePatch,
+      },
+    },
+  });
+
+  return {
+    changed: result.changed,
+    id,
+    message: result.changed
+      ? 'Updated Writer rich box style.'
+      : result.reason,
+  };
+}
+
+export async function applyRichBoxStylePreset(
+  presetId: WriterRichBoxStylePresetId,
+): Promise<WriterRichBoxStyleResult> {
+  const preset = WRITER_RICH_BOX_STYLE_PRESETS.find((item) => item.id === presetId);
+
+  if (!preset) {
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      id: null,
+      message: `Unknown Writer rich box style preset: ${presetId}`,
+    };
+  }
+
+  return patchActiveRichBoxStyle(preset.style);
+}
+
+export function getActiveRichBoxStyleTarget(): WriterElementV2 | null {
+  const store = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+  const ids = [
+    store.activeId,
+    ...store.selectedIds,
+    store.editingId,
+  ].filter(Boolean) as string[];
+
+  for (const id of ids) {
+    const element = store.elements.find((item) => item.id === id);
+
+    if (
+      element &&
+      RICH_STYLE_TARGET_TYPES.has(element.type) &&
+      !element.locked &&
+      !element.hidden
+    ) {
+      return element;
+    }
+  }
+
+  return null;
+}
+
+async function ensureRichBoxStyleTarget(): Promise<string | null> {
+  const existing = getActiveRichBoxStyleTarget();
+
+  if (existing) {
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().beginEditingWriterElement(existing.id);
+    return existing.id;
+  }
+
+  return ensureRichTextEditing();
+}
```

# Patch 2 — Add Ribbon box style control

## New file

`frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterRichBoxStyleControl.tsx`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterRichBoxStyleControl.tsx b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterRichBoxStyleControl.tsx
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterRichBoxStyleControl.tsx
@@
+import React from 'react';
+import { PaintBucket } from 'lucide-react';
+import { ColorPickerPopover } from '@/components/ui/ColorPickerPopover';
+import { useWriterV2Store } from '@/core/writer-v2/writerV2Store';
+import {
+  applyRichBoxStylePreset,
+  getActiveRichBoxStyleTarget,
+  patchActiveRichBoxStyle,
+  WRITER_RICH_BOX_STYLE_PRESETS,
+  type WriterRichBoxStylePresetId,
+} from '@/core/writer-v2/writerRichBoxStyleActions';
+
+export const WriterRichBoxStyleControl: React.FC = () => {
+  const elements = useWriterV2Store((state) => state.elements);
+  const activeId = useWriterV2Store((state) => state.activeId);
+  const selectedIds = useWriterV2Store((state) => state.selectedIds);
+  const editingId = useWriterV2Store((state) => state.editingId);
+  const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false);
+
+  const target = React.useMemo(
+    () => getActiveRichBoxStyleTarget(),
+    [activeId, editingId, elements, selectedIds],
+  );
+
+  const style = target?.style;
+
+  const fill = style?.backgroundColor ?? '#ffffff';
+  const border = style?.borderColor ?? '#2563eb';
+  const borderWidth = style?.borderWidth ?? 1;
+  const padding = style?.padding ?? 8;
+  const opacity = style?.opacity ?? 1;
+
+  return (
+    <div
+      data-writer-rich-box-style-control="true"
+      style={{
+        position: 'relative',
+        display: 'inline-flex',
+        alignItems: 'center',
+      }}
+    >
+      <button
+        type="button"
+        aria-label="Writer box style"
+        title="Style Writer rich box background, border, padding, and opacity"
+        onClick={() => setOpen((value) => !value)}
+        style={{
+          height: 30,
+          borderRadius: 7,
+          border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
+          background: open ? '#eff6ff' : '#ffffff',
+          color: open ? '#1d4ed8' : '#334155',
+          display: 'inline-flex',
+          alignItems: 'center',
+          gap: 6,
+          padding: '0 8px',
+          fontSize: 11,
+          fontWeight: 800,
+          cursor: 'pointer',
+        }}
+      >
+        <PaintBucket size={15} />
+        Box
+      </button>
+
+      {open ? (
+        <div
+          role="dialog"
+          aria-label="Writer rich box style panel"
+          style={{
+            position: 'absolute',
+            top: 36,
+            left: 0,
+            zIndex: 260,
+            width: 260,
+            padding: 10,
+            borderRadius: 10,
+            border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
+            background: '#ffffff',
+            boxShadow: '0 14px 36px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.18)',
+            display: 'grid',
+            gap: 8,
+          }}
+        >
+          <div>
+            <div style={sectionTitleStyle}>Preset</div>
+            <select
+              aria-label="Writer box style preset"
+              defaultValue=""
+              onChange={(event) => {
+                const value = event.currentTarget.value as WriterRichBoxStylePresetId;
+                if (!value) return;
+                void applyRichBoxStylePreset(value);
+                event.currentTarget.value = '';
+              }}
+              style={selectStyle}
+            >
+              <option value="">Apply preset...</option>
+              {WRITER_RICH_BOX_STYLE_PRESETS.map((preset) => (
+                <option key={preset.id} value={preset.id}>
+                  {preset.label}
+                </option>
+              ))}
+            </select>
+          </div>
+
+          <div style={rowStyle}>
+            <span style={labelStyle}>Fill</span>
+            <ColorPickerPopover
+              value={fill}
+              onChange={(value) => {
+                void patchActiveRichBoxStyle({ backgroundColor: value });
+              }}
+              label="Fill"
+            />
+          </div>
+
+          <div style={rowStyle}>
+            <span style={labelStyle}>Border</span>
+            <ColorPickerPopover
+              value={border}
+              onChange={(value) => {
+                void patchActiveRichBoxStyle({ borderColor: value });
+              }}
+              label="Border"
+            />
+            <input
+              aria-label="Writer box border width"
+              type="number"
+              min={0}
+              max={20}
+              value={borderWidth}
+              onChange={(event) => {
+                void patchActiveRichBoxStyle({
+                  borderWidth: Number(event.currentTarget.value),
+                });
+              }}
+              style={numberStyle}
+            />
+          </div>
+
+          <div style={rowStyle}>
+            <span style={labelStyle}>Padding</span>
+            <input
+              aria-label="Writer box padding"
+              type="number"
+              min={0}
+              max={60}
+              value={padding}
+              onChange={(event) => {
+                void patchActiveRichBoxStyle({
+                  padding: Number(event.currentTarget.value),
+                });
+              }}
+              style={numberStyle}
+            />
+          </div>
+
+          <div style={rowStyle}>
+            <span style={labelStyle}>Opacity</span>
+            <input
+              aria-label="Writer box opacity"
+              type="range"
+              min={0}
+              max={1}
+              step={0.05}
+              value={opacity}
+              onChange={(event) => {
+                void patchActiveRichBoxStyle({
+                  opacity: Number(event.currentTarget.value),
+                });
+              }}
+              style={{ flex: 1 }}
+            />
+            <span
+              style={{
+                width: 34,
+                textAlign: 'right',
+                fontSize: 11,
+                color: '#64748b',
+              }}
+            >
+              {Math.round(opacity * 100)}%
+            </span>
+          </div>
+
+          <button
+            type="button"
+            onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
+            style={{
+              height: 26,
+              borderRadius: 6,
+              border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
+              background: '#f8fafc',
+              color: '#334155',
+              fontSize: 11,
+              fontWeight: 700,
+              cursor: 'pointer',
+            }}
+          >
+            Close
+          </button>
+        </div>
+      ) : null}
+    </div>
+  );
+};
+

+  fontSize: 10,
+  fontWeight: 800,

+  letterSpacing: '0.06em',
+  marginBottom: 4,
+};

+};

+};

+  height: 28,
```

# Patch 4 — Add action tests

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichBoxStyleActions.test.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichBoxStyleActions.test.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichBoxStyleActions.test.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichBoxStyleActions.test.ts
@@
+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import { PDFDocument } from 'pdf-lib';
+import type { WriterElementV2 } from './types';
+import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';
+import { useWriterV2Store } from './writerV2Store';
+import {
+  applyRichBoxStylePreset,
+  getActiveRichBoxStyleTarget,
+  patchActiveRichBoxStyle,
+} from './writerRichBoxStyleActions';
+
+async function samplePdf(): Promise<Uint8Array> {
+  const pdf = await PDFDocument.create();
+  pdf.addPage([612, 792]);
+  return pdf.save();
+}
+
+function element(
+  id: string,
+  patch: Partial<WriterElementV2> = {},
+): WriterElementV2 {
+  return {
+    id,
+    documentFingerprint: 'doc-1',
+    pageIndex: 0,
+    type: 'richTextBox',
+    rect: { x: 10, y: 20, width: 180, height: 90 },
+    plainText: 'Rich box',
+    editorStateJson: '{"root":{"children":[]}}',
+    style: {
+      zIndex: 1,
+      backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
+      borderColor: '#111827',
+      borderWidth: 1,
+      padding: 8,
+      opacity: 1,
+    },
+    createdAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
+    updatedAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
+    locked: false,
+    hidden: false,
+    ...patch,
+  };
+}
+
+describe('writerRichBoxStyleActions', () => {
+  beforeEach(async () => {
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().clearWriterV2();
+    useSessionStore.getState().openDocument({
+      documentKey: 'doc-1',
+      fileName: 'sample.pdf',
+      bytes: await samplePdf(),
+      pageCount: 1,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('patches selected rich box style', async () => {
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().hydrateWriterSession({
+      documentFingerprint: 'doc-1',
+      elements: [element('writer-1')],
+    });
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().dispatchWriterCommand({
+      type: 'SET_ACTIVE_WRITER_ELEMENT',
+      id: 'writer-1',
+    });
+
+    const result = await patchActiveRichBoxStyle({
+      backgroundColor: '#eff6ff',
+      borderColor: '#2563eb',
+      padding: 14,
+    });
+
+    const updated = useWriterV2Store.getState().elements[0];
+
+    expect(result.changed).toBe(true);
+    expect(updated.style.backgroundColor).toBe('#eff6ff');
+    expect(updated.style.borderColor).toBe('#2563eb');
+    expect(updated.style.padding).toBe(14);
+  });
+
+  it('creates a rich box when no style target exists', async () => {
+    const result = await patchActiveRichBoxStyle({
+      backgroundColor: '#fff7ed',
+    });
+
+    const state = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+
+    expect(result.changed).toBe(true);
+    expect(state.elements).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(state.elements[0].type).toBe('richTextBox');
+    expect(state.elements[0].style.backgroundColor).toBe('#fff7ed');
+    expect(state.editingId).toBe(state.elements[0].id);
+  });
+
+  it('applies rich box style preset', async () => {
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().hydrateWriterSession({
+      documentFingerprint: 'doc-1',
+      elements: [element('writer-1')],
+    });
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().dispatchWriterCommand({
+      type: 'SET_ACTIVE_WRITER_ELEMENT',
+      id: 'writer-1',
+    });
+
+    const result = await applyRichBoxStylePreset('warningBox');
+    const updated = useWriterV2Store.getState().elements[0];
+
+    expect(result.changed).toBe(true);
+    expect(updated.style.backgroundColor).toBe('#fef2f2');
+    expect(updated.style.borderColor).toBe('#dc2626');
+    expect(updated.style.borderWidth).toBe(2);
+  });
+
+  it('ignores locked selected box and creates editable fallback', async () => {
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().hydrateWriterSession({
+      documentFingerprint: 'doc-1',
+      elements: [
+        element('locked-1', {
+          locked: true,
+        }),
+      ],
+    });
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().dispatchWriterCommand({
+      type: 'SET_ACTIVE_WRITER_ELEMENT',
+      id: 'locked-1',
+    });
+
+    const targetBefore = getActiveRichBoxStyleTarget();
+    expect(targetBefore).toBeNull();
+
+    const result = await patchActiveRichBoxStyle({
+      backgroundColor: '#ecfdf5',
+    });
+
+    const state = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+
+    expect(result.changed).toBe(true);
+    expect(state.elements).toHaveLength(2);
+    expect(state.elements[1].style.backgroundColor).toBe('#ecfdf5');
+  });
+});
```

# Expected result

```txt
Before:
Background/fill/stroke exists only in the inspector and feels separated from the Writer authoring workflow.

After:
Ribbon has a Box control:
- Fill
- Border color
- Border width
- Padding
- Opacity
- Presets:
  Plain White
  Review Card
  Engineering Note
  Warning Box
  Transparent Overlay
  No Border

If no rich box is active:
- control auto-creates/opens a rich text box
- then applies the style
```

This moves Writer V2 closer to your required model: **one rich text box that handles content and visual box styling together.**

----------------------------------------------------
Proceeding with the next coding slice.

# WR2-RICH.7 — Flatten/export rich table + rich image blocks inside one Rich Text Box

This is the missing export-fidelity slice for your target UX.

Repo-grounding:

* `richTextRenderer.ts` already parses rich Lexical content into `PdfBlock` variants for `table` and `image`. 
* It also parses Lexical `table`, `tablerow`, `tablecell`, and `inline-image` nodes into those block types. 
* `flattenedPdfExport.ts` already imports `editorStateToPdfBlocks()` and calls `drawRichBlocks()` for normal Writer elements. 
* `flattenedPdfExport.ts` already has separate object-level `drawImageBox()` and `drawTableBox()` branches, but the target now is **table/image inside the same rich text box**, not only separate overlay objects. 

So this patch adds drawing support for `PdfBlock.type === 'table'` and `PdfBlock.type === 'image'` inside `drawRichBlocks()`.

I cannot truthfully claim local test pass from this chat runtime. Use the gates at the end before merge.

---

# Patch 1 — Draw rich image/table blocks during flatten export

## `frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/flattenedPdfExport.ts`

### 1A. Add rich block handling inside `drawRichBlocks`

Find this loop:

```ts
for (const block of blocks) {
  if (y < rect.y + padding) break;

  if (block.type === 'paragraph' || block.type === 'heading') {
```

Patch it like this:

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/flattenedPdfExport.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/flattenedPdfExport.ts
--- a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/flattenedPdfExport.ts
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/flattenedPdfExport.ts
@@
   for (const block of blocks) {
     if (y < rect.y + padding) break;
 
+    if (block.type === 'image') {
+      y = await drawRichImageBlock(context, block, rect, padding, y);
+      continue;
+    }
+
+    if (block.type === 'table') {
+      y = await drawRichTableBlock(context, block, rect, padding, y, defaultFontSize);
+      continue;
+    }
+
     if (block.type === 'paragraph' || block.type === 'heading') {
```

### 1B. Add helper functions after `drawRichBlocks`

Add these helpers **after** `drawRichBlocks()` and before existing utility functions such as `parseCssColor`, `dataUrlToBytes`, or `normalizeRect`.

```diff
@@
 async function drawRichBlocks(
   context: DrawContext,
   blocks: PdfBlock[],
   rect: PdfRect,
   padding: number,
   defaultFontSize: number,
   startY: number,
 ): Promise<number> {
@@
 
   return y;
 }
+
+async function drawRichImageBlock(
+  context: DrawContext,
+  block: Extract<PdfBlock, { type: 'image' }>,
+  rect: PdfRect,
+  padding: number,
+  y: number,
+): Promise<number> {
+  const maxWidth = Math.max(1, rect.width - padding * 2);
+  const remainingHeight = Math.max(1, y - (rect.y + padding));
+  const maxHeight = Math.min(remainingHeight, Math.max(60, rect.height * 0.42));
+
+  if (!block.src || !block.src.startsWith('data:image/')) {
+    return drawRichBlockPlaceholder(
+      context,
+      rect,
+      padding,
+      y,
+      maxWidth,
+      34,
+      block.altText ? `Image: ${block.altText}` : 'Image placeholder',
+    );
+  }
+
+  try {
+    const imageBytes = dataUrlToBytes(block.src);
+    const isPng = block.src.startsWith('data:image/png');
+    const isJpg = block.src.startsWith('data:image/jpeg') || block.src.startsWith('data:image/jpg');
+
+    if (!isPng && !isJpg) {
+      return drawRichBlockPlaceholder(
+        context,
+        rect,
+        padding,
+        y,
+        maxWidth,
+        34,
+        'Unsupported rich image format',
+      );
+    }
+
+    const image = isPng
+      ? await context.doc.embedPng(imageBytes)
+      : await context.doc.embedJpg(imageBytes);
+
+    const requestedWidth = typeof block.width === 'number' ? block.width : image.width;
+    const requestedHeight = typeof block.height === 'number' ? block.height : image.height;
+    const scale = Math.min(
+      maxWidth / Math.max(1, requestedWidth),
+      maxHeight / Math.max(1, requestedHeight),
+      1,
+    );
+    const width = Math.max(1, requestedWidth * scale);
+    const height = Math.max(1, requestedHeight * scale);
+    const imageX = rect.x + padding + (maxWidth - width) / 2;
+    const imageY = y - height;
+
+    if (imageY < rect.y + padding) {
+      return rect.y + padding - 1;
+    }
+
+    context.page.drawImage(image, {
+      x: imageX,
+      y: imageY,
+      width,
+      height,
+    });
+
+    const matchingAsset = findAssetByDataUrl(context, block.src);
+    if (matchingAsset) {
+      context.embeddedAssetIds.add(matchingAsset.id);
+    }
+
+    let nextY = imageY - 6;
+
+    if (block.caption) {
+      const captionFont = await getFontForStyle(context, {
+        fontSize: 9,
+        italic: true,
+      });
+      const captionColor = rgb(0.39, 0.45, 0.55);
+
+      if (nextY - 10 >= rect.y + padding) {
+        context.page.drawText(block.caption, {
+          x: rect.x + padding,
+          y: nextY - 10,
+          size: 9,
+          font: captionFont,
+          color: captionColor,
+          maxWidth,
+        });
+        nextY -= 16;
+      }
+    }
+
+    return nextY;
+  } catch {
+    return drawRichBlockPlaceholder(
+      context,
+      rect,
+      padding,
+      y,
+      maxWidth,
+      34,
+      'Unable to embed rich image',
+    );
+  }
+}
+
+async function drawRichTableBlock(
+  context: DrawContext,
+  block: Extract<PdfBlock, { type: 'table' }>,
+  rect: PdfRect,
+  padding: number,
+  y: number,
+  defaultFontSize: number,
+): Promise<number> {
+  const rows = block.rows;
+
+  if (rows.length === 0) {
+    return y;
+  }
+
+  const maxColumns = Math.max(
+    1,
+    ...rows.map((row) => row.cells.length),
+  );
+  const maxWidth = Math.max(1, rect.width - padding * 2);
+  const colWidth = maxWidth / maxColumns;
+  const textSize = Math.max(7, Math.min(defaultFontSize, 10));
+  const baseRowHeight = Math.max(20, textSize * 2.4);
+  const tableHeight = baseRowHeight * rows.length;
+  const tableTop = y - 4;
+  const tableBottom = tableTop - tableHeight;
+
+  if (tableBottom < rect.y + padding) {
+    return drawRichBlockPlaceholder(
+      context,
+      rect,
+      padding,
+      y,
+      maxWidth,
+      30,
+      'Table continues beyond box',
+    );
+  }
+
+  const borderColor = rgb(0.79, 0.84, 0.91);
+  const headerFill = rgb(0.95, 0.97, 0.99);
+  const textColor = rgb(0.07, 0.09, 0.15);
+  const font = await getFontForStyle(context, {
+    fontSize: textSize,
+  });
+  const fontBold = await getFontForStyle(context, {
+    fontSize: textSize,
+    bold: true,
+  });
+
+  for (let rowIndex = 0; rowIndex < rows.length; rowIndex += 1) {
+    const row = rows[rowIndex];
+    const rowY = tableTop - (rowIndex + 1) * baseRowHeight;
+    const isHeader = rowIndex === 0;
+
+    for (let colIndex = 0; colIndex < maxColumns; colIndex += 1) {
+      const cell = row.cells[colIndex];
+      const cellX = rect.x + padding + colIndex * colWidth;
+
+      context.page.drawRectangle({
+        x: cellX,
+        y: rowY,
+        width: colWidth,
+        height: baseRowHeight,
+        color: isHeader ? headerFill : undefined,
+        borderColor,
+        borderWidth: 0.5,
+      });
+
+      const text = cell ? richCellText(cell.children) : '';
+
+      if (text) {
+        context.page.drawText(text, {
+          x: cellX + 4,
+          y: rowY + Math.max(4, baseRowHeight - textSize - 5),
+          size: textSize,
+          font: isHeader ? fontBold : font,
+          color: textColor,
+          maxWidth: Math.max(1, colWidth - 8),
+        });
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  return tableBottom - 8;
+}
+
+async function drawRichBlockPlaceholder(
+  context: DrawContext,
+  rect: PdfRect,
+  padding: number,
+  y: number,
+  width: number,
+  height: number,
+  label: string,
+): Promise<number> {
+  const boxY = y - height;
+
+  if (boxY < rect.y + padding) {
+    return rect.y + padding - 1;
+  }
+
+  context.page.drawRectangle({
+    x: rect.x + padding,
+    y: boxY,
+    width,
+    height,
+    color: rgb(0.96, 0.97, 0.98),
+    borderColor: rgb(0.79, 0.84, 0.91),
+    borderWidth: 0.5,
+  });
+
+  const font = await getFontForStyle(context, {
+    fontSize: 9,
+    italic: true,
+  });
+
+  context.page.drawText(label, {
+    x: rect.x + padding + 6,
+    y: boxY + height / 2 - 4,
+    size: 9,
+    font,
+    color: rgb(0.39, 0.45, 0.55),
+    maxWidth: Math.max(1, width - 12),
+  });
+
+  return boxY - 6;
+}
+
+function richCellText(blocks: PdfBlock[]): string {
+  return blocks
+    .flatMap((block) => {
+      if (block.type === 'paragraph' || block.type === 'heading') {
+        return block.children.map((child) => child.text);
+      }
+
+      if (block.type === 'list') {
+        return block.items.flatMap((item) =>
+          item.children.map((child) => child.text),
+        );
+      }
+
+      if (block.type === 'image') {
+        return [block.altText ?? 'Image'];
+      }
+
+      if (block.type === 'table') {
+        return ['Nested table'];
+      }
+
+      return [];
+    })
+    .join(' ')
+    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
+    .trim();
+}
+
+function findAssetByDataUrl(
+  context: DrawContext,
+  dataUrl: string,
+): WriterAssetRecordV2 | null {
+  for (const asset of context.assetsById.values()) {
+    if (asset.dataUrl === dataUrl) {
+      return asset;
+    }
+  }
+
+  return null;
+}
```

# Expected result

```txt
Before:
Rich Text Box can visually contain tables/images in editor,
but flattened PDF export may drop or ignore rich table/image blocks.

After:
Flattened export handles rich text box content:
- paragraphs
- headings
- lists
- inline images
- rich tables

This supports the target workflow:
one Writer box = text + heading + table + image + styling.
```

--------------------------------------------------

Proceeding with the next coding slice.

# WR2-RICH.8 — Preflight rich content inside single Writer box

Now that Writer V2 can create/export a single rich text box containing headings, tables, images, and styled content, the export preflight must stop thinking only in terms of separate `tableBox` / `imageBox`.

Repo-grounding:

`writerExportPreflight.ts` currently validates separate `imageBox` / `tableBox` objects and generic text objects only. It has issue codes for `MISSING_IMAGE_ASSET`, `EMPTY_IMAGE_OBJECT`, `EMPTY_TABLE_OBJECT`, and `TABLE_SHAPE_MISMATCH`, but nothing for **rich images/tables inside `editorStateJson`**. 

`richTextRenderer.ts` already parses `PdfBlock` types for rich `table` and `image`. 

This patch adds preflight validation for rich content inside one `richTextBox`.

---

# Patch 1 — Extend Writer export preflight

## `frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/writerExportPreflight.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/writerExportPreflight.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/writerExportPreflight.ts
--- a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/writerExportPreflight.ts
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/writerExportPreflight.ts
@@
 import type { WriterElementV2 } from '../types';
 import type { WriterAssetRecordV2 } from '../persistence/indexedDbWriterStore';
 import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';
 import { useWriterV2Store } from '../writerV2Store';
+import {
+  editorStateToPdfBlocks,
+  type PdfBlock,
+} from './richTextRenderer';
@@
   | 'EMPTY_IMAGE_OBJECT'
   | 'EMPTY_TABLE_OBJECT'
   | 'TABLE_SHAPE_MISMATCH'
+  | 'RICH_IMAGE_EMPTY_SOURCE'
+  | 'RICH_IMAGE_UNSUPPORTED_FORMAT'
+  | 'RICH_TABLE_EMPTY'
+  | 'RICH_TABLE_LARGE'
+  | 'RICH_CONTENT_MAY_CLIP'
   | 'HIDDEN_OBJECTS_EXCLUDED';
@@
   if (TEXT_TYPES.has(element.type) && !element.plainText?.trim() && !element.editorStateJson?.trim()) {
     issues.push({
       code: 'EMPTY_TEXT_OBJECT',
       severity: 'warning',
       elementId: element.id,
       pageIndex: element.pageIndex,
       message: `Writer text object ${shortId(element.id)} is empty.`,
     });
   }
+
+  if (TEXT_TYPES.has(element.type)) {
+    collectRichContentIssues(element, issues);
+  }
 
   if (element.type === 'imageBox') {
     collectImageIssues(element, assetsById, issues);
```
