```diff
@@
   }
 }
+
+function collectRichContentIssues(
+  element: WriterElementV2,
+  issues: WriterExportPreflightIssue[],
+): void {
+  const blocks = editorStateToPdfBlocks(element.editorStateJson, element.plainText ?? '');
+
+  visitRichBlocks(blocks, (block) => {
+    if (block.type === 'image') {
+      collectRichImageIssues(element, block, issues);
+    }
+
+    if (block.type === 'table') {
+      collectRichTableIssues(element, block, issues);
+    }
+  });
+
+  const padding = element.style.padding ?? 8;
+  const availableHeight = Math.max(1, element.rect.height - padding * 2);
+  const estimatedHeight = estimateRichBlockHeight(
+    blocks,
+    element.style.fontSize ?? 11,
+  );
+
+  if (estimatedHeight > availableHeight * 1.25) {
+    issues.push({
+      code: 'RICH_CONTENT_MAY_CLIP',
+      severity: 'warning',
+      elementId: element.id,
+      pageIndex: element.pageIndex,
+      message: `Writer rich box ${shortId(element.id)} may clip content during flattened export.`,
+    });
+  }
+}
+
+function collectRichImageIssues(
+  element: WriterElementV2,
+  block: Extract<PdfBlock, { type: 'image' }>,
+  issues: WriterExportPreflightIssue[],
+): void {
+  if (!block.src) {
+    issues.push({
+      code: 'RICH_IMAGE_EMPTY_SOURCE',
+      severity: 'error',
+      elementId: element.id,
+      pageIndex: element.pageIndex,
+      message: `Writer rich box ${shortId(element.id)} contains an image block with no source.`,
+    });
+    return;
+  }
+
+  const supported =
+    block.src.startsWith('data:image/png') ||
+    block.src.startsWith('data:image/jpeg') ||
+    block.src.startsWith('data:image/jpg');
+
+  if (!supported) {
+    issues.push({
+      code: 'RICH_IMAGE_UNSUPPORTED_FORMAT',
+      severity: 'warning',
+      elementId: element.id,
+      pageIndex: element.pageIndex,
+      message: `Writer rich box ${shortId(element.id)} contains an image format that flattened export may replace with a placeholder.`,
+    });
+  }
+}
+
+function collectRichTableIssues(
+  element: WriterElementV2,
+  block: Extract<PdfBlock, { type: 'table' }>,
+  issues: WriterExportPreflightIssue[],
+): void {
+  if (block.rows.length === 0 || block.rows.every((row) => row.cells.length === 0)) {
+    issues.push({
+      code: 'RICH_TABLE_EMPTY',
+      severity: 'warning',
+      elementId: element.id,
+      pageIndex: element.pageIndex,
+      message: `Writer rich box ${shortId(element.id)} contains an empty table.`,
+    });
+    return;
+  }
+
+  const maxColumns = Math.max(0, ...block.rows.map((row) => row.cells.length));
+
+  if (block.rows.length > 20 || maxColumns > 8) {
+    issues.push({
+      code: 'RICH_TABLE_LARGE',
+      severity: 'warning',
+      elementId: element.id,
+      pageIndex: element.pageIndex,
+      message: `Writer rich box ${shortId(element.id)} contains a large table that may not fit inside the overlay box.`,
+    });
+  }
+}
+
+function visitRichBlocks(
+  blocks: PdfBlock[],
+  visitor: (block: PdfBlock) => void,
+): void {
+  for (const block of blocks) {
+    visitor(block);
+
+    if (block.type === 'table') {
+      for (const row of block.rows) {
+        for (const cell of row.cells) {
+          visitRichBlocks(cell.children, visitor);
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
+}
+
+function estimateRichBlockHeight(
+  blocks: PdfBlock[],
+  defaultFontSize: number,
+): number {
+  let height = 0;
+
+  for (const block of blocks) {
+    if (block.type === 'heading') {
+      if (block.tag === 'h1') {
+        height += 26;
+      } else if (block.tag === 'h2') {
+        height += 22;
+      } else {
+        height += 18;
+      }
+    } else if (block.type === 'paragraph') {
+      height += Math.max(16, defaultFontSize * 1.5);
+    } else if (block.type === 'list') {
+      height += Math.max(16, defaultFontSize * 1.5) * Math.max(1, block.items.length);
+    } else if (block.type === 'image') {
+      const requestedHeight = typeof block.height === 'number' ? block.height : 120;
+      height += Math.min(220, Math.max(40, requestedHeight)) + 10;
+    } else if (block.type === 'table') {
+      height += Math.max(24, block.rows.length * 24) + 10;
+    }
+  }
+
+  return height;
+}
 
 function collectImageIssues(
```

# Patch 2 — Add rich preflight tests

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/writerExportPreflight.richContent.test.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/writerExportPreflight.richContent.test.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/writerExportPreflight.richContent.test.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/writerExportPreflight.richContent.test.ts
@@
+import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import { PDFDocument } from 'pdf-lib';
+import type { WriterElementV2 } from '../types';
+import { runWriterExportPreflight } from './writerExportPreflight';
+
+async function samplePdf(): Promise<Uint8Array> {
+  const pdf = await PDFDocument.create();
+  pdf.addPage([612, 792]);
+  return pdf.save();
+}
+
+function richElement(
+  editorStateJson: string,
+  patch: Partial<WriterElementV2> = {},
+): WriterElementV2 {
+  return {
+    id: 'rich-1',
+    documentFingerprint: 'doc-1',
+    pageIndex: 0,
+    type: 'richTextBox',
+    rect: { x: 72, y: 360, width: 420, height: 220 },
+    plainText: 'Rich content',
+    editorStateJson,
+    style: {
+      zIndex: 1,
+      backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
+      borderColor: '#2563eb',
+      borderWidth: 1,
+      padding: 12,
+      opacity: 1,
+      fontSize: 11,
+      fontFamily: 'Helvetica',
+      textColor: '#111827',
+    },
+    createdAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
+    updatedAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
+    locked: false,
+    hidden: false,
+    ...patch,
+  };
+}
+
+function stateWithChildren(children: unknown[]): string {
+  return JSON.stringify({
+    root: {
+      type: 'root',
+      version: 1,
+      direction: null,
+      format: '',
+      indent: 0,
+      children,
+    },
+  });
+}
+
+function paragraph(text: string) {
+  return {
+    type: 'paragraph',
+    version: 1,
+    direction: null,
+    format: '',
+    indent: 0,
+    children: [
+      {
+        type: 'text',
+        version: 1,
+        text,
+        format: 0,
+        style: '',
+        mode: 'normal',
+        detail: 0,
+      },
+    ],
+  };
+}
+
+describe('writerExportPreflight rich content', () => {
+  it('flags empty rich image source as blocking error', async () => {
+    const result = await runWriterExportPreflight({
+      sourceBytes: await samplePdf(),
+      pageCount: 1,
+      elements: [
+        richElement(
+          stateWithChildren([
+            {
+              type: 'inline-image',
+              version: 1,
+              src: '',
+              altText: 'Missing image',
+              width: 120,
+              height: 80,
+            },
+          ]),
+        ),
+      ],
+      assets: [],
+    });
+
+    expect(result.hasErrors).toBe(true);
+    expect(result.issues.map((issue) => issue.code)).toContain('RICH_IMAGE_EMPTY_SOURCE');
+  });
+
+  it('warns for unsupported rich image format', async () => {
+    const result = await runWriterExportPreflight({
+      sourceBytes: await samplePdf(),
+      pageCount: 1,
+      elements: [
+        richElement(
+          stateWithChildren([
+            {
+              type: 'inline-image',
+              version: 1,
+              src: 'data:image/webp;base64,AA==',
+              altText: 'WebP image',
+              width: 120,
+              height: 80,
+            },
+          ]),
+        ),
+      ],
+      assets: [],
+    });
+
+    expect(result.hasWarnings).toBe(true);
+    expect(result.issues.map((issue) => issue.code)).toContain('RICH_IMAGE_UNSUPPORTED_FORMAT');
+  });
+
+  it('warns for large rich table', async () => {
+    const rows = Array.from({ length: 21 }, (_, rowIndex) => ({
+      type: 'tablerow',
+      version: 1,
+      children: Array.from({ length: 2 }, (_, colIndex) => ({
+        type: 'tablecell',
+        version: 1,
+        children: [paragraph(`R${rowIndex + 1}C${colIndex + 1}`)],
+      })),
+    }));
+
+    const result = await runWriterExportPreflight({
+      sourceBytes: await samplePdf(),
+      pageCount: 1,
+      elements: [
+        richElement(
+          stateWithChildren([
+            {
+              type: 'table',
+              version: 1,
+              children: rows,
+            },
+          ]),
+        ),
+      ],
+      assets: [],
+    });
+
+    expect(result.hasWarnings).toBe(true);
+    expect(result.issues.map((issue) => issue.code)).toContain('RICH_TABLE_LARGE');
+  });
+
+  it('warns when rich content may clip inside box', async () => {
+    const result = await runWriterExportPreflight({
+      sourceBytes: await samplePdf(),
+      pageCount: 1,
+      elements: [
+        richElement(
+          stateWithChildren(
+            Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, index) =>
+              paragraph(`Paragraph ${index + 1}`),
+            ),
+          ),
+          {
+            rect: { x: 72, y: 360, width: 420, height: 90 },
+          },
+        ),
+      ],
+      assets: [],
+    });
+
+    expect(result.hasWarnings).toBe(true);
+    expect(result.issues.map((issue) => issue.code)).toContain('RICH_CONTENT_MAY_CLIP');
+  });
+});
```

# Expected result

```txt
Before:
Preflight validates separate imageBox/tableBox, but not rich images/tables inside a single rich text box.

After:
Preflight checks rich text box content:
- empty rich image source = error
- unsupported rich image format = warning
- empty rich table = warning
- very large rich table = warning
- rich content likely to clip = warning
```

This keeps Writer V2 aligned with your target: **one rich overlay box that can safely contain text, heading, table, image, styling, and review content before export.**
-------------------------------------------------------
Proceeding with next coding slice.

# WR2-RICH.9 — Fit Rich Box to Content

This fixes the practical issue created by rich text + tables + images inside one Writer box:

```txt
User inserts heading/table/image
→ content may overflow/clips in flattened export
→ user needs one-click "Fit Box to Content"
```

This slice adds:

```txt
1. Rich content height estimator
2. Fit active rich box height to content
3. Preserve top edge while expanding/shrinking
4. Clamp to current PDF page bounds
5. Add "Fit content" button in Box control
6. Tests
```

Repo-grounding:

* `writerExportPreflight.ts` already warns with `RICH_CONTENT_MAY_CLIP` after previous slice.
* `writerV2Store.ts` already supports `UPDATE_WRITER_ELEMENT` via `dispatchWriterCommand`.
* Writer rects already use PDF coordinates, and previous keyboard handler notes PDF origin is bottom-left.
* `richTextRenderer.ts` already converts Lexical JSON into `PdfBlock[]`.

I still cannot run the repo in this chat runtime. Run the gates at the end before merge.

# Patch 1 — Add reusable rich content metrics

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/richContentMetrics.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/richContentMetrics.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/richContentMetrics.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/richContentMetrics.ts
@@
+import type { PdfBlock } from './richTextRenderer';
+
+export interface RichContentMetricsOptions {
+  fontSize: number;
+  availableWidth: number;
+  padding: number;
+}
+
+export interface RichContentMetrics {
+  estimatedHeight: number;
+  blockCount: number;
+  tableCount: number;
+  imageCount: number;
+  paragraphCount: number;
+  headingCount: number;
+  listCount: number;
+}
+
+export function estimateRichContentMetrics(
+  blocks: PdfBlock[],
+  options: RichContentMetricsOptions,
+): RichContentMetrics {
+  const counters = {
+    blockCount: 0,
+    tableCount: 0,
+    imageCount: 0,
+    paragraphCount: 0,
+    headingCount: 0,
+    listCount: 0,
+  };
+
+  const estimatedHeight = estimateBlocksHeight(blocks, options, counters);
+
+  return {
+    estimatedHeight,
+    ...counters,
+  };
+}
+
+function estimateBlocksHeight(
+  blocks: PdfBlock[],
+  options: RichContentMetricsOptions,
+  counters: Omit<RichContentMetrics, 'estimatedHeight'>,
+): number {
+  let height = 0;
+
+  for (const block of blocks) {
+    counters.blockCount += 1;
+
+    if (block.type === 'heading') {
+      counters.headingCount += 1;
+      height += headingHeight(block.tag, options.fontSize);
+      continue;
+    }
+
+    if (block.type === 'paragraph') {
+      counters.paragraphCount += 1;
+      height += paragraphHeight(block, options);
+      continue;
+    }
+
+    if (block.type === 'list') {
+      counters.listCount += 1;
+      height += Math.max(1, block.items.length) * Math.max(16, options.fontSize * 1.55);
+      continue;
+    }
+
+    if (block.type === 'image') {
+      counters.imageCount += 1;
+      const requestedHeight = typeof block.height === 'number' ? block.height : 120;
+      height += Math.min(260, Math.max(48, requestedHeight)) + 10;
+      if (block.caption) {
+        height += 16;
+      }
+      continue;
+    }
+
+    if (block.type === 'table') {
+      counters.tableCount += 1;
+      height += estimateTableHeight(block, options, counters);
+      continue;
+    }
+  }
+
+  return height;
+}
+
+function headingHeight(
+  tag: 'h1' | 'h2' | 'h3',
+  fontSize: number,
+): number {
+  if (tag === 'h1') return Math.max(28, fontSize * 2.2);
+  if (tag === 'h2') return Math.max(24, fontSize * 1.9);
+  return Math.max(20, fontSize * 1.65);
+}
+
+function paragraphHeight(
+  block: Extract<PdfBlock, { type: 'paragraph' }>,
+  options: RichContentMetricsOptions,
+): number {
+  const text = block.children.map((child) => child.text).join('');
+  const lineCount = estimateLineCount(text, options.availableWidth, options.fontSize);
+
+  return Math.max(16, lineCount * options.fontSize * 1.45);
+}
+
+function estimateTableHeight(
+  block: Extract<PdfBlock, { type: 'table' }>,
+  options: RichContentMetricsOptions,
+  counters: Omit<RichContentMetrics, 'estimatedHeight'>,
+): number {
+  const baseRowHeight = Math.max(24, options.fontSize * 2.2);
+  let height = 0;
+
+  for (const row of block.rows) {
+    let rowHeight = baseRowHeight;
+
+    for (const cell of row.cells) {
+      const nestedHeight = estimateBlocksHeight(cell.children, options, counters);
+      rowHeight = Math.max(rowHeight, nestedHeight + 10);
+    }
+
+    height += Math.min(90, rowHeight);
+  }
+
+  return height + 10;
+}
+
+function estimateLineCount(
+  text: string,
+  availableWidth: number,
+  fontSize: number,
+): number {
+  const safeText = text.trim();
+  if (!safeText) return 1;
+
+  const averageCharWidth = Math.max(4, fontSize * 0.52);
+  const charsPerLine = Math.max(12, Math.floor(availableWidth / averageCharWidth));
+
+  return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(safeText.length / charsPerLine));
+}
```

# Patch 3 — Add fit action

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichBoxFitActions.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichBoxFitActions.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichBoxFitActions.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichBoxFitActions.ts
@@
+import { PDFDocument } from 'pdf-lib';
+import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';
+import { editorStateToPdfBlocks } from './export/richTextRenderer';
+import { estimateRichContentMetrics } from './export/richContentMetrics';
+import { getActiveRichBoxStyleTarget } from './writerRichBoxStyleActions';
+import { ensureRichTextEditing } from './ensureRichTextEditing';
+import { useWriterV2Store } from './writerV2Store';
+
+export interface WriterRichBoxFitResult {
+  changed: boolean;
+  id: string | null;
+  message: string;
+  estimatedHeight?: number;
+  appliedHeight?: number;
+}
+
+const MIN_RICH_BOX_HEIGHT = 80;
+const MAX_RICH_BOX_HEIGHT_RATIO = 0.9;
+const EXTRA_EXPORT_SAFE_PADDING = 18;
+
+export async function fitActiveRichBoxToContent(): Promise<WriterRichBoxFitResult> {
+  const target = getActiveRichBoxStyleTarget();
+  const id = target?.id ?? await ensureRichTextEditing();
+
+  if (!id) {
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      id: null,
+      message: 'Open a PDF before fitting Writer rich box content.',
+    };
+  }
+
+  const store = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+  const session = useSessionStore.getState();
+  const element = store.elements.find((item) => item.id === id);
+  const sourceBytes = session.workingBytes ?? session.originalBytes;
+
+  if (!element || !sourceBytes) {
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      id,
+      message: 'Writer rich box or source PDF is not available.',
+    };
+  }
+
+  const pdf = await PDFDocument.load(sourceBytes);
+  const page = pdf.getPage(Math.max(0, Math.min(element.pageIndex, pdf.getPageCount() - 1)));
+  const pageSize = page.getSize();
+  const padding = element.style.padding ?? 8;
+  const blocks = editorStateToPdfBlocks(element.editorStateJson, element.plainText ?? '');
+  const metrics = estimateRichContentMetrics(blocks, {
+    fontSize: element.style.fontSize ?? 11,
+    availableWidth: Math.max(1, element.rect.width - padding * 2),
+    padding,
+  });
+
+  const requestedHeight = Math.ceil(
+    metrics.estimatedHeight + padding * 2 + EXTRA_EXPORT_SAFE_PADDING,
+  );
+  const maxPageHeight = Math.max(
+    MIN_RICH_BOX_HEIGHT,
+    Math.floor(pageSize.height * MAX_RICH_BOX_HEIGHT_RATIO),
+  );
+  const nextHeight = clamp(
+    requestedHeight,
+    MIN_RICH_BOX_HEIGHT,
+    maxPageHeight,
+  );
+
+  const currentTop = element.rect.y + element.rect.height;
+  const nextY = clamp(
+    currentTop - nextHeight,
+    0,
+    Math.max(0, pageSize.height - nextHeight),
+  );
+
+  const result = store.dispatchWriterCommand({
+    type: 'UPDATE_WRITER_ELEMENT',
+    id,
+    patch: {
+      rect: {
+        ...element.rect,
+        y: nextY,
+        height: nextHeight,
+      },
+    },
+  });
+
+  return {
+    changed: result.changed,
+    id,
+    message: result.changed
+      ? `Fitted Writer rich box to ${nextHeight}px height.`
+      : result.reason,
+    estimatedHeight: metrics.estimatedHeight,
+    appliedHeight: nextHeight,
+  };
+}
+
+function clamp(value: number, min: number, max: number): number {
+  return Math.max(min, Math.min(value, max));
+}
```

# Patch 5 — Metrics tests

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/richContentMetrics.test.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/richContentMetrics.test.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/richContentMetrics.test.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/richContentMetrics.test.ts
@@
+import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import type { PdfBlock } from './richTextRenderer';
+import { estimateRichContentMetrics } from './richContentMetrics';
+
+describe('richContentMetrics', () => {
+  it('estimates paragraph and heading content height', () => {
+    const blocks: PdfBlock[] = [
+      {
+        type: 'heading',
+        tag: 'h1',
+        children: [{ text: 'Title', bold: false, italic: false }],
+      },
+      {
+        type: 'paragraph',
+        alignment: 'left',
+        children: [{ text: 'A short paragraph.', bold: false, italic: false }],
+      },
+    ];
+
+    const metrics = estimateRichContentMetrics(blocks, {
+      fontSize: 11,
+      availableWidth: 300,
+      padding: 8,
+    });
+
+    expect(metrics.headingCount).toBe(1);
+    expect(metrics.paragraphCount).toBe(1);
+    expect(metrics.estimatedHeight).toBeGreaterThan(30);
+  });
+
+  it('counts images and tables', () => {
+    const blocks: PdfBlock[] = [
+      {
+        type: 'image',
+        src: 'data:image/png;base64,AA==',
+        altText: 'Image',
+        width: 100,
+        height: 80,
+      },
+      {
+        type: 'table',
+        rows: [
+          {
+            cells: [
+              {
+                children: [
+                  {
+                    type: 'paragraph',
+                    alignment: 'left',
+                    children: [{ text: 'Cell', bold: false, italic: false }],
+                  },
+                ],
+              },
+            ],
+          },
+        ],
+      },
+    ];
+
+    const metrics = estimateRichContentMetrics(blocks, {
+      fontSize: 11,
+      availableWidth: 300,
+      padding: 8,
+    });
+
+    expect(metrics.imageCount).toBe(1);
+    expect(metrics.tableCount).toBe(1);
+    expect(metrics.estimatedHeight).toBeGreaterThan(100);
+  });
+});
```

# Patch 6 — Fit action tests

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichBoxFitActions.test.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichBoxFitActions.test.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichBoxFitActions.test.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichBoxFitActions.test.ts
@@
+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import { PDFDocument } from 'pdf-lib';
+import type { WriterElementV2 } from './types';
+import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';
+import { useWriterV2Store } from './writerV2Store';
+import { fitActiveRichBoxToContent } from './writerRichBoxFitActions';
+
+async function samplePdf(): Promise<Uint8Array> {
+  const pdf = await PDFDocument.create();
+  pdf.addPage([612, 792]);
+  return pdf.save();
+}
+
+function richElement(editorStateJson: string): WriterElementV2 {
+  return {
+    id: 'rich-1',
+    documentFingerprint: 'doc-1',
+    pageIndex: 0,
+    type: 'richTextBox',
+    rect: { x: 72, y: 500, width: 420, height: 90 },
+    plainText: 'Rich content',
+    editorStateJson,
+    style: {
+      zIndex: 1,
+      backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
+      borderColor: '#2563eb',
+      borderWidth: 1,
+      padding: 12,
+      opacity: 1,
+      fontSize: 11,
+      fontFamily: 'Helvetica',
+      textColor: '#111827',
+    },
+    createdAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
+    updatedAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
+    locked: false,
+    hidden: false,
+  };
+}
+
+function stateWithParagraphs(count: number): string {
+  return JSON.stringify({
+    root: {
+      type: 'root',
+      version: 1,
+      direction: null,
+      format: '',
+      indent: 0,
+      children: Array.from({ length: count }, (_, index) => ({
+        type: 'paragraph',
+        version: 1,
+        direction: null,
+        format: '',
+        indent: 0,
+        children: [
+          {
+            type: 'text',
+            version: 1,
+            text: `Paragraph ${index + 1} with rich authoring content.`,
+            format: 0,
+            style: '',
+            mode: 'normal',
+            detail: 0,
+          },
+        ],
+      })),
+    },
+  });
+}
+
+describe('writerRichBoxFitActions', () => {
+  beforeEach(async () => {
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().clearWriterV2();
+    useSessionStore.getState().openDocument({
+      documentKey: 'doc-1',
+      fileName: 'sample.pdf',
+      bytes: await samplePdf(),
+      pageCount: 1,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('expands active rich box to fit estimated content', async () => {
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().hydrateWriterSession({
+      documentFingerprint: 'doc-1',
+      elements: [richElement(stateWithParagraphs(16))],
+    });
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().dispatchWriterCommand({
+      type: 'SET_ACTIVE_WRITER_ELEMENT',
+      id: 'rich-1',
+    });
+
+    const before = useWriterV2Store.getState().elements[0].rect;
+    const result = await fitActiveRichBoxToContent();
+    const after = useWriterV2Store.getState().elements[0].rect;
+
+    expect(result.changed).toBe(true);
+    expect(after.height).toBeGreaterThan(before.height);
+    expect(after.y + after.height).toBeLessThanOrEqual(792);
+  });
+
+  it('creates a rich box when no target exists', async () => {
+    const result = await fitActiveRichBoxToContent();
+    const state = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+
+    expect(result.changed).toBe(true);
+    expect(state.elements).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(state.elements[0].type).toBe('richTextBox');
+  });
+});
```

# Patch 8 — Add to certification

## `frontend/scripts/certify-writer-overlay-v2.mjs`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/scripts/certify-writer-overlay-v2.mjs b/frontend/scripts/certify-writer-overlay-v2.mjs
--- a/frontend/scripts/certify-writer-overlay-v2.mjs
```

# Expected user-visible result

```txt
Before:
Preflight can warn that rich content may clip, but user has no quick fix.

After:
Box menu includes "Fit content height".
It expands/shrinks the active rich Writer box to estimated content height.
It preserves the top edge and clamps within page bounds.
```

This is a high-ROI usability patch because it closes the loop between rich authoring and export preflight.

-------------------------------------------------------------
Proceeding with next coding slice.

# WR2-RICH.10 — Preflight “Fix clipped rich boxes” quick action

This closes the loop:

```txt
Preflight detects RICH_CONTENT_MAY_CLIP
→ user clicks Fix clipped rich boxes
→ affected rich boxes are fitted to content
→ preflight reruns
→ user can continue export
```

Repo-grounding:

* `WriterExportPreflightDialog.tsx` currently only shows issue groups and Cancel/Continue; it has no repair action. 
* Current preflight dialog already groups `errors`, `warnings`, and `info`. 
* `WriterV2ToolbarControls.tsx` already stores `pendingPreflight` and calls `runCurrentWriterExportPreflight()` before export. 
* Previous slice added `fitActiveRichBoxToContent()`. This slice adds bulk fitting by preflight issue IDs.

---

## `frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichBoxFitActions.ts`

```diff

@@
 import { PDFDocument } from 'pdf-lib';
+import type { PDFDocument as PdfLibDocument } from 'pdf-lib';
 import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';

 export interface WriterRichBoxFitResult {
   changed: boolean;

+export interface WriterRichBoxBulkFitResult {

+  fittedCount: number;

+
 const MIN_RICH_BOX_HEIGHT = 80;

 const EXTRA_EXPORT_SAFE_PADDING = 18;
+const FITTABLE_TYPES = new Set<WriterElementV2['type']>([
+  'richTextBox',
+  'textCallout',
+  'richNote',
+]);
 
 export async function fitActiveRichBoxToContent(): Promise<WriterRichBoxFitResult> {
   const target = getActiveRichBoxStyleTarget();
   const id = target?.id ?? await ensureRichTextEditing();
@@

+export async function fitRichBoxToContentById(
+  id: string,
+): Promise<WriterRichBoxFitResult> {
+  const store = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+  const session = useSessionStore.getState();
+  const sourceBytes = session.workingBytes ?? session.originalBytes;
+  const element = store.elements.find((item) => item.id === id);
+
+  if (!element || !sourceBytes) {
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      id,
+      message: 'Writer rich box or source PDF is not available.',
+    };
+  }
+
+  if (!isFittableRichBox(element)) {
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      id,
+      message: 'Writer element is not an editable rich box.',
+    };
+  }
+
+  const pdf = await PDFDocument.load(sourceBytes);
+  const fit = computeRichBoxFit(element, pdf);
+  const result = applyRichBoxFit(element, fit.nextY, fit.nextHeight);
+
+  return {
+    changed: result.changed,
+    id,
+    message: result.changed
+      ? `Fitted Writer rich box ${shortId(id)} to ${fit.nextHeight}px height.`
+      : result.reason,
+    estimatedHeight: fit.estimatedHeight,
+    appliedHeight: fit.nextHeight,
+  };
+}
+
+export async function fitRichBoxesToContentByIds(
+  ids: string[],
+): Promise<WriterRichBoxBulkFitResult> {
+  const uniqueIds = [...new Set(ids.filter(Boolean))];
+  const store = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+  const session = useSessionStore.getState();
+  const sourceBytes = session.workingBytes ?? session.originalBytes;
+
+  if (uniqueIds.length === 0) {
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      requestedCount: 0,
+      fittedCount: 0,
+      skippedCount: 0,
+      message: 'No Writer rich boxes were selected for fitting.',
+    };
+  }
+
+  if (!sourceBytes) {
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      requestedCount: uniqueIds.length,
+      fittedCount: 0,
+      skippedCount: uniqueIds.length,
+      message: 'Open a PDF before fitting Writer rich boxes.',
+    };
+  }
+
+  const pdf = await PDFDocument.load(sourceBytes);
+  let fittedCount = 0;
+  let skippedCount = 0;
+
+  for (const id of uniqueIds) {
+    const latest = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+    const element = latest.elements.find((item) => item.id === id);
+
+    if (!element || !isFittableRichBox(element)) {
+      skippedCount += 1;
+      continue;
+    }
+
+    const fit = computeRichBoxFit(element, pdf);
+    const result = applyRichBoxFit(element, fit.nextY, fit.nextHeight);
+
+    if (result.changed) {
+      fittedCount += 1;
+    } else {
+      skippedCount += 1;
+    }
+  }
+
+  return {
+    changed: fittedCount > 0,
+    requestedCount: uniqueIds.length,
+    fittedCount,
+    skippedCount,
+    message: `Fitted ${fittedCount} Writer rich box(es); skipped ${skippedCount}.`,
+  };
+}
+
+function computeRichBoxFit(
+  element: WriterElementV2,
+  pdf: PdfLibDocument,
+): {
+  nextY: number;
+  nextHeight: number;
+  estimatedHeight: number;
+} {
+  const page = pdf.getPage(Math.max(0, Math.min(element.pageIndex, pdf.getPageCount() - 1)));
+  const pageSize = page.getSize();
+  const padding = element.style.padding ?? 8;
+  const blocks = editorStateToPdfBlocks(element.editorStateJson, element.plainText ?? '');
+  const metrics = estimateRichContentMetrics(blocks, {
+    fontSize: element.style.fontSize ?? 11,
+    availableWidth: Math.max(1, element.rect.width - padding * 2),
+    padding,
+  });
+
+  const requestedHeight = Math.ceil(
+    metrics.estimatedHeight + padding * 2 + EXTRA_EXPORT_SAFE_PADDING,
+  );
+  const maxPageHeight = Math.max(
+    MIN_RICH_BOX_HEIGHT,
+    Math.floor(pageSize.height * MAX_RICH_BOX_HEIGHT_RATIO),
+  );
+  const nextHeight = clamp(
+    requestedHeight,
+    MIN_RICH_BOX_HEIGHT,
+    maxPageHeight,
+  );
+
+  const currentTop = element.rect.y + element.rect.height;
+  const nextY = clamp(
+    currentTop - nextHeight,
+    0,
+    Math.max(0, pageSize.height - nextHeight),
+  );
+
+  return {
+    nextY,
+    nextHeight,
+    estimatedHeight: metrics.estimatedHeight,
+  };
+}
+
+function applyRichBoxFit(
+  element: WriterElementV2,
+  nextY: number,
+  nextHeight: number,
+) {
+  return useWriterV2Store.getState().dispatchWriterCommand({
+    type: 'UPDATE_WRITER_ELEMENT',
+    id: element.id,
+    patch: {
+      rect: {
+        ...element.rect,
+        y: nextY,
+        height: nextHeight,
+      },
+    },
+  });
+}
+
+function isFittableRichBox(element: WriterElementV2): boolean {
+  return FITTABLE_TYPES.has(element.type) && !element.locked && !element.hidden;
+}
+
 function clamp(value: number, min: number, max: number): number {
   return Math.max(min, Math.min(value, max));
 }
+
+function shortId(id: string): string {
+  return id.length <= 8 ? id : id.slice(0, 8);
+}
```

# Patch 2 — Add preflight fix helper

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/writerPreflightFixes.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/writerPreflightFixes.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/writerPreflightFixes.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/writerPreflightFixes.ts
@@
+import { fitRichBoxesToContentByIds } from '../writerRichBoxFitActions';
+import type {
+  WriterExportPreflightIssue,
+  WriterExportPreflightResult,
+} from './writerExportPreflight';
+
+export function richContentClipIssueIds(
+  issues: WriterExportPreflightIssue[],
+): string[] {
+  return [
+    ...new Set(
+      issues
+        .filter((issue) => issue.code === 'RICH_CONTENT_MAY_CLIP')
+        .map((issue) => issue.elementId)
+        .filter(Boolean) as string[],
+    ),
+  ];
+}
+
+export function canFixRichContentClipIssues(
+  result: WriterExportPreflightResult,
+): boolean {
+  return richContentClipIssueIds(result.issues).length > 0;
+}
+
+export async function fixRichContentClipIssues(
+  result: WriterExportPreflightResult,
+): Promise<{
+  changed: boolean;
+  fixedCount: number;
+  message: string;
+}> {
+  const ids = richContentClipIssueIds(result.issues);
+
+  if (ids.length === 0) {
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      fixedCount: 0,
+      message: 'No clipped rich Writer boxes were found.',
+    };
+  }
+
+  const fit = await fitRichBoxesToContentByIds(ids);
+
+  return {
+    changed: fit.changed,
+    fixedCount: fit.fittedCount,
+    message: fit.message,
+  };
+}
```

# Patch 4 — Wire fix action in toolbar preflight flow

## `frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterV2ToolbarControls.tsx`

Add import:

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterV2ToolbarControls.tsx b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterV2ToolbarControls.tsx
--- a/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterV2ToolbarControls.tsx
+++ b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterV2ToolbarControls.tsx
@@
 import {
   runCurrentWriterExportPreflight,
   type WriterExportPreflightResult,
 } from '@/core/writer-v2/export/writerExportPreflight';
+import { fixRichContentClipIssues } from '@/core/writer-v2/export/writerPreflightFixes';
```

Add helper inside `WriterV2ToolbarControls` after `runWithWriterPreflight`:

```diff
@@
   const runWithWriterPreflight = async (
     label: string,
     action: () => Promise<void>,
   ) => {
@@
 
     await action();
   };
+
+  const fixPendingRichContentClipping = async () => {
+    if (!pendingPreflight) return;
+
+    try {
+      beginExportOperation('Fitting clipped Writer rich boxes...');
+
+      const fix = await fixRichContentClipIssues(pendingPreflight.result);
+      const refreshed = await runCurrentWriterExportPreflight();
+
+      setPendingPreflight({
+        ...pendingPreflight,
+        result: refreshed,
+      });
+
+      if (fix.changed) {
+        completeSaveOperation(fix.message);
+      } else {
+        failSaveOperation(fix.message);
+      }
+    } catch (error) {
+      failSaveOperation(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
+    }
+  };
```

Patch dialog rendering:

```diff
@@
       {pendingPreflight ? (
         <WriterExportPreflightDialog
           result={pendingPreflight.result}
           exportLabel={pendingPreflight.label}
           onCancel={() => setPendingPreflight(null)}
+          onFixRichContentClipping={fixPendingRichContentClipping}
           onContinue={() => {
             const action = pendingPreflight.action;
             setPendingPreflight(null);
             void action();
```

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/writerPreflightFixes.test.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/writerPreflightFixes.test.ts
new file mode 100644

+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/writerPreflightFixes.test.ts

+import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import type { WriterExportPreflightResult } from './writerExportPreflight';
+import {
+  canFixRichContentClipIssues,
+  richContentClipIssueIds,
+} from './writerPreflightFixes';
+
+function resultWithIssues(
+  issues: WriterExportPreflightResult['issues'],
+): WriterExportPreflightResult {
+  return {
+    ok: !issues.some((issue) => issue.severity === 'error'),
+    hasErrors: issues.some((issue) => issue.severity === 'error'),
+    hasWarnings: issues.some((issue) => issue.severity === 'warning'),
+    pageCount: 1,
+    totalElementCount: 1,
+    visibleElementCount: 1,
+    hiddenElementCount: 0,
+    textElementCount: 1,
+    tableElementCount: 0,
+    imageElementCount: 0,
+    assetCount: 0,
+    issues,
+    byPage: [
+      {
+        pageIndex: 0,
+        visibleElementCount: 1,
+        hiddenElementCount: 0,
+      },
+    ],
+  };
+}
+
+describe('writerPreflightFixes', () => {
+  it('extracts unique rich clip issue ids', () => {
+    const ids = richContentClipIssueIds([
+      {
+        code: 'RICH_CONTENT_MAY_CLIP',
+        severity: 'warning',
+        message: 'clip',
+        elementId: 'a',
+      },
+      {
+        code: 'RICH_CONTENT_MAY_CLIP',
+        severity: 'warning',
+        message: 'clip',
+        elementId: 'a',
+      },
+      {
+        code: 'OBJECT_LOW_OPACITY',
+        severity: 'warning',
+        message: 'opacity',
+        elementId: 'b',
+      },
+    ]);
+
+    expect(ids).toEqual(['a']);
+  });
+
+  it('reports fix availability only for rich clip issues', () => {
+    expect(
+      canFixRichContentClipIssues(
+        resultWithIssues([
+          {
+            code: 'RICH_CONTENT_MAY_CLIP',
+            severity: 'warning',
+            message: 'clip',
+            elementId: 'a',
+          },
+        ]),
+      ),
+    ).toBe(true);
+
+    expect(
+      canFixRichContentClipIssues(
+        resultWithIssues([
+          {
+            code: 'OBJECT_LOW_OPACITY',
+            severity: 'warning',
+            message: 'opacity',
+            elementId: 'b',
+          },
+        ]),
+      ),
+    ).toBe(false);
+  });
+});
```

```diff
+import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+import { fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
+import type { WriterExportPreflightResult } from '@/core/writer-v2/export/writerExportPreflight';
+import { WriterExportPreflightDialog } from './WriterExportPreflightDialog';
+
+function resultWithClipIssue(): WriterExportPreflightResult {
+  return {
+    ok: true,
+    hasErrors: false,
+    hasWarnings: true,
+    pageCount: 1,
+    totalElementCount: 1,
+    visibleElementCount: 1,
+    hiddenElementCount: 0,
+    textElementCount: 1,
+    tableElementCount: 0,
+    imageElementCount: 0,
+    assetCount: 0,
+    issues: [
+      {
+        code: 'RICH_CONTENT_MAY_CLIP',
+        severity: 'warning',
+        elementId: 'rich-1',
+        pageIndex: 0,
+        message: 'Writer rich box may clip.',
+      },
+    ],
+    byPage: [
+      {
+        pageIndex: 0,
+        visibleElementCount: 1,
+        hiddenElementCount: 0,
+      },
+    ],
+  };
+}
+
+describe('WriterExportPreflightDialog', () => {
+  it('shows fit action for rich clipping warnings', () => {
+    const onFix = vi.fn();
+
+    render(
+      <WriterExportPreflightDialog
+        result={resultWithClipIssue()}
+        exportLabel="Flattened PDF"
+        onCancel={vi.fn()}
+        onContinue={vi.fn()}
+        onFixRichContentClipping={onFix}
+      />,
+    );
+
+    const button = screen.getByRole('button', {
+      name: 'Fit clipped rich boxes (1)',
+    });
+
+    fireEvent.click(button);
+
+    expect(onFix).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+  });
+
+  it('does not show fit action without callback', () => {
+    render(
+      <WriterExportPreflightDialog
+        result={resultWithClipIssue()}
+        exportLabel="Flattened PDF"
+        onCancel={vi.fn()}
+        onContinue={vi.fn()}
+      />,
+    );
+
+    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', {
+      name: 'Fit clipped rich boxes (1)',
+    })).toBeNull();
+  });
+});
```

```bash
cd frontend

npm run typecheck
npx vitest run src/core/writer-v2/export/writerPreflightFixes.test.ts --pool=forks --maxWorkers=1
npx vitest run src/core/writer-v2/writerRichBoxFitActions.test.ts --pool=forks --maxWorkers=1

```

# Expected result

```txt
Before:
Preflight warns that rich content may clip, but the fix is manual.

After:
Preflight dialog shows:
"Fit clipped rich boxes (N)"

Clicking it:
- fits all rich boxes with RICH_CONTENT_MAY_CLIP
- reruns preflight
- keeps the export dialog open with updated issue status
```

This makes the Writer V2 export workflow much more professional: warning → repair → export, without forcing the user to hunt through objects manually.


----------------------------------------
# WR2-RICH.11 — Convert toolbar Table/Image into Rich Box insert actions

This removes the remaining silo behavior from the Writer toolbar:

```txt
Before:
Toolbar Table → activates separate tableBox tool
Toolbar Image → creates separate imageBox object

After:
Toolbar Table → inserts table inside active/new Rich Text Box
Toolbar Image → inserts image inside active/new Rich Text Box
```

This aligns with your target: **one rich overlay box containing text + heading + table + image + styling**.

---

# Patch 1 — Add rich insert actions

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichInsertActions.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichInsertActions.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichInsertActions.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichInsertActions.ts
@@
+import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';
+import type { WriterAssetRecordV2 } from './persistence/indexedDbWriterStore';
+import { ensureRichTextEditing } from './ensureRichTextEditing';
+import { useRichEditorCommandQueue } from './richEditorCommandQueue';
+import { useWriterV2Store } from './writerV2Store';
+
+export interface WriterRichInsertResult {
+  changed: boolean;
+  message: string;
+  elementId?: string | null;
+  assetId?: string;
+}
+
+export async function insertRichTableIntoActiveBox(
+  rows = 3,
+  cols = 3,
+): Promise<WriterRichInsertResult> {
+  const sourceBytes = currentSourceBytes();
+
+  if (!sourceBytes) {
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      message: 'Open a PDF before inserting a Writer table.',
+    };
+  }
+
+  const safeRows = clampInt(rows, 1, 50);
+  const safeCols = clampInt(cols, 1, 20);
+
+  useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().enqueue({
+    type: 'insertTable',
+    rows: safeRows,
+    cols: safeCols,
+  });
+
+  const elementId = await ensureRichTextEditing();
+
+  if (!elementId) {
+    useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().clear();
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      message: 'Unable to open a Writer rich box for table insertion.',
+    };
+  }
+
+  return {
+    changed: true,
+    elementId,
+    message: `Inserted ${safeRows}x${safeCols} table into Writer rich box.`,
+  };
+}
+
+export async function insertImageFileIntoRichBox(
+  file: File,
+): Promise<WriterRichInsertResult> {
+  const session = useSessionStore.getState();
+  const writer = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+  const sourceBytes = currentSourceBytes();
+
+  if (!sourceBytes) {
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      message: 'Open a PDF before inserting an image into Writer.',
+    };
+  }
+
+  if (!/^image\/(png|jpeg|webp)$/i.test(file.type)) {
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      message: 'Only PNG, JPG, and WEBP images are supported.',
+    };
+  }
+
+  const originalDataUrl = await readFileAsDataUrl(file);
+  const dataUrl = file.type.toLowerCase() === 'image/webp'
+    ? await renderImageDataUrlToPng(originalDataUrl)
+    : originalDataUrl;
+  const mimeType = file.type.toLowerCase() === 'image/webp'
+    ? 'image/png'
+    : file.type;
+  const imageSize = await measureImageDataUrl(dataUrl);
+  const documentFingerprint =
+    writer.currentDocumentFingerprint ??
+    session.documentKey ??
+    session.fileName ??
+    'writer-v2-unsaved-document';
+  const assetId = createWriterAssetId();
+  const now = new Date().toISOString();
+
+  const asset: WriterAssetRecordV2 = {
+    id: assetId,
+    documentFingerprint,
+    kind: 'image',
+    mimeType,
+    dataUrl,
+    width: imageSize.width,
+    height: imageSize.height,
+    createdAt: now,
+    updatedAt: now,
+  };
+
+  useWriterV2Store.getState().addWriterAssets([asset]);
+
+  useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().enqueue({
+    type: 'insertImage',
+    src: dataUrl,
+    altText: file.name,
+    width: Math.min(360, Math.max(80, imageSize.width)),
+    height: Math.min(240, Math.max(60, imageSize.height)),
+    caption: file.name,
+  });
+
+  const elementId = await ensureRichTextEditing();
+
+  if (!elementId) {
+    useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().clear();
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      assetId,
+      message: 'Unable to open a Writer rich box for image insertion.',
+    };
+  }
+
+  return {
+    changed: true,
+    elementId,
+    assetId,
+    message: 'Inserted image into Writer rich box.',
+  };
+}
+
+function currentSourceBytes(): Uint8Array | null {
+  const session = useSessionStore.getState();
+  return session.workingBytes ?? session.originalBytes ?? null;
+}
+
+function readFileAsDataUrl(file: File): Promise<string> {
+  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    const reader = new FileReader();
+
+    reader.onload = () => {
+      if (typeof reader.result !== 'string') {
+        reject(new Error('Image import did not produce a data URL.'));
+        return;
+      }
+
+      resolve(reader.result);
+    };
+
+    reader.onerror = () => reject(reader.error ?? new Error('Failed to read image file.'));
+    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
+  });
+}
+
+function measureImageDataUrl(
+  dataUrl: string,
+): Promise<{ width: number; height: number }> {
+  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    const image = new Image();
+
+    image.onload = () => {
+      resolve({
+        width: image.naturalWidth || image.width || 1,
+        height: image.naturalHeight || image.height || 1,
+      });
+    };
+
+    image.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Failed to measure imported image.'));
+    image.src = dataUrl;
+  });
+}
+
+function renderImageDataUrlToPng(dataUrl: string): Promise<string> {
+  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    const image = new Image();
+
+    image.onload = () => {
+      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
+      canvas.width = image.naturalWidth || image.width || 1;
+      canvas.height = image.naturalHeight || image.height || 1;
+
+      const context = canvas.getContext('2d');
+      if (!context) {
+        reject(new Error('Unable to create image conversion canvas.'));
+        return;
+      }
+
+      context.drawImage(image, 0, 0);
+      resolve(canvas.toDataURL('image/png'));
+    };
+
+    image.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Failed to convert WEBP image.'));
+    image.src = dataUrl;
+  });
+}
+
+function createWriterAssetId(): string {
+  if (globalThis.crypto?.randomUUID) {
+    return `writer-asset-${globalThis.crypto.randomUUID()}`;
+  }
+
+  return `writer-asset-${Date.now().toString(36)}-${Math.random()
+    .toString(36)
+    .slice(2)}`;
+}
+
+function clampInt(value: number, min: number, max: number): number {
+  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return min;
+  return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, Math.round(value)));
+}
```

# Patch 4 — Add tests for rich insert actions

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichInsertActions.test.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichInsertActions.test.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichInsertActions.test.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichInsertActions.test.ts
@@
+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+import { PDFDocument } from 'pdf-lib';
+import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';
+import { useRichEditorCommandQueue } from './richEditorCommandQueue';
+import { useWriterV2Store } from './writerV2Store';
+import {
+  insertImageFileIntoRichBox,
+  insertRichTableIntoActiveBox,
+} from './writerRichInsertActions';
+
+const ONE_BY_ONE_PNG =
+  'iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAAEAAAABCAQAAAC1HAwCAAAAC0lEQVR42mP8/x8AAwMCAO+/p9sAAAAASUVORK5CYII=';
+
+async function samplePdf(): Promise<Uint8Array> {
+  const pdf = await PDFDocument.create();
+  pdf.addPage([612, 792]);
+  return pdf.save();
+}
+
+function installImageStub() {
+  class TestImage {
+    onload: (() => void) | null = null;
+    onerror: (() => void) | null = null;
+    naturalWidth = 1;
+    naturalHeight = 1;
+    width = 1;
+    height = 1;
+
+    set src(_value: string) {
+      queueMicrotask(() => this.onload?.());
+    }
+  }
+
+  vi.stubGlobal('Image', TestImage);
+}
+
+describe('writerRichInsertActions', () => {
+  beforeEach(async () => {
+    vi.restoreAllMocks();
+    installImageStub();
+    useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().clear();
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().clearWriterV2();
+    useSessionStore.getState().openDocument({
+      documentKey: 'doc-1',
+      fileName: 'sample.pdf',
+      bytes: await samplePdf(),
+      pageCount: 1,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('inserts table command into a newly opened rich box', async () => {
+    const result = await insertRichTableIntoActiveBox(2, 4);
+    const state = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+
+    expect(result.changed).toBe(true);
+    expect(state.elements).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(state.elements[0].type).toBe('richTextBox');
+    expect(state.editingId).toBe(state.elements[0].id);
+    expect(useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().pending).toEqual([
+      {
+        type: 'insertTable',
+        rows: 2,
+        cols: 4,
+      },
+    ]);
+  });
+
+  it('clamps table dimensions', async () => {
+    await insertRichTableIntoActiveBox(200, 100);
+
+    expect(useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().pending).toEqual([
+      {
+        type: 'insertTable',
+        rows: 50,
+        cols: 20,
+      },
+    ]);
+  });
+
+  it('inserts image command and stores asset', async () => {
+    const bytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(ONE_BY_ONE_PNG), (char) => char.charCodeAt(0));
+    const file = new File([bytes], 'tiny.png', {
+      type: 'image/png',
+    });
+
+    const result = await insertImageFileIntoRichBox(file);
+    const state = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+    const pending = useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().pending;
+
+    expect(result.changed).toBe(true);
+    expect(state.assetRecords).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(state.elements).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(state.elements[0].type).toBe('richTextBox');
+    expect(pending).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(pending[0]).toMatchObject({
+      type: 'insertImage',
+      altText: 'tiny.png',
+      caption: 'tiny.png',
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('rejects insert without PDF', async () => {
+    useSessionStore.getState().clearDocument();
+
+    const result = await insertRichTableIntoActiveBox(3, 3);
+
+    expect(result.changed).toBe(false);
+    expect(result.message).toMatch(/open a pdf/i);
+    expect(useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().pending).toEqual([]);
+  });
+});
```

# WR2-RICH.12 — Convert Quick Templates into Rich Box snippets

This is the next cleanup after converting Toolbar Table/Image into rich-box inserts.

Current repo problem:

* `writerQuickTemplates.ts` still creates **separate overlay objects** using `createTextStampElement`, `createReviewLabelElement`, `createRichNoteElement`, and `createTextCalloutElement`. 
* The current `insertWriterQuickTemplate()` loads the PDF, calculates a centered rect, creates a separate Writer element, then dispatches `ADD_WRITER_ELEMENT`. 
* That keeps Quick Templates in the old silo model.

Target behavior:

```txt
Quick Templates panel:
  DRAFT
  REVIEWED
  Engineering Review
  Safety Review
  General Note
  Check Dimension
→ insert formatted snippet inside active/new Rich Text Box
→ no separate stamp/review/note/callout object
```

This keeps the UI direction consistent:

```txt
One Rich Box = text + heading + table + image + style + templates
```

I still cannot run npm tests inside this chat runtime. Apply the patch and run the gates at the end.

---

# Patch 1 — Add rich snippet command to queue

## `frontend/src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.ts
--- a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.ts
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.ts
@@
 export type PendingListCommand =
```

# Patch 2 — Make queued commands drain whenever queue changes

This is important. Previous queue-drain logic only drained when the inline editor mounted. But Quick Templates can be clicked **while the editor is already active**. This patch makes the inline editor drain new queued commands whenever `pending.length` changes.

## `frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterInlineEditor.tsx`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterInlineEditor.tsx b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterInlineEditor.tsx
--- a/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterInlineEditor.tsx
+++ b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterInlineEditor.tsx
@@
 import type { EditorState, LexicalEditor, TextFormatType } from 'lexical';
 import {
   $createParagraphNode,
+  $createTextNode,
   $getRoot,
   $getSelection,
   $insertNodes,
@@
 } from 'lexical';
@@
 import {
+  $createHeadingNode,
+  type HeadingTagType,
+} from '@lexical/rich-text';
+import {
   HeadingNode,
   toggleHeading,
 } from '@/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/headingHelpers';
@@
 function QueuedRichEditorCommandPlugin(): null {
   const [editor] = useLexicalComposerContext();
+  const pendingCount = useRichEditorCommandQueue((state) => state.pending.length);
 
   React.useEffect(() => {
     const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
       const commands = useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().drain();
@@
             case 'clearFormatting':
               clearEditorFormatting(editor);
               break;
+
+            case 'insertTemplateSnippet':
+              editor.update(() => {
+                const nodes = command.blocks.map((block) => {
+                  const node = block.kind === 'heading'
+                    ? $createHeadingNode(levelToTag(block.level ?? 2))
+                    : $createParagraphNode();
+
+                  if (block.text) {
+                    const textNode = $createTextNode(block.text);
+
+                    if (block.style) {
+                      textNode.setStyle(block.style);
+                    }
+
+                    node.append(textNode);
+                  }
+
+                  return node;
+                });
+
+                $insertNodes(nodes);
+              });
+              break;
 
             case 'focus':
               break;
           }
@@
 
     return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
-  }, [editor]);
+  }, [editor, pendingCount]);
 
   return null;
 }
+
+function levelToTag(level: 1 | 2 | 3): HeadingTagType {
+  return `h${level}` as HeadingTagType;
+}
```

Why this is grounded: the repo’s heading helper already uses `$createHeadingNode` and `HeadingTagType` from `@lexical/rich-text`.

```diff
+function templateToRichBlocks(
+  template: WriterQuickTemplate,
+): PendingRichTemplateBlock[] {
+  const textStyle = templateTextStyle(template);
+
+  switch (template.group) {
+    case 'stamp':
+      return [
+        {
+          kind: 'heading',
+          level: 2,
+          text: template.text,
+          style: textStyle,
+        },
+      ];
+
+    case 'review':
+      return [
+        {
+          kind: 'heading',
+          level: 3,
+          text: template.text,
+          style: textStyle,
+        },
+        {
+          kind: 'paragraph',
+          text: `Status: ${template.review?.status ?? 'open'} | Priority: ${template.review?.priority ?? 'medium'} | Category: ${template.review?.category ?? 'general'}`,
+        },
+        {
+          kind: 'paragraph',
+          text: 'Comment: ',
+        },
+      ];
+
+    case 'note':
+      return [
+        {
+          kind: 'heading',
+          level: 3,
+          text: template.text,
+          style: textStyle,
+        },
+        {
+          kind: 'paragraph',
+          text: 'Details: ',
+        },
+        {
+          kind: 'paragraph',
+          text: 'Action: ',
+        },
+      ];
+
+    case 'callout':
+      return [
+        {
+          kind: 'paragraph',
+          text: `Callout: ${template.text}`,
+          style: textStyle,
+        },
+        {
+          kind: 'paragraph',
+          text: 'Resolution: ',
+        },
+      ];
+  }
+}
+
+function templateTextStyle(template: WriterQuickTemplate): string {
+  const parts: string[] = [];
+
+  if (template.style.textColor) {
+    parts.push(`color: ${template.style.textColor}`);
+  }
+
+  if (template.style.fontSize) {
+    parts.push(`font-size: ${template.style.fontSize}px`);
+  }
+
+  if (template.style.backgroundColor && template.style.backgroundColor !== 'transparent') {
+    parts.push(`background-color: ${template.style.backgroundColor}`);
+  }
+
+  return parts.join('; ');
+}
+
+function applyTemplateReviewMetadata(
+  elementId: string,
+  template: WriterQuickTemplate,
+): void {
+  if (!template.review) return;
+
+  const store = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+  const element = store.elements.find((item) => item.id === elementId);
+
+  if (!element) return;
+
+  const now = new Date().toISOString();
+
+  store.dispatchWriterCommand({
+    type: 'UPDATE_WRITER_ELEMENT',
+    id: elementId,
+    patch: {
+      review: {
+        ...element.review,
+        ...template.review,
+        commentId: element.review?.commentId ?? createReviewId(),
+        createdAt: element.review?.createdAt ?? now,
+        updatedAt: now,
+      },
+    },
+  });
+}
 
 function createReviewId(): string {
```

# Expected result

```txt
Before:
Quick Templates created separate overlay objects:
- textStamp
- reviewLabel
- richNote
- textCallout

After:
Quick Templates insert rich snippets inside active/new Rich Text Box:
- stamp snippets as styled headings
- review snippets as heading + status/priority/category + comment line
- note snippets as heading + details/action lines
- callout snippets as callout/resolution lines

Remaining separate object tools can still exist for advanced use,
but Quick Templates now follow the main Writer V2 rich-box workflow.
```

Proceeding with next coding slice.

# WR2-RICH.13 — Convert Toolbar Text/Callout/Stamp/Review/Note into Rich Box actions

After the earlier Table/Image and Quick Template changes, the remaining toolbar silo is this group:

```txt
Text Box
Callout
Stamp
Review
Note
```

Current repo toolbar still toggles these as separate `activeWriterTool` overlay tools. 

This patch changes them to:

```txt
Text Box  → create/open main Rich Writer Box
Callout   → insert callout snippet into Rich Box
Stamp     → insert stamp snippet into Rich Box
Review    → insert review snippet into Rich Box
Note      → insert note snippet into Rich Box
```

`Copy Region` remains a tool mode because it is a region-selection workflow.

---

# Patch 1 — Add toolbar rich action controller

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichToolbarActions.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichToolbarActions.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichToolbarActions.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichToolbarActions.ts
@@
+import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';
+import { ensureRichTextEditing } from './ensureRichTextEditing';
+import {
+  insertWriterQuickTemplate,
+  type WriterQuickTemplateId,
+} from './writerQuickTemplates';
+import { useWriterV2Store } from './writerV2Store';
+
+export type WriterRichToolbarActionId =
+  | 'mainRichBox'
+  | 'calloutSnippet'
+  | 'stampSnippet'
+  | 'reviewSnippet'
+  | 'noteSnippet';
+
+export interface WriterRichToolbarActionResult {
+  changed: boolean;
+  id?: string | null;
+  message: string;
+}
+
+const TOOLBAR_TEMPLATE_BY_ACTION: Record<
+  Exclude<WriterRichToolbarActionId, 'mainRichBox'>,
+  WriterQuickTemplateId
+> = {
+  calloutSnippet: 'calloutCheckDimension',
+  stampSnippet: 'stampReviewed',
+  reviewSnippet: 'reviewEngineering',
+  noteSnippet: 'noteGeneral',
+};
+
+export async function runWriterRichToolbarAction(
+  actionId: WriterRichToolbarActionId,
+): Promise<WriterRichToolbarActionResult> {
+  const session = useSessionStore.getState();
+  const sourceBytes = session.workingBytes ?? session.originalBytes;
+
+  if (!sourceBytes || session.pageCount <= 0) {
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      id: null,
+      message: 'Open a PDF before using Writer rich authoring tools.',
+    };
+  }
+
+  useWriterV2Store.getState().setActiveWriterTool('select');
+
+  if (actionId === 'mainRichBox') {
+    const id = await ensureRichTextEditing({
+      starterTemplate: 'authoringCard',
+      includeStarterTable: false,
+    });
+
+    return {
+      changed: Boolean(id),
+      id,
+      message: id
+        ? 'Opened Writer rich authoring box.'
+        : 'Unable to open Writer rich authoring box.',
+    };
+  }
+
+  const templateId = TOOLBAR_TEMPLATE_BY_ACTION[actionId];
+  const result = await insertWriterQuickTemplate(templateId);
+
+  return {
+    changed: result.changed,
+    id: result.id ?? null,
+    message: result.message,
+  };
+}
```

```diff
+        disabled={!sourceBytes}
+        onClick={() => {
+          void runRichToolbarAction('mainRichBox', 'Opening Writer rich box');
+        }}
+        title="Create or open the main Writer rich authoring box"
       />
       <ResponsiveBtn
         icon={MessageSquareDot}
         label="Callout"
-        active={activeWriterTool === 'textCallout'}
-        onClick={() => toggleTool('textCallout')}
-        title="Writer V2 Text Callout"
+        disabled={!sourceBytes}
+        onClick={() => {
+          void runRichToolbarAction('calloutSnippet', 'Inserting Writer callout');
+        }}
+        title="Insert a callout snippet inside the active Writer rich box"
       />
       <ResponsiveBtn
         icon={Stamp}
         label="Stamp"
-        active={activeWriterTool === 'textStamp'}
-        onClick={() => toggleTool('textStamp')}
-        title="Text Stamp - borderless bold text (DRAFT, REVIEWED, etc.)"
+        disabled={!sourceBytes}
+        onClick={() => {
+          void runRichToolbarAction('stampSnippet', 'Inserting Writer stamp');
+        }}
+        title="Insert a stamp snippet inside the active Writer rich box"
       />
       <ResponsiveBtn
         icon={Tag}
         label="Review"
-        active={activeWriterTool === 'reviewLabel'}
-        onClick={() => toggleTool('reviewLabel')}
-        title="Review Label - amber highlight annotation"
+        disabled={!sourceBytes}

+          void runRichToolbarAction('reviewSnippet', 'Inserting Writer review');
+        }}
+        title="Insert a review snippet inside the active Writer rich box"
       />
       <ResponsiveBtn
         icon={NotebookPen}
         label="Note"
-        active={activeWriterTool === 'richNote'}
-        onClick={() => toggleTool('richNote')}
-        title="Rich Note - yellow notepad-style sticky"
+        disabled={!sourceBytes}
+        onClick={() => {
+          void runRichToolbarAction('noteSnippet', 'Inserting Writer note');
+        }}
+        title="Insert a note snippet inside the active Writer rich box"
       />
```

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichToolbarActions.test.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichToolbarActions.test.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRichToolbarActions.test.ts
@@
+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import { PDFDocument } from 'pdf-lib';
+import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';
+import { useRichEditorCommandQueue } from './richEditorCommandQueue';
+import { useWriterV2Store } from './writerV2Store';
+import { runWriterRichToolbarAction } from './writerRichToolbarActions';
+
+async function samplePdf(): Promise<Uint8Array> {
+  const pdf = await PDFDocument.create();
+  pdf.addPage([612, 792]);
+  return pdf.save();
+}
+
+describe('writerRichToolbarActions', () => {
+  beforeEach(async () => {
+    useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().clear();
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().clearWriterV2();
+    useSessionStore.getState().openDocument({
+      documentKey: 'doc-1',
+      fileName: 'sample.pdf',
+      bytes: await samplePdf(),
+      pageCount: 1,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('opens main rich authoring box', async () => {
+    const result = await runWriterRichToolbarAction('mainRichBox');
+    const state = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+
+    expect(result.changed).toBe(true);
+    expect(state.elements).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(state.elements[0].type).toBe('richTextBox');
+    expect(state.elements[0].plainText).toContain('Document Heading');
+    expect(state.editingId).toBe(state.elements[0].id);
+    expect(state.activeWriterTool).toBe('select');
+  });
+
+  it('inserts stamp snippet into rich box', async () => {
+    const result = await runWriterRichToolbarAction('stampSnippet');
+    const state = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+    const pending = useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().pending;
+
+    expect(result.changed).toBe(true);
+    expect(state.elements).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(state.elements[0].type).toBe('richTextBox');
+    expect(pending).toEqual([
+      {
+        type: 'insertTemplateSnippet',
+        blocks: [
+          expect.objectContaining({
+            kind: 'heading',
+            text: 'REVIEWED',
+          }),
+        ],
+      },
+    ]);
+  });
+
+  it('inserts review snippet and applies review metadata to rich box', async () => {
+    const result = await runWriterRichToolbarAction('reviewSnippet');
+    const state = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+
+    expect(result.changed).toBe(true);
+    expect(state.elements[0].type).toBe('richTextBox');
+    expect(state.elements[0].review?.status).toBe('open');
+    expect(state.elements[0].review?.priority).toBe('medium');
+    expect(state.elements[0].review?.category).toBe('engineering');
+  });
+
+  it('inserts note snippet into rich box', async () => {
+    const result = await runWriterRichToolbarAction('noteSnippet');
+    const pending = useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().pending;
+
+    expect(result.changed).toBe(true);
+    expect(pending[0]).toMatchObject({
+      type: 'insertTemplateSnippet',
+    });
+    expect(JSON.stringify(pending[0])).toContain('Note:');
+  });
+
+  it('rejects action when no PDF is open', async () => {
+    useSessionStore.getState().clearDocument();
+
+    const result = await runWriterRichToolbarAction('mainRichBox');
+
+    expect(result.changed).toBe(false);
+    expect(result.message).toMatch(/open a pdf/i);
+    expect(useWriterV2Store.getState().elements).toHaveLength(0);
+  });
+});
```
