# Expected result

```txt
Before:
Toolbar still pushed users into separate overlay object modes:
Text Box / Callout / Stamp / Review / Note.

After:
Toolbar becomes rich-box-first:
Writer Box opens the main rich authoring box.
Callout/Stamp/Review/Note insert snippets inside that box.
Copy Region remains the only region tool mode.
```

This removes the biggest remaining Writer V2 silo from the main toolbar.

--------------------------------------------------------------
# WR2-RICH.14 — Legacy Object Consolidator: convert old Writer objects into one Rich Box per page

This is the next high-ROI slice because the repo still has old object types:

```txt
textCallout
reviewLabel
textStamp
richNote
tableBox
imageBox
```

The type model confirms these are still active Writer element kinds.  The model also already carries table data, image asset IDs, review metadata, styles, and plain/editor text on each object. 

After the rich-box-first changes, users may still have old silo objects from earlier versions. This patch adds a safe migration action:

```txt
Convert current page legacy Writer objects
→ one richTextBox
→ legacy text/stamp/review/note become rich snippet paragraphs/headings
→ tableBox becomes rich table block
→ imageBox becomes rich inline-image block
→ original objects hidden, not deleted
```

This gives rollback safety and avoids data loss.

---

# Patch 1 — Add legacy consolidation engine

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerLegacyObjectConsolidation.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerLegacyObjectConsolidation.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerLegacyObjectConsolidation.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerLegacyObjectConsolidation.ts
@@
+import { PDFDocument } from 'pdf-lib';
+import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';
+import type {
+  PdfRect,
+  WriterElementV2,
+  WriterReviewMetadata,
+  WriterTableData,
+} from './types';
+import { createRichTextBoxElement } from './writerElementFactory';
+import { useWriterV2Store } from './writerV2Store';
+
+export interface WriterLegacyConsolidationResult {
+  changed: boolean;
+  id?: string;
+  convertedCount: number;
+  message: string;
+}
+
+const LEGACY_CONSOLIDATABLE_TYPES = new Set<WriterElementV2['type']>([
+  'textCallout',
+  'reviewLabel',
+  'textStamp',
+  'richNote',
+  'tableBox',
+  'imageBox',
+]);
+
+export async function consolidateCurrentPageLegacyWriterObjects(): Promise<WriterLegacyConsolidationResult> {
+  const session = useSessionStore.getState();
+  const sourceBytes = session.workingBytes ?? session.originalBytes;
+
+  if (!sourceBytes || session.pageCount <= 0) {
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      convertedCount: 0,
+      message: 'Open a PDF before consolidating Writer objects.',
+    };
+  }
+
+  const pdf = await PDFDocument.load(sourceBytes);
+  const pageIndex = Math.max(
+    0,
+    Math.min(session.viewState.currentPage - 1, pdf.getPageCount() - 1),
+  );
+
+  return consolidateLegacyWriterObjectsOnPage(pageIndex, sourceBytes);
+}
+
+export async function consolidateLegacyWriterObjectsOnPage(
+  pageIndex: number,
+  sourceBytes: Uint8Array,
+): Promise<WriterLegacyConsolidationResult> {
+  const store = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+  const candidates = store.elements
+    .filter((element) =>
+      element.pageIndex === pageIndex &&
+      !element.hidden &&
+      !element.locked &&
+      LEGACY_CONSOLIDATABLE_TYPES.has(element.type),
+    )
+    .sort((a, b) => {
+      const topDelta = (b.rect.y + b.rect.height) - (a.rect.y + a.rect.height);
+      if (Math.abs(topDelta) > 2) return topDelta;
+      return a.rect.x - b.rect.x;
+    });
+
+  if (candidates.length === 0) {
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      convertedCount: 0,
+      message: 'No legacy Writer objects found on this page.',
+    };
+  }
+
+  const pdf = await PDFDocument.load(sourceBytes);
+  const page = pdf.getPage(Math.max(0, Math.min(pageIndex, pdf.getPageCount() - 1)));
+  const pageSize = page.getSize();
+  const boundingRect = clampRectToPage(
+    expandRect(unionRects(candidates.map((element) => element.rect)), 18),
+    pageSize.width,
+    pageSize.height,
+  );
+  const richBoxRect = normalizeRichBoxRect(boundingRect, pageSize.width, pageSize.height);
+  const assetById = new Map(store.assetRecords.map((asset) => [asset.id, asset]));
+  const editorStateJson = createConsolidatedEditorStateJson(candidates, assetById);
+  const plainText = candidates
+    .map((element) => element.plainText ?? element.type)
+    .filter(Boolean)
+    .join('\n');
+  const maxZIndex = store.elements.reduce(
+    (max, element) => Math.max(max, element.style.zIndex),
+    0,
+  );
+  const documentFingerprint =
+    store.currentDocumentFingerprint ??
+    useSessionStore.getState().documentKey ??
+    useSessionStore.getState().fileName ??
+    'writer-v2-unsaved-document';
+  const consolidated = createRichTextBoxElement({
+    documentFingerprint,
+    pageIndex,
+    rect: richBoxRect,
+    text: plainText,
+    editorStateJson,
+    zIndex: maxZIndex + 1,
+  });
+
+  const now = new Date().toISOString();
+  const review = mergeReviewMetadata(candidates, now);
+  const result = store.dispatchWriterCommand({
+    type: 'BATCH_UPDATE_WRITER_ELEMENTS',
+    patches: [
+      ...candidates.map((element) => ({
+        id: element.id,
+        patch: {
+          hidden: true,
+          updatedAt: now,
+        },
+      })),
+      {
+        id: consolidated.id,
+        element: {
+          ...consolidated,
+          review,
+          style: {
+            ...consolidated.style,
+            backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
+            borderColor: '#2563eb',
+            borderWidth: 1,
+            padding: 12,
+            opacity: 1,
+            zIndex: maxZIndex + 1,
+          },
+        },
+      },
+    ],
+  } as never);
+
+  if (!result.changed) {
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      convertedCount: 0,
+      message: result.reason,
+    };
+  }
+
+  useWriterV2Store.getState().beginEditingWriterElement(consolidated.id);
+
+  return {
+    changed: true,
+    id: consolidated.id,
+    convertedCount: candidates.length,
+    message: `Consolidated ${candidates.length} legacy Writer object(s) into one rich box.`,
+  };
+}
+
+function createConsolidatedEditorStateJson(
+  elements: WriterElementV2[],
+  assetById: Map<string, { dataUrl?: string; width?: number; height?: number }>,
+): string {
+  return JSON.stringify({
+    root: {
+      type: 'root',
+      version: 1,
+      direction: null,
+      format: '',
+      indent: 0,
+      children: elements.flatMap((element) => legacyElementToLexicalNodes(element, assetById)),
+    },
+  });
+}
+
+function legacyElementToLexicalNodes(
+  element: WriterElementV2,
+  assetById: Map<string, { dataUrl?: string; width?: number; height?: number }>,
+): Record<string, unknown>[] {
+  if (element.type === 'tableBox') {
+    return tableDataToLexicalNodes(element.tableData);
+  }
+
+  if (element.type === 'imageBox') {
+    const assetId = element.imageAssetId ?? element.assetIds?.[0];
+    const asset = assetId ? assetById.get(assetId) : null;
+
+    if (!asset?.dataUrl) {
+      return [
+        paragraphNode(`Image placeholder: ${element.plainText ?? assetId ?? element.id}`),
+      ];
+    }
+
+    return [
+      {
+        type: 'inline-image',
+        version: 1,
+        src: asset.dataUrl,
+        altText: element.plainText ?? 'Image',
+        width: Math.max(60, Math.min(element.rect.width, asset.width ?? element.rect.width)),
+        height: Math.max(40, Math.min(element.rect.height, asset.height ?? element.rect.height)),
+        caption: element.plainText,
+      },
+    ];
+  }
+
+  if (element.type === 'textStamp') {
+    return [
+      headingNode(element.plainText ?? 'Stamp', 'h2', styleFromElement(element)),
+    ];
+  }
+
+  if (element.type === 'reviewLabel') {
+    return [
+      headingNode(element.plainText ?? 'Review', 'h3', styleFromElement(element)),
+      paragraphNode(reviewLine(element.review)),
+      paragraphNode('Comment: '),
+    ];
+  }
+
+  if (element.type === 'richNote') {
+    return [
+      headingNode(element.plainText ?? 'Note', 'h3', styleFromElement(element)),
+      paragraphNode('Details: '),
+    ];
+  }
+
+  if (element.type === 'textCallout') {
+    return [
+      paragraphNode(`Callout: ${element.plainText ?? ''}`, styleFromElement(element)),
+      paragraphNode('Resolution: '),
+    ];
+  }
+
+  return [paragraphNode(element.plainText ?? '')];
+}
+
+function tableDataToLexicalNodes(tableData?: WriterTableData): Record<string, unknown>[] {
+  if (!tableData || tableData.rows.length === 0) {
+    return [paragraphNode('Empty table')];
+  }
+
+  const headerRow = {
+    type: 'tablerow',
+    version: 1,
+    children: tableData.columns.map((column) => ({
+      type: 'tablecell',
+      version: 1,
+      children: [paragraphNode(column.label ?? '')],
+    })),
+  };
+
+  const rows = tableData.rows.map((row) => ({
+    type: 'tablerow',
+    version: 1,
+    children: row.cells.map((cell) => ({
+      type: 'tablecell',
+      version: 1,
+      colSpan: cell.colSpan,
+      rowSpan: cell.rowSpan,
+      children: [paragraphNode(cell.text)],
+    })),
+  }));
+
+  return [
+    {
+      type: 'table',
+      version: 1,
+      children: [headerRow, ...rows],
+    },
+  ];
+}
+
+function headingNode(
+  text: string,
+  tag: 'h1' | 'h2' | 'h3',
+  style = '',
+): Record<string, unknown> {
+  return {
+    type: 'heading',
+    tag,
+    version: 1,
+    direction: null,
+    format: '',
+    indent: 0,
+    children: [textNode(text, style)],
+  };
+}
+
+function paragraphNode(text: string, style = ''): Record<string, unknown> {
+  return {
+    type: 'paragraph',
+    version: 1,
+    direction: null,
+    format: '',
+    indent: 0,
+    children: text ? [textNode(text, style)] : [],
+  };
+}
+
+function textNode(text: string, style = ''): Record<string, unknown> {
+  return {
+    type: 'text',
+    version: 1,
+    text,
+    format: 0,
+    style,
+    mode: 'normal',
+    detail: 0,
+  };
+}
+
+function styleFromElement(element: WriterElementV2): string {
+  const parts: string[] = [];
+
+  if (element.style.textColor) {
+    parts.push(`color: ${element.style.textColor}`);
+  }
+
+  if (element.style.fontSize) {
+    parts.push(`font-size: ${element.style.fontSize}px`);
+  }
+
+  if (element.style.backgroundColor && element.style.backgroundColor !== 'transparent') {
+    parts.push(`background-color: ${element.style.backgroundColor}`);
+  }
+
+  return parts.join('; ');
+}
+
+function reviewLine(review?: WriterReviewMetadata): string {
+  if (!review) return 'Status: open';
+  return `Status: ${review.status} | Priority: ${review.priority} | Category: ${review.category}`;
+}
+
+function mergeReviewMetadata(
+  elements: WriterElementV2[],
+  now: string,
+): WriterReviewMetadata | undefined {
+  const first = elements.find((element) => element.review)?.review;
+
+  if (!first) return undefined;
+
+  return {
+    ...first,
+    commentId: first.commentId ?? createReviewId(),
+    createdAt: first.createdAt ?? now,
+    updatedAt: now,
+  };
+}
+
+function unionRects(rects: PdfRect[]): PdfRect {
+  const minX = Math.min(...rects.map((rect) => rect.x));
+  const minY = Math.min(...rects.map((rect) => rect.y));
+  const maxX = Math.max(...rects.map((rect) => rect.x + rect.width));
+  const maxY = Math.max(...rects.map((rect) => rect.y + rect.height));
+
+  return {
+    x: minX,
+    y: minY,
+    width: maxX - minX,
+    height: maxY - minY,
+  };
+}
+
+function expandRect(rect: PdfRect, amount: number): PdfRect {
+  return {
+    x: rect.x - amount,
+    y: rect.y - amount,
+    width: rect.width + amount * 2,
+    height: rect.height + amount * 2,
+  };
+}
+
+function normalizeRichBoxRect(
+  rect: PdfRect,
+  pageWidth: number,
+  pageHeight: number,
+): PdfRect {
+  const width = Math.min(Math.max(rect.width, 320), Math.max(120, pageWidth - 48));
+  const height = Math.min(Math.max(rect.height, 180), Math.max(120, pageHeight - 48));
+
+  return clampRectToPage(
+    {
+      x: rect.x,
+      y: rect.y,
+      width,
+      height,
+    },
+    pageWidth,
+    pageHeight,
+  );
+}
+
+function clampRectToPage(
+  rect: PdfRect,
+  pageWidth: number,
+  pageHeight: number,
+): PdfRect {
+  const width = Math.min(rect.width, pageWidth);
+  const height = Math.min(rect.height, pageHeight);
+
+  return {
+    x: Math.max(0, Math.min(rect.x, pageWidth - width)),
+    y: Math.max(0, Math.min(rect.y, pageHeight - height)),
+    width,
+    height,
+  };
+}
+
+function createReviewId(): string {
+  if (globalThis.crypto?.randomUUID) {
+    return `review-${globalThis.crypto.randomUUID()}`;
+  }
+
+  return `review-${Date.now().toString(36)}-${Math.random()
+    .toString(36)
+    .slice(2)}`;
+}
```

> Note: this uses `BATCH_UPDATE_WRITER_ELEMENTS`. If your current command type does not yet support adding a new element inside batch, use the fallback below.

---

# Patch 1B — Fallback if `BATCH_UPDATE_WRITER_ELEMENTS` cannot add new element

Replace the dispatch block in Patch 1 with two calls:

```ts
for (const element of candidates) {
  store.dispatchWriterCommand({
    type: 'UPDATE_WRITER_ELEMENT',
    id: element.id,
    patch: {
      hidden: true,
      updatedAt: now,
    },
  });
}

const addResult = useWriterV2Store.getState().dispatchWriterCommand({
  type: 'ADD_WRITER_ELEMENT',
  element: {
    ...consolidated,
    review,
    style: {
      ...consolidated.style,
      backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
      borderColor: '#2563eb',
      borderWidth: 1,
      padding: 12,
      opacity: 1,
      zIndex: maxZIndex + 1,
    },
  },
});
```

Then use `addResult.changed`.

# Patch 2 — Add UI action inside Writer Tools Hub

## `frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterToolsHub.tsx`

Add import:

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterToolsHub.tsx b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterToolsHub.tsx
--- a/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterToolsHub.tsx
+++ b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterToolsHub.tsx
@@
 import { WriterLayersPanel } from './WriterLayersPanel';
 import { WriterQuickTemplatesPanel } from './WriterQuickTemplatesPanel';
 import { WriterObjectSearchPanel } from './WriterObjectSearchPanel';
+import { consolidateCurrentPageLegacyWriterObjects } from '@/core/writer-v2/writerLegacyObjectConsolidation';
+import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';
```

Inside component:

```diff
@@
 export const WriterToolsHub: React.FC = () => {
+  const beginExportOperation = useSessionStore((state) => state.beginExportOperation);
+  const completeSaveOperation = useSessionStore((state) => state.completeSaveOperation);
+  const failSaveOperation = useSessionStore((state) => state.failSaveOperation);
@@
   const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = React.useState(initial.collapsed);
   const [tab, setTab] = React.useState<WriterToolsTab>(initial.tab);
+
+  const consolidateLegacyObjects = async () => {
+    try {
+      beginExportOperation('Consolidating legacy Writer objects...');
+      const result = await consolidateCurrentPageLegacyWriterObjects();
+
+      if (result.changed) {
+        completeSaveOperation(result.message);
+      } else {
+        failSaveOperation(result.message);
+      }
+    } catch (error) {
+      failSaveOperation(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
+    }
```

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerLegacyObjectConsolidation.test.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerLegacyObjectConsolidation.test.ts

+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import { PDFDocument } from 'pdf-lib';
+import type { WriterElementV2 } from './types';
+import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';
+import { useWriterV2Store } from './writerV2Store';
+import { consolidateCurrentPageLegacyWriterObjects } from './writerLegacyObjectConsolidation';
+
+async function samplePdf(): Promise<Uint8Array> {
+  const pdf = await PDFDocument.create();
+  pdf.addPage([612, 792]);
+  return pdf.save();
+}
+
+function legacyElement(
+  id: string,
+  type: WriterElementV2['type'],
+  plainText: string,
+  patch: Partial<WriterElementV2> = {},
+): WriterElementV2 {
+  return {
+    id,
+    documentFingerprint: 'doc-1',
+    pageIndex: 0,
+    type,
+    rect: { x: 72, y: 500 - Number(id.replace(/\D/g, '') || 0) * 40, width: 180, height: 40 },
+    plainText,
+    style: {
+      zIndex: 1,
+      backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
+      borderColor: '#2563eb',
+      borderWidth: 1,
+      padding: 8,
+      opacity: 1,
+      fontSize: 11,
+      textColor: '#111827',
+    },
+    createdAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
+    updatedAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
+    locked: false,
+    hidden: false,
+    ...patch,
+  };
+}
+
+describe('writerLegacyObjectConsolidation', () => {
+  beforeEach(async () => {
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().clearWriterV2();
+    useSessionStore.getState().openDocument({
+      documentKey: 'doc-1',
+      fileName: 'sample.pdf',
+      bytes: await samplePdf(),
+      pageCount: 1,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('consolidates legacy text objects into one rich text box and hides originals', async () => {
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().hydrateWriterSession({
+      documentFingerprint: 'doc-1',
+      elements: [
+        legacyElement('legacy-1', 'textStamp', 'REVIEWED'),
+        legacyElement('legacy-2', 'reviewLabel', 'ENGINEERING REVIEW', {
+          review: {
+            status: 'open',
+            priority: 'medium',
+            category: 'engineering',
+            commentId: 'comment-1',
+            createdAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
+            updatedAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
+          },
+        }),
+        legacyElement('legacy-3', 'richNote', 'Note:'),
+      ],
+    });
+
+    const result = await consolidateCurrentPageLegacyWriterObjects();
+    const state = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+    const rich = state.elements.find((element) => element.type === 'richTextBox');
+
+    expect(result.changed).toBe(true);
+    expect(result.convertedCount).toBe(3);
+    expect(rich).toBeTruthy();
+    expect(rich?.editorStateJson).toContain('REVIEWED');
+    expect(rich?.editorStateJson).toContain('ENGINEERING REVIEW');
+    expect(rich?.review?.category).toBe('engineering');
+    expect(state.elements.filter((element) => element.id.startsWith('legacy-')).every((element) => element.hidden)).toBe(true);
+    expect(state.editingId).toBe(rich?.id);
+  });
+
+  it('consolidates table data into rich table JSON', async () => {
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().hydrateWriterSession({
+      documentFingerprint: 'doc-1',
+      elements: [
+        legacyElement('legacy-1', 'tableBox', 'Table', {
+          tableData: {
+            columns: [
+              { id: 'col-1', label: 'A' },
+              { id: 'col-2', label: 'B' },
+            ],
+            rows: [
+              {
+                id: 'row-1',
+                cells: [
+                  { id: 'cell-1', text: 'A1' },
+                  { id: 'cell-2', text: 'B1' },
+                ],
+              },
+            ],
+          },
+        }),
+      ],
+    });
+
+    const result = await consolidateCurrentPageLegacyWriterObjects();
+    const rich = useWriterV2Store.getState().elements.find((element) => element.type === 'richTextBox');
+
+    expect(result.changed).toBe(true);
+    expect(rich?.editorStateJson).toContain('"type":"table"');
+    expect(rich?.editorStateJson).toContain('A1');
+    expect(rich?.editorStateJson).toContain('B1');
+  });
+
+  it('returns unchanged when no legacy objects exist', async () => {
+    const result = await consolidateCurrentPageLegacyWriterObjects();
+
+    expect(result.changed).toBe(false);
+    expect(result.convertedCount).toBe(0);
+    expect(result.message).toMatch(/no legacy writer objects/i);
+  });
+});
```

# Expected result

```txt
Before:
Old Writer objects remain as scattered silos.

After:
Writer Tools Hub has:
Convert page objects to Rich Box

It converts visible legacy page objects into one editable richTextBox and hides originals:
- textStamp → heading
- reviewLabel → heading + review line + comment line
- richNote → heading + details line
- textCallout → callout paragraph + resolution line
- tableBox → rich table
- imageBox → rich image if asset is available
```

This gives users a migration path from the old Writer V2 object model to the new rich-box-first workflow without deleting any old objects.
-----------------------------------------------------------------------
# WR2-RICH.15 — Disable legacy placement modes by default

This is the next cleanup slice after converting the toolbar and templates to rich-box-first behavior.

Current repo still has old placement routes:

* `WriterV2Tool` still includes old placement modes: `richTextBox`, `textCallout`, `textStamp`, `reviewLabel`, `richNote`, `tableBox`, `selectBoxCopy`. 
* `WriterOverlayLayer` still creates separate overlay objects from `activeWriterTool` by calling `createWriterElementFromPlacement()`. 
* `WriterOverlayLayer` still enables pointer events for `richTextBox`, `textCallout`, `textStamp`, `reviewLabel`, and `richNote` placement modes. 
* `writerPlacement.ts` still creates separate legacy objects for those tool modes. 

This patch keeps legacy code available for project compatibility, but prevents accidental new silo-object placement by default.

---

# Patch 1 — Add Writer tool policy

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerToolPolicy.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerToolPolicy.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerToolPolicy.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerToolPolicy.ts
@@
+import type { WriterV2Tool } from './writerV2Store';
+
+const LEGACY_PLACEMENT_TOOLS = new Set<WriterV2Tool>([
+  'richTextBox',
+  'textCallout',
+  'textStamp',
+  'reviewLabel',
+  'richNote',
+  'tableBox',
+]);
+
+const ACTIVE_PLACEMENT_TOOLS = new Set<WriterV2Tool>([
+  'selectBoxCopy',
+]);
+
+export function isLegacyWriterPlacementTool(tool: WriterV2Tool): boolean {
+  return LEGACY_PLACEMENT_TOOLS.has(tool);
+}
+
+export function isActiveWriterPlacementTool(tool: WriterV2Tool): boolean {
+  return ACTIVE_PLACEMENT_TOOLS.has(tool);
+}
+
+export function isLegacyWriterPlacementEnabled(): boolean {
+  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
+    return false;
+  }
+
+  return localStorage.getItem('writer-v2-enable-legacy-placement') === '1';
+}
+
+export function normalizeWriterToolForRichAuthoring(
+  tool: WriterV2Tool,
+): WriterV2Tool {
+  if (tool === 'select' || tool === 'selectBoxCopy') {
+    return tool;
+  }
+
+  if (isLegacyWriterPlacementTool(tool) && !isLegacyWriterPlacementEnabled()) {
+    return 'select';
+  }
+
+  return tool;
+}
+
+export function shouldWriterOverlayCapturePlacement(tool: WriterV2Tool): boolean {
+  if (tool === 'selectBoxCopy') {
+    return false;
+  }
+
+  if (isLegacyWriterPlacementTool(tool)) {
+    return isLegacyWriterPlacementEnabled();
+  }
+
+  return false;
+}
```


This means any old button/hotkey/test path that tries to set `textStamp`, `reviewLabel`, etc. will fall back to `select` unless the local debug flag is explicitly enabled.

```diff
+    expect(normalizeWriterToolForRichAuthoring('selectBoxCopy')).toBe('selectBoxCopy');

+

+
+    expect(isLegacyWriterPlacementEnabled()).toBe(true);
+    expect(normalizeWriterToolForRichAuthoring('textStamp')).toBe('textStamp');
+    expect(shouldWriterOverlayCapturePlacement('textStamp')).toBe(true);
+  });
```

---------------------------------------------
--------------------------------------------------------

You are right. I had **not fully audited the table/list/indent path** before. I was improving the rich-box architecture, but the repo shows a more basic failure: the existing ribbon functions are wired in a way that can silently no-op.

## Audit finding

The inline editor itself has the required Lexical pieces: `ListPlugin`, `CheckListPlugin`, `TablePlugin`, `ListNode`, `ListItemNode`, `TableNode`, `TableRowNode`, and `TableCellNode` are registered. So the missing plugin is **not** the primary issue.  

The real failures are:

```txt
1. WriterRibbon commands depend on activeEditor.
2. Many handlers immediately return when activeEditor is null.
3. Table helper silently returns when Lexical selection is lost.
4. List/indent commands dispatch without reliably refocusing the editor.
5. CSS/theme only styles checklist items; normal ul/ol bullets are not explicitly styled.
```

`insertTable()` and `insertTableFromData()` both stop if `$isRangeSelection(selection)` is false. That means a toolbar click can lose selection and table insertion silently does nothing.  

Also, the theme CSS only defines checklist visual styling. It has no explicit standard unordered/ordered list styling for normal bullets/numbers. 

Below is the focused fix.

---

# Fix Pack — Table / Bullet / Indent Reliability

## Patch 1 — Make table insertion focus-safe

### `frontend/src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableHelpers.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableHelpers.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableHelpers.ts
--- a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableHelpers.ts
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableHelpers.ts
@@
 import {
   $createParagraphNode,
+  $getRoot,
   $getSelection,
   $isRangeSelection,
   $createTextNode,
   $isElementNode,
+  type RangeSelection,
   type LexicalEditor,
 } from 'lexical';
@@
 export function insertTable(
   editor: LexicalEditor,
   rows: number,
   cols: number,
 ): void {
   const clampedRows = Math.max(1, Math.min(rows, 50));
   const clampedCols = Math.max(1, Math.min(cols, 20));
 
-  editor.update(() => {
-    const selection = $getSelection();
-    if (!$isRangeSelection(selection)) return;
+  runWithStableRangeSelection(editor, (selection) => {
     const tableNode = $createTableNodeWithDimensions(
       clampedRows,
       clampedCols,
       false, // includeHeaders
     );
@@
     const paragraph = $createParagraphNode();
     tableNode.insertAfter(paragraph);
     paragraph.selectStart();
   });
 }
@@
 export function insertTableFromData(
   editor: LexicalEditor,
   data: string[][],
 ): void {
   if (data.length === 0) return;
-  const cols = Math.max(...data.map((r) => r.length));
+  const safeData = data.slice(0, 50);
+  const cols = Math.min(20, Math.max(...safeData.map((r) => r.length)));
   if (cols === 0) return;
 
-  editor.update(() => {
-    const selection = $getSelection();
-    if (!$isRangeSelection(selection)) return;
-
+  runWithStableRangeSelection(editor, (selection) => {
     const tableNode = $createTableNodeWithDimensions(
-      data.length,
+      safeData.length,
       cols,
       false,
     );
@@
       const rowNode = rows[r];
       if (!(rowNode instanceof TableRowNode)) continue;
       const cells = rowNode.getChildren();
       for (let c = 0; c < cells.length; c++) {
         const cellNode = cells[c];
         if (!(cellNode instanceof TableCellNode)) continue;
-        const text = data[r]?.[c] ?? '';
+        const text = safeData[r]?.[c] ?? '';
         const paragraph = cellNode.getFirstChild();
         if (paragraph && $isElementNode(paragraph)) {
           paragraph.clear();
           paragraph.append($createTextNode(text));
         }
@@
     }
 
     selection.insertNodes([tableNode]);
+
+    const paragraph = $createParagraphNode();
+    tableNode.insertAfter(paragraph);
+    paragraph.selectStart();
   });
 }
+
+function runWithStableRangeSelection(
+  editor: LexicalEditor,
+  operation: (selection: RangeSelection) => void,
+): void {
+  editor.focus(() => {
+    editor.update(() => {
+      let selection = $getSelection();
+
+      if (!$isRangeSelection(selection)) {
+        $getRoot().selectEnd();
+        selection = $getSelection();
+      }
+
+      if (!$isRangeSelection(selection)) return;
+
+      operation(selection);
+    });
+  });
+}
```

Why this matters:

```txt
Before:
Toolbar click loses Lexical selection → insertTable returns silently.

After:
Table helper focuses editor and repairs missing selection by selecting document end.
```

## Patch 2 — Add reliable list/indent helpers

### New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/listCommandHelpers.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/listCommandHelpers.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/listCommandHelpers.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/listCommandHelpers.ts
@@
+import {
+  INDENT_CONTENT_COMMAND,
+  OUTDENT_CONTENT_COMMAND,
+  type LexicalEditor,
+} from 'lexical';
+import {
+  INSERT_CHECK_LIST_COMMAND,
+  INSERT_ORDERED_LIST_COMMAND,
+  INSERT_UNORDERED_LIST_COMMAND,
+  REMOVE_LIST_COMMAND,
+} from '@lexical/list';
+
+export type WriterListCommandKind =
+  | 'bullet'
+  | 'number'
+  | 'check'
+  | 'remove';
+
+export function applyWriterListCommand(
+  editor: LexicalEditor,
+  kind: WriterListCommandKind,
+): void {
+  editor.focus(() => {
+    if (kind === 'bullet') {
+      editor.dispatchCommand(INSERT_UNORDERED_LIST_COMMAND, undefined);
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (kind === 'number') {
+      editor.dispatchCommand(INSERT_ORDERED_LIST_COMMAND, undefined);
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (kind === 'check') {
+      editor.dispatchCommand(INSERT_CHECK_LIST_COMMAND, undefined);
+      return;
+    }
+
+    editor.dispatchCommand(REMOVE_LIST_COMMAND, undefined);
+  });
+}
+
+export function applyWriterIndentCommand(
+  editor: LexicalEditor,
+  direction: 'in' | 'out',
+): void {
+  editor.focus(() => {
+    editor.dispatchCommand(
+      direction === 'in'
+        ? INDENT_CONTENT_COMMAND
+        : OUTDENT_CONTENT_COMMAND,
+      undefined,
+    );
+  });
+}
```

```diff
+import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
```

## Verdict

Your observation is correct: **table/list/indent were not reliably functional**.

The root problem is not that Lexical cannot do it. The editor has table/list plugins. The problem is command wiring:

```txt
Toolbar click → editor selection lost / activeEditor null → command silently returns.
```

This patch makes table insertion selection-safe, makes list/indent commands focus-safe, and adds the missing visual CSS for bullets and numbering.
-----------------------------------------------

TABLE FIXES
Yes. Starting with the concrete failure path: **Table / Bullet / Number / Checklist / Indent / Outdent must never silently no-op.**

Repo audit confirms the editor has Lexical table/list plugins registered, so the issue is command wiring and selection loss, not missing plugins. `WriterInlineEditor` registers `ListPlugin`, `CheckListPlugin`, `TablePlugin`, `ListNode`, `ListItemNode`, `TableNode`, `TableRowNode`, and `TableCellNode`.   Current `insertTable()` silently returns if selection is not a range selection.  CSS also only styles checklist items, not normal bullets/numbers. 

Below is the coding patch.

# Patch A — Add command queue

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.ts
@@
+import { create } from 'zustand';
+
+export type PendingWriterListCommand =
+  | 'bullet'
+  | 'number'
+  | 'check'
+  | 'remove';
+
+export type PendingRichEditorCommand =
+  | {
+      type: 'insertTable';
+      rows: number;
+      cols: number;
+    }
+  | {
+      type: 'list';
+      command: PendingWriterListCommand;
+    }
+  | {
+      type: 'indent';
+    }
+  | {
+      type: 'outdent';
+    }
+  | {
+      type: 'focus';
+    };
+
+interface RichEditorCommandQueueState {
+  pending: PendingRichEditorCommand[];
+  enqueue: (command: PendingRichEditorCommand) => void;
+  drain: () => PendingRichEditorCommand[];
+  clear: () => void;
+}
+
+export const useRichEditorCommandQueue = create<RichEditorCommandQueueState>((set, get) => ({
+  pending: [],
+
+  enqueue: (command) =>
+    set((state) => ({
+      pending: [...state.pending, command],
+    })),
+
+  drain: () => {
+    const pending = get().pending;
+    set({ pending: [] });
+    return pending;
+  },
+
+  clear: () => set({ pending: [] }),
+}));
```

# Patch B — Add smart rich-box activation

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/ensureRichTextEditing.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/ensureRichTextEditing.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/ensureRichTextEditing.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/ensureRichTextEditing.ts
@@
+import { PDFDocument } from 'pdf-lib';
+import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';
+import type { WriterElementV2 } from './types';
+import { createRichTextBoxElement } from './writerElementFactory';
+import { useWriterV2Store } from './writerV2Store';
+
+const RICH_EDITABLE_TYPES = new Set<WriterElementV2['type']>([
+  'richTextBox',
+  'textCallout',
+  'richNote',
+]);
+
+export async function ensureRichTextEditing(): Promise<string | null> {
+  const writer = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+  const session = useSessionStore.getState();
+
+  const selected = writer.elements.find((element) =>
+    (writer.activeId === element.id || writer.selectedIds.includes(element.id)) &&
+    RICH_EDITABLE_TYPES.has(element.type) &&
+    !element.locked &&
+    !element.hidden,
+  );
+
+  if (selected) {
+    writer.beginEditingWriterElement(selected.id);
+    return selected.id;
+  }
+
+  const sourceBytes = session.workingBytes ?? session.originalBytes;
+
+  if (!sourceBytes || session.pageCount <= 0) {
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  const pdf = await PDFDocument.load(sourceBytes);
+  const pageIndex = Math.max(
+    0,
+    Math.min(session.viewState.currentPage - 1, pdf.getPageCount() - 1),
+  );
+  const page = pdf.getPage(pageIndex);
+  const pageSize = page.getSize();
+  const maxZIndex = writer.elements.reduce(
+    (max, element) => Math.max(max, element.style.zIndex),
+    0,
+  );
+
+  const element = createRichTextBoxElement({
+    documentFingerprint:
+      writer.currentDocumentFingerprint ??
+      session.documentKey ??
+      session.fileName ??
+      'writer-v2-unsaved-document',
+    pageIndex,
+    rect: {
+      x: 54,
+      y: Math.max(54, pageSize.height - 260),
+      width: Math.min(520, pageSize.width - 108),
+      height: 190,
+    },
+    text: '',
+    zIndex: maxZIndex + 1,
+  });
+
+  writer.dispatchWriterCommand({
+    type: 'ADD_WRITER_ELEMENT',
+    element,
+  });
+
+  useWriterV2Store.getState().beginEditingWriterElement(element.id);
+
+  return element.id;
+}
```

```diff
   return null;

+function QueuedRichEditorCommandPlugin(): null {
+  const [editor] = useLexicalComposerContext();
+  const pendingCount = useRichEditorCommandQueue((state) => state.pending.length);
+
+  React.useEffect(() => {
+    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
+      const commands = useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().drain();
+
+      if (commands.length === 0) {
+        return;
+      }
+
+      for (const command of commands) {
+        if (command.type === 'insertTable') {
+          insertTable(editor, command.rows, command.cols);
+        } else if (command.type === 'list') {
+          applyWriterListCommand(editor, command.command);
+        } else if (command.type === 'indent') {
+          applyWriterIndentCommand(editor, 'in');
+        } else if (command.type === 'outdent') {
+          applyWriterIndentCommand(editor, 'out');
+        } else {
+          editor.focus();
+        }
+      }
+    }, 0);
+
+    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
+  }, [editor, pendingCount]);
+
+  return null;
+}
```

```diff
@@
   const runWithFocusedEditor = React.useCallback((
     operation: (editor: LexicalEditor) => void,
   ): void => {
     const editor = activeEditor;
     if (!editor) return;
 
     editor.focus(() => {
       operation(editor);
     });
   }, [activeEditor]);
+
+  const runOrQueueEditorCommand = React.useCallback(async (
+    command: PendingRichEditorCommand,
+    immediate: (editor: LexicalEditor) => void,
+  ): Promise<void> => {
+    if (activeEditor) {
+      runWithFocusedEditor(immediate);
+      return;
+    }
+
+    useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().enqueue(command);
+    const editorElementId = await ensureRichTextEditing();
+
+    if (!editorElementId) {
+      useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().clear();
+    }
+  }, [activeEditor, runWithFocusedEditor]);
```

# Patch H — Tests

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.test.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.test.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.test.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.test.ts
@@
+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import { useRichEditorCommandQueue } from './richEditorCommandQueue';
+
+describe('richEditorCommandQueue', () => {
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().clear();
+  });
+
+  it('queues and drains table/list/indent commands in order', () => {
+    useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().enqueue({
+      type: 'insertTable',
+      rows: 2,
+      cols: 3,
+    });
+    useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().enqueue({
+      type: 'list',
+      command: 'bullet',
+    });
+    useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().enqueue({
+      type: 'indent',
+    });
+
+    expect(useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().pending).toHaveLength(3);
+
+    expect(useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().drain()).toEqual([
+      {
+        type: 'insertTable',
+        rows: 2,
+        cols: 3,
+      },
+      {
+        type: 'list',
+        command: 'bullet',
+      },
+      {
+        type: 'indent',
+      },
+    ]);
+    expect(useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().pending).toEqual([]);
+  });
+});
```

# What this fixes

```txt
Before:
Table/Bullet/Number/Checklist/Indent/Outdent could silently fail because:
- activeEditor was null
- toolbar click lost Lexical selection
- table helper returned silently
- normal list CSS was missing

After:
- Table command opens/creates a rich editor if needed.
- Table insertion repairs missing selection.
- Bullet/number/checklist commands focus editor before dispatch.
- Indent/outdent commands focus editor before dispatch.
- Normal bullet/number lists have visible CSS.
```

I would implement this before any more feature work. This is core reliability.
-----------------------------------------------------------------------
Proceeding with the next coding slice.

# WR2-RICH.16 — No Silent No-op Writer Command Guard

This adds a central command bridge and a static audit so Writer ribbon commands cannot silently fail again.

The audit is needed because the repo already showed the failure pattern: table insertion returns silently when there is no valid Lexical range selection.  The editor has the required List/Table plugins, so this is command routing/selection reliability, not missing plugin registration. 

---

# Patch A — Add command bridge

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerEditorCommandBridge.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerEditorCommandBridge.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerEditorCommandBridge.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerEditorCommandBridge.ts
@@
+import type { LexicalEditor } from 'lexical';
+import { ensureRichTextEditing } from './ensureRichTextEditing';
+import {
+  useRichEditorCommandQueue,
+  type PendingRichEditorCommand,
+} from './richEditorCommandQueue';
+
+export interface WriterEditorCommandBridgeRequest {
+  activeEditor: LexicalEditor | null;
+  command: PendingRichEditorCommand;
+  commandName: string;
+  apply: (editor: LexicalEditor) => void;
+}
+
+export interface WriterEditorCommandBridgeResult {
+  changed: boolean;
+  queued: boolean;
+  editorElementId: string | null;
+  message: string;
+}
+
+export async function runWriterEditorCommand(
+  request: WriterEditorCommandBridgeRequest,
+): Promise<WriterEditorCommandBridgeResult> {
+  if (request.activeEditor) {
+    request.activeEditor.focus(() => {
+      request.apply(request.activeEditor as LexicalEditor);
+    });
+
+    return {
+      changed: true,
+      queued: false,
+      editorElementId: null,
+      message: `Applied ${request.commandName}.`,
+    };
+  }
+
+  useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().enqueue(request.command);
+  const editorElementId = await ensureRichTextEditing();
+
+  if (!editorElementId) {
+    useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().clear();
+
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      queued: false,
+      editorElementId: null,
+      message: `Open a PDF before using ${request.commandName}.`,
+    };
+  }
+
+  return {
+    changed: true,
+    queued: true,
+    editorElementId,
+    message: `Queued ${request.commandName}.`,
+  };
+}
```

---

# Patch C — Add static no-op audit

## New file

`frontend/scripts/audit-writer-ribbon-no-silent-noop.mjs`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/scripts/audit-writer-ribbon-no-silent-noop.mjs b/frontend/scripts/audit-writer-ribbon-no-silent-noop.mjs
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/scripts/audit-writer-ribbon-no-silent-noop.mjs
@@
+import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { resolve } from 'node:path';
+
+const repoRoot = resolve(process.cwd());
+
+const targets = [
+  'src/components/writer-v2/WriterRibbon.tsx',
+  'src/components/writer-v2/WriterV2ToolbarControls.tsx',
+];
+
+const forbiddenRules = [
+  {
+    id: 'ACTIVE_EDITOR_OPTIONAL_DISPATCH',
+    pattern: /activeEditor\?\.[\s\S]{0,80}dispatchCommand/g,
+    message: 'Do not dispatch directly through activeEditor?. Use runWriterEditorCommand.',
+  },
+  {
+    id: 'ACTIVE_EDITOR_NULL_RETURN',
+    pattern: /if\s*\(\s*!activeEditor\s*\)\s*return\s*;/g,
+    message: 'Do not silently return when activeEditor is missing. Queue or disable explicitly.',
+  },
+  {
+    id: 'EDITOR_NULL_RETURN',
+    pattern: /if\s*\(\s*!editor\s*\)\s*return\s*;/g,
+    message: 'Do not silently return when editor is missing. Queue or disable explicitly.',
+  },
+  {
+    id: 'FILE_EDITOR_NULL_RETURN',
+    pattern: /if\s*\(\s*!file\s*\|\|\s*!editor\s*\)\s*return\s*;/g,
+    message: 'Image/file handlers must report or queue instead of silently returning.',
+  },
+  {
+    id: 'DIRECT_LEXICAL_LIST_DISPATCH',
+    pattern: /dispatchCommand\(\s*INSERT_(?:UNORDERED|ORDERED|CHECK)_LIST_COMMAND/g,
+    message: 'List commands must go through applyWriterListCommand / runWriterEditorCommand.',
+  },
+  {
+    id: 'DIRECT_LEXICAL_INDENT_DISPATCH',
+    pattern: /dispatchCommand\(\s*(?:INDENT_CONTENT_COMMAND|OUTDENT_CONTENT_COMMAND)/g,
+    message: 'Indent commands must go through applyWriterIndentCommand / runWriterEditorCommand.',
+  },
+];
+
+let failed = false;
+
+for (const target of targets) {
+  const path = resolve(repoRoot, target);
+  const source = readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
+
+  for (const rule of forbiddenRules) {
+    const matches = [...source.matchAll(rule.pattern)];
+
+    for (const match of matches) {
+      failed = true;
+      const index = match.index ?? 0;
+      const line = source.slice(0, index).split('\n').length;
+      const preview = source
+        .slice(index, index + 140)
+        .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
+        .trim();
+
+      console.error(
+        `[${rule.id}] ${target}:${line}\n  ${rule.message}\n  Found: ${preview}\n`,
+      );
+    }
+  }
+}
+
+if (failed) {
+  console.error('Writer ribbon no-op audit failed.');
+  process.exit(1);
+}
+
+console.log('Writer ribbon no-op audit passed.');
```

# Patch F — Tests for bridge

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerEditorCommandBridge.test.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerEditorCommandBridge.test.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerEditorCommandBridge.test.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerEditorCommandBridge.test.ts
@@
+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+import { PDFDocument } from 'pdf-lib';
+import type { LexicalEditor } from 'lexical';
+import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';
+import { useRichEditorCommandQueue } from './richEditorCommandQueue';
+import { useWriterV2Store } from './writerV2Store';
+import { runWriterEditorCommand } from './writerEditorCommandBridge';
+
+async function samplePdf(): Promise<Uint8Array> {
+  const pdf = await PDFDocument.create();
+  pdf.addPage([612, 792]);
+  return pdf.save();
+}
+
+function fakeEditor(): LexicalEditor {
+  return {
+    focus: vi.fn((callback?: () => void) => callback?.()),
+  } as unknown as LexicalEditor;
+}
+
+describe('writerEditorCommandBridge', () => {
+  beforeEach(async () => {
+    useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().clear();
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().clearWriterV2();
+    useSessionStore.getState().openDocument({
+      documentKey: 'doc-1',
+      fileName: 'sample.pdf',
+      bytes: await samplePdf(),
+      pageCount: 1,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('applies immediately when active editor exists', async () => {
+    const editor = fakeEditor();
+    const apply = vi.fn();
+
+    const result = await runWriterEditorCommand({
+      activeEditor: editor,
+      commandName: 'bullet list',
+      command: {
+        type: 'list',
+        command: 'bullet',
+      },
+      apply,
+    });
+
+    expect(result.changed).toBe(true);
+    expect(result.queued).toBe(false);
+    expect(editor.focus).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+    expect(apply).toHaveBeenCalledWith(editor);
+    expect(useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().pending).toEqual([]);
+  });
+
+  it('queues command and opens rich box when no active editor exists', async () => {
+    const result = await runWriterEditorCommand({
+      activeEditor: null,
+      commandName: 'insert table',
+      command: {
+        type: 'insertTable',
+        rows: 2,
+        cols: 3,
+      },
+      apply: vi.fn(),
+    });
+
+    const state = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+
+    expect(result.changed).toBe(true);
+    expect(result.queued).toBe(true);
+    expect(state.elements).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(state.elements[0].type).toBe('richTextBox');
+    expect(state.editingId).toBe(state.elements[0].id);
+    expect(useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().pending).toEqual([
+      {
+        type: 'insertTable',
+        rows: 2,
+        cols: 3,
+      },
+    ]);
+  });
+
+  it('clears queue and reports failure when no PDF is open', async () => {
+    useSessionStore.getState().clearDocument();
+
+    const result = await runWriterEditorCommand({
+      activeEditor: null,
+      commandName: 'insert table',
+      command: {
+        type: 'insertTable',
+        rows: 2,
+        cols: 3,
+      },
+      apply: vi.fn(),
+    });
+
+    expect(result.changed).toBe(false);
+    expect(result.message).toMatch(/open a pdf/i);
+    expect(useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().pending).toEqual([]);
+  });
+});
```

# Required gates

```bash
cd frontend

npm run typecheck
npm run audit:writer-no-noop
npx vitest run src/core/writer-v2/writerEditorCommandBridge.test.ts --pool=forks --maxWorkers=1
npx vitest run src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.test.ts --pool=forks --maxWorkers=1
npx vitest run src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/listCommandHelpers.test.ts --pool=forks --maxWorkers=1
npx vitest run src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableHelpers.test.ts --pool=forks --maxWorkers=1
npm run build
npm run certify:writer-overlay-v2
```

# Expected result

```txt
Before:
A visible WriterRibbon control could still contain:
- activeEditor?.dispatchCommand(...)
- if (!activeEditor) return;
- if (!editor) return;

After:
Certification fails if those silent no-op patterns return.

Visible Writer ribbon commands must:
- apply immediately to active editor, or
- queue and open/create a rich box, or
- explicitly disable/report failure.
```

This is the guardrail needed before continuing feature work.
-----------------------------------------------------------
Proceeding with next coding slice.

# WR2-RICH.17 — Fix remaining silent no-op WriterRibbon actions

The repo still has many direct `if (!activeEditor) return;` paths in `WriterRibbon.tsx`, including font, color, line height, clear formatting, link, table, and image upload. The fetched file shows the current ribbon still directly imports Lexical commands and keeps command handlers tied to `activeEditor`. 

This patch makes the remaining controls follow one rule:

```txt
If editor is active → apply immediately.
If editor is not active → create/open rich box and queue the command.
If PDF is not open → queue is cleared and no silent failure.
```

---

# Patch B — Add rich editor command applier

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerEditorCommandApplier.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerEditorCommandApplier.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerEditorCommandApplier.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerEditorCommandApplier.ts
@@
+import type { LexicalEditor, TextFormatType } from 'lexical';
+import {
+  $createParagraphNode,
+  $getRoot,
+  $getSelection,
+  $insertNodes,
+  $isRangeSelection,
+  FORMAT_ELEMENT_COMMAND,
+  FORMAT_TEXT_COMMAND,
+  type RangeSelection,
+} from 'lexical';
+import { $patchStyleText, $setBlocksType } from '@lexical/selection';
+import { TOGGLE_LINK_COMMAND } from '@lexical/link';
+import { INSERT_HORIZONTAL_RULE_COMMAND } from '@lexical/react/LexicalHorizontalRuleNode';
+import { insertTable } from './lexical-nodes/tableHelpers';
+import {
+  applyWriterIndentCommand,
+  applyWriterListCommand,
+} from './lexical-nodes/listCommandHelpers';
+import {
+  applyBlockStyle,
+  toggleBlockType,
+} from './lexical-nodes/blockHelpers';
+import { toggleHeading } from './lexical-nodes/headingHelpers';
+import { $createImageNode } from './lexical-nodes/ImageNode';
+import type { PendingRichEditorCommand } from './richEditorCommandQueue';
+
+export function applyPendingRichEditorCommand(
+  editor: LexicalEditor,
+  command: PendingRichEditorCommand,
+): void {
+  switch (command.type) {
+    case 'insertTable':
+      insertTable(editor, command.rows, command.cols);
+      return;
+
+    case 'list':
+      applyWriterListCommand(editor, command.command);
+      return;
+
+    case 'indent':
+      applyWriterIndentCommand(editor, 'in');
+      return;
+
+    case 'outdent':
+      applyWriterIndentCommand(editor, 'out');
+      return;
+
+    case 'formatText':
+      editor.focus(() => {
+        editor.dispatchCommand(FORMAT_TEXT_COMMAND, command.format);
+      });
+      return;
+
+    case 'formatElement':
+      editor.focus(() => {
+        editor.dispatchCommand(FORMAT_ELEMENT_COMMAND, command.format);
+      });
+      return;
+
+    case 'applyTextStyle':
+      applyTextStyle(editor, command.property, command.value);
+      return;
+
+    case 'applyBlockStyle':
+      applyBlockStyle(editor, command.style);
+      return;
+
+    case 'toggleBlockType':
+      toggleBlockType(editor, command.blockType);
+      return;
+
+    case 'heading':
+      toggleHeading(editor, command.level);
+      return;
+
+    case 'horizontalRule':
+      editor.focus(() => {
+        editor.dispatchCommand(INSERT_HORIZONTAL_RULE_COMMAND, undefined);
+      });
+      return;
+
+    case 'clearFormatting':
+      clearEditorFormatting(editor);
+      return;
+
+    case 'toggleLink':
+      editor.focus(() => {
+        editor.dispatchCommand(TOGGLE_LINK_COMMAND, command.url);
+      });
+      return;
+
+    case 'insertImage':
+      insertImage(editor, command);
+      return;
+
+    case 'focus':
+      editor.focus();
+      return;
+  }
+}
+
+export function applyTextStyle(
+  editor: LexicalEditor,
+  property: string,
+  value: string,
+): void {
+  runWithStableRangeSelection(editor, (selection) => {
+    $patchStyleText(selection, {
+      [property]: value,
+    });
+  });
+}
+
+export function clearEditorFormatting(editor: LexicalEditor): void {
+  runWithStableRangeSelection(editor, (selection) => {
+    ([
+      'bold',
+      'italic',
+      'underline',
+      'strikethrough',
+      'superscript',
+      'subscript',
+      'code',
+    ] as TextFormatType[]).forEach((format) => {
+      if (selection.hasFormat(format)) {
+        selection.formatText(format);
+      }
+    });
+
+    $patchStyleText(selection, {
+      color: '',
+      'background-color': '',
+      'font-size': '',
+      'font-family': '',
+      'letter-spacing': '',
+      'line-height': '',
+      'text-transform': '',
+    });
+
+    $setBlocksType(selection, () => $createParagraphNode());
+  });
+
+  applyWriterListCommand(editor, 'remove');
+}
+
+function insertImage(
+  editor: LexicalEditor,
+  command: Extract<PendingRichEditorCommand, { type: 'insertImage' }>,
+): void {
+  runWithStableRangeSelection(editor, () => {
+    $insertNodes([
+      $createImageNode(
+        command.src,
+        command.altText,
+        command.width,
+        command.height,
+        command.caption,
+      ),
+    ]);
+  });
+}
+
+function runWithStableRangeSelection(
+  editor: LexicalEditor,
+  operation: (selection: RangeSelection) => void,
+): void {
+  editor.focus(() => {
+    editor.update(() => {
+      let selection = $getSelection();
+
+      if (!$isRangeSelection(selection)) {
+        $getRoot().selectEnd();
+        selection = $getSelection();
+      }
+
+      if (!$isRangeSelection(selection)) {
+        return;
+      }
+
+      operation(selection);
+    });
+  });
+}
```

# Patch C — Make inline editor drain all queued commands

## Modify

`frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterInlineEditor.tsx`

The current editor already registers the required table/list plugins.  It does not yet drain command queue.

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterInlineEditor.tsx b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterInlineEditor.tsx
--- a/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterInlineEditor.tsx
+++ b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterInlineEditor.tsx
@@
 import {
   normalizeEditorStateJson,
   type WriterEditorSavePayload,
 } from '@/core/writer-v2/editor/writerEditorState';
 import { useActiveEditorStore } from '@/core/writer-v2/activeEditorStore';
+import { useRichEditorCommandQueue } from '@/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue';
+import { applyPendingRichEditorCommand } from '@/core/writer-v2/writerEditorCommandApplier';
 import './writerEditorTheme.css';
@@
         <ActiveEditorRegisterPlugin
           onEditor={(editor) => {
             editorRef.current = editor;
@@
               editor.focus();
             });
           }}
         />
+        <QueuedRichEditorCommandPlugin />
 
         <OnChangePlugin
@@
 function ActiveEditorRegisterPlugin({
@@
 
   return null;
 }
+
+function QueuedRichEditorCommandPlugin(): null {
+  const [editor] = useLexicalComposerContext();
+  const pendingCount = useRichEditorCommandQueue((state) => state.pending.length);
+
+  React.useEffect(() => {
+    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
+      const commands = useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().drain();
+
+      for (const command of commands) {
+        applyPendingRichEditorCommand(editor, command);
+      }
+    }, 0);
+
+    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
+  }, [editor, pendingCount]);
+
+  return null;
+}
```

# Patch E — Update command bridge to use the applier

## Modify

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerEditorCommandBridge.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerEditorCommandBridge.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerEditorCommandBridge.ts

@@
 export interface WriterEditorCommandBridgeRequest {
   activeEditor: LexicalEditor | null;
   command: PendingRichEditorCommand;
   commandName: string;
-  apply: (editor: LexicalEditor) => void;
+  apply?: (editor: LexicalEditor) => void;
 }
@@
   if (request.activeEditor) {
-    request.activeEditor.focus(() => {
-      request.apply(request.activeEditor as LexicalEditor);
-    });
+    if (request.apply) {
+      request.activeEditor.focus(() => {

```

This lets ribbon code simply submit a command; the bridge can apply it directly or queue it.

# Patch F — Replace WriterRibbon silent-return functions

## Modify

`frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterRibbon.tsx`

Add imports:

```diff
@@
 import { $createImageNode } from '@/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/ImageNode';
+import { runWriterEditorCommand } from '@/core/writer-v2/writerEditorCommandBridge';
+import type { PendingRichEditorCommand } from '@/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue';
```

Add helper after `runWithFocusedEditor`:

```diff
@@
   const runWithFocusedEditor = React.useCallback((
     operation: (editor: LexicalEditor) => void,
   ): void => {
     const editor = activeEditor;
     if (!editor) return;
 
     editor.focus(() => {
       operation(editor);
     });
   }, [activeEditor]);
+
+  const runRibbonCommand = React.useCallback((
+    commandName: string,
+    command: PendingRichEditorCommand,
+  ) => {
+    void runWriterEditorCommand({
+      activeEditor,
+      commandName,
+      command,
+    });
+  }, [activeEditor]);
```

Now replace functions.

# Patch H — Static audit

## New file

`frontend/scripts/audit-writer-ribbon-no-silent-noop.mjs`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/scripts/audit-writer-ribbon-no-silent-noop.mjs b/frontend/scripts/audit-writer-ribbon-no-silent-noop.mjs
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/scripts/audit-writer-ribbon-no-silent-noop.mjs
@@
+import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { resolve } from 'node:path';
+
+const files = [
+  'src/components/writer-v2/WriterRibbon.tsx',
+  'src/components/writer-v2/WriterV2ToolbarControls.tsx',
+];
+
+const rules = [
+  {
+    id: 'ACTIVE_EDITOR_NULL_RETURN',
+    pattern: /if\s*\(\s*!activeEditor\s*\)\s*return\s*;/g,
+  },
+  {
+    id: 'ACTIVE_EDITOR_OPTIONAL_DISPATCH',
+    pattern: /activeEditor\?\.[\s\S]{0,100}dispatchCommand/g,
+  },
+  {
+    id: 'DIRECT_LIST_DISPATCH',
+    pattern: /dispatchCommand\(\s*INSERT_(?:UNORDERED|ORDERED|CHECK)_LIST_COMMAND/g,
+  },
+  {
+    id: 'DIRECT_INDENT_DISPATCH',
+    pattern: /dispatchCommand\(\s*(?:INDENT_CONTENT_COMMAND|OUTDENT_CONTENT_COMMAND)/g,
+  },
+  {
+    id: 'DIRECT_LINK_DISPATCH',
+    pattern: /dispatchCommand\(\s*TOGGLE_LINK_COMMAND/g,
+  },
+];
+
+let failed = false;
+
+for (const file of files) {
+  const path = resolve(process.cwd(), file);
+  const source = readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
+
+  for (const rule of rules) {
+    const matches = [...source.matchAll(rule.pattern)];
+
+    for (const match of matches) {
+      failed = true;
+      const index = match.index ?? 0;
+      const line = source.slice(0, index).split('\n').length;
+      const preview = source
+        .slice(index, index + 120)
+        .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
+        .trim();
+
+      console.error(`[${rule.id}] ${file}:${line}`);
+      console.error(`  ${preview}`);
+    }
+  }
+}
+
+if (failed) {
+  console.error('Writer ribbon no silent no-op audit failed.');
+  process.exit(1);
+}
+
+console.log('Writer ribbon no silent no-op audit passed.');
```

```diff
+import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+import type { LexicalEditor } from 'lexical';
+import { FORMAT_TEXT_COMMAND } from 'lexical';
+import { applyPendingRichEditorCommand } from './writerEditorCommandApplier';
+
+function fakeEditor(): LexicalEditor {
+  return {
+    focus: vi.fn((callback?: () => void) => callback?.()),
+    dispatchCommand: vi.fn(),
+  } as unknown as LexicalEditor;
+}
+
+describe('writerEditorCommandApplier', () => {
+  it('applies text format command through focused editor', () => {
+    const editor = fakeEditor();
+
+    applyPendingRichEditorCommand(editor, {
+      type: 'formatText',
+      format: 'bold',
+    });
+
+    expect(editor.focus).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+    expect(editor.dispatchCommand).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
+      FORMAT_TEXT_COMMAND,
+      'bold',
+    );
+  });
+
+  it('focus command focuses editor', () => {
+    const editor = fakeEditor();
+
+    applyPendingRichEditorCommand(editor, {
+      type: 'focus',
+    });
+
+    expect(editor.focus).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+  });
+});
```

# Expected result

```txt
Before:
Font, color, highlight, line height, clear formatting, link, table, image, bullets, and indent could no-op when no active editor existed.

After:
All those commands go through one command path:
WriterRibbon → runWriterEditorCommand()
  active editor exists → apply command
  no active editor → queue command + create/open rich box
```

This is the real reliability layer needed before adding table row/column editing.
-------------------------------------------------------
Proceeding with the next coding slice.

# WR2-RICH.18 — Table Editing Tools: row/column/delete actions

This addresses the next table failure: even after insertion works, the table still feels dead because there are no row/column editing actions.

Repo-grounding:

* The repo uses Lexical `0.44.0`, including `@lexical/table` `0.44.0`. 
* `WriterInlineEditor` already registers `TableNode`, `TableRowNode`, `TableCellNode`, and `TablePlugin`.  
* So the editor can host tables; what is missing is a usable table command surface.

This patch adds:

```txt
Insert row below
Insert column right
Delete row
Delete column
Delete table
```

---

# Patch A — Add table editing helper

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableEditHelpers.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableEditHelpers.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableEditHelpers.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableEditHelpers.ts
@@
+import {
+  $createParagraphNode,
+  $getSelection,
+  $isRangeSelection,
+  type LexicalEditor,
+} from 'lexical';
+import {
+  $createTableCellNode,
+  $createTableRowNode,
+  $isTableCellNode,
+  $isTableNode,
+  $isTableRowNode,
+  TableCellHeaderStates,
+  type TableCellNode,
+  type TableNode,
+  type TableRowNode,
+} from '@lexical/table';
+
+export interface WriterTableSelectionState {
+  insideTable: boolean;
+  rowIndex: number;
+  columnIndex: number;
+  rowCount: number;
+  columnCount: number;
+}
+
+export type WriterTableEditAction =
+  | 'insertRowBelow'
+  | 'insertColumnRight'
+  | 'deleteRow'
+  | 'deleteColumn'
+  | 'deleteTable';
+
+export function getWriterTableSelectionState(
+  editor: LexicalEditor,
+): WriterTableSelectionState {
+  let state: WriterTableSelectionState = emptyTableState();
+
+  editor.getEditorState().read(() => {
+    const context = getActiveTableContext();
+
+    if (!context) {
+      state = emptyTableState();
+      return;
+    }
+
+    state = {
+      insideTable: true,
+      rowIndex: context.rowIndex,
+      columnIndex: context.columnIndex,
+      rowCount: context.rows.length,
+      columnCount: context.columnCount,
+    };
+  });
+
+  return state;
+}
+
+export function applyWriterTableEditAction(
+  editor: LexicalEditor,
+  action: WriterTableEditAction,
+): boolean {
+  let changed = false;
+
+  editor.focus(() => {
+    editor.update(() => {
+      const context = getActiveTableContext();
+
+      if (!context) {
+        changed = false;
+        return;
+      }
+
+      if (action === 'insertRowBelow') {
+        insertRowBelow(context);
+        changed = true;
+        return;
+      }
+
+      if (action === 'insertColumnRight') {
+        insertColumnRight(context);
+        changed = true;
+        return;
+      }
+
+      if (action === 'deleteRow') {
+        deleteRow(context);
+        changed = true;
+        return;
+      }
+
+      if (action === 'deleteColumn') {
+        deleteColumn(context);
+        changed = true;
+        return;
+      }
+
+      if (action === 'deleteTable') {
+        context.tableNode.remove();
+        changed = true;
+      }
+    });
+  });
+
+  return changed;
+}
+
+interface WriterTableContext {
+  tableNode: TableNode;
+  rowNode: TableRowNode;
+  cellNode: TableCellNode;
+  rows: TableRowNode[];
+  rowIndex: number;
+  columnIndex: number;
+  columnCount: number;
+}
+
+function getActiveTableContext(): WriterTableContext | null {
+  const selection = $getSelection();
+
+  if (!$isRangeSelection(selection)) {
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  const anchorNode = selection.anchor.getNode();
+  const cellNode = findParentTableCell(anchorNode);
+
+  if (!cellNode) {
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  const rowNode = cellNode.getParent();
+  const tableNode = rowNode?.getParent();
+
+  if (!$isTableRowNode(rowNode) || !$isTableNode(tableNode)) {
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  const rows = tableNode
+    .getChildren()
+    .filter($isTableRowNode);
+  const rowCells = rowNode
+    .getChildren()
+    .filter($isTableCellNode);
+  const rowIndex = rows.findIndex((row) => row.getKey() === rowNode.getKey());
+  const columnIndex = rowCells.findIndex((cell) => cell.getKey() === cellNode.getKey());
+  const columnCount = Math.max(
+    1,
+    ...rows.map((row) => row.getChildren().filter($isTableCellNode).length),
+  );
+
+  if (rowIndex < 0 || columnIndex < 0) {
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  return {
+    tableNode,
+    rowNode,
+    cellNode,
+    rows,
+    rowIndex,
+    columnIndex,
+    columnCount,
+  };
+}
+
+function findParentTableCell(node: ReturnType<NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof $getSelection>>['anchor']['getNode']>): TableCellNode | null {
+  let current = node;
+
+  while (current) {
+    if ($isTableCellNode(current)) {
+      return current;
+    }
+
+    const parent = current.getParent();
+    if (!parent) {
+      return null;
+    }
+
+    current = parent;
+  }
+
+  return null;
+}
+
+function insertRowBelow(context: WriterTableContext): void {
+  const newRow = $createTableRowNode();
+
+  for (let index = 0; index < context.columnCount; index += 1) {
+    newRow.append(createEmptyTableCell());
+  }
+
+  context.rowNode.insertAfter(newRow);
+
+  const firstCell = newRow.getFirstChild();
+  if ($isTableCellNode(firstCell)) {
+    firstCell.selectStart();
+  }
+}
+
+function insertColumnRight(context: WriterTableContext): void {
+  let selectedNewCell: TableCellNode | null = null;
+
+  for (const row of context.rows) {
+    const cells = row.getChildren().filter($isTableCellNode);
+    const referenceCell = cells[Math.min(context.columnIndex, cells.length - 1)];
+    const newCell = createEmptyTableCell();
+
+    if (referenceCell) {
+      referenceCell.insertAfter(newCell);
+    } else {
+      row.append(newCell);
+    }
+
+    if (row.getKey() === context.rowNode.getKey()) {
+      selectedNewCell = newCell;
+    }
+  }
+
+  selectedNewCell?.selectStart();
+}
+
+function deleteRow(context: WriterTableContext): void {
+  if (context.rows.length <= 1) {
+    context.tableNode.remove();
+    return;
+  }
+
+  const nextRow =
+    context.rows[context.rowIndex + 1] ??
+    context.rows[context.rowIndex - 1] ??
+    null;
+
+  context.rowNode.remove();
+
+  const nextCell = nextRow?.getFirstChild();
+  if ($isTableCellNode(nextCell)) {
+    nextCell.selectStart();
+  }
+}
+
+function deleteColumn(context: WriterTableContext): void {
+  if (context.columnCount <= 1) {
+    context.tableNode.remove();
+    return;
+  }
+
+  let nextSelectionCell: TableCellNode | null = null;
+
+  for (const row of context.rows) {
+    const cells = row.getChildren().filter($isTableCellNode);
+    const cellToRemove = cells[context.columnIndex];
+    const fallbackCell =
+      cells[context.columnIndex + 1] ??
+      cells[context.columnIndex - 1] ??
+      null;
+
+    if (row.getKey() === context.rowNode.getKey()) {
+      nextSelectionCell = fallbackCell;
+    }
+
+    cellToRemove?.remove();
+  }
+
+  nextSelectionCell?.selectStart();
+}
+
+function createEmptyTableCell(): TableCellNode {
+  const cell = $createTableCellNode(TableCellHeaderStates.NO_STATUS);
+  cell.append($createParagraphNode());
+  return cell;
+}
+
+function emptyTableState(): WriterTableSelectionState {
+  return {
+    insideTable: false,
+    rowIndex: -1,
+    columnIndex: -1,
+    rowCount: 0,
+    columnCount: 0,
+  };
+}
```

### If TypeScript complains about the `findParentTableCell` parameter type

Use this simpler import:

```ts
import type { LexicalNode } from 'lexical';
```

Then replace the signature:

```ts
function findParentTableCell(node: LexicalNode): TableCellNode | null {
```

That is the safer version if the complex inferred type is not accepted.

# Patch B — Add table command control component

## New file

`frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterTableActionsControl.tsx`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterTableActionsControl.tsx b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterTableActionsControl.tsx
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterTableActionsControl.tsx
@@
+import React from 'react';
+import { Rows3, Columns3, Trash2, Table as TableIcon } from 'lucide-react';
+import { useActiveEditorStore } from '@/core/writer-v2/activeEditorStore';
+import {
+  applyWriterTableEditAction,
+  getWriterTableSelectionState,
+  type WriterTableEditAction,
+  type WriterTableSelectionState,
+} from '@/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableEditHelpers';
+
+export const WriterTableActionsControl: React.FC = () => {
+  const activeEditor = useActiveEditorStore((state) => state.activeEditor);
+  const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false);
+  const [state, setState] = React.useState<WriterTableSelectionState>({
+    insideTable: false,
+    rowIndex: -1,
+    columnIndex: -1,
+    rowCount: 0,
+    columnCount: 0,
+  });
+
+  React.useEffect(() => {
+    if (!activeEditor) {
+      setState({
+        insideTable: false,
+        rowIndex: -1,
+        columnIndex: -1,
+        rowCount: 0,
+        columnCount: 0,
+      });
+      return;
+    }
+
+    const update = () => {
+      setState(getWriterTableSelectionState(activeEditor));
+    };
+
+    update();
+
+    return activeEditor.registerUpdateListener(update);
+  }, [activeEditor]);
+
+  const disabled = !activeEditor || !state.insideTable;
+
+  const runAction = (action: WriterTableEditAction) => {
+    if (!activeEditor) return;
+
+    applyWriterTableEditAction(activeEditor, action);
+    setState(getWriterTableSelectionState(activeEditor));
+  };
+
+  return (
+    <div
+      data-writer-table-actions-control="true"
+      style={{
+        position: 'relative',
+        display: 'inline-flex',
+        alignItems: 'center',
+      }}
+    >
+      <button
+        type="button"
+        aria-label="Table actions"
+        title={disabled ? 'Place the cursor inside a table' : 'Edit selected table'}
+        disabled={disabled}
+        onClick={() => setOpen((value) => !value)}
+        style={{
+          height: 30,
+          borderRadius: 7,
+          border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
+          background: open ? '#eff6ff' : '#ffffff',
+          color: disabled ? '#94a3b8' : open ? '#1d4ed8' : '#334155',
+          display: 'inline-flex',
+          alignItems: 'center',
+          gap: 6,
+          padding: '0 8px',
+          fontSize: 11,
+          fontWeight: 800,
+          cursor: disabled ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
+        }}
+      >
+        <TableIcon size={15} />
+        Table
+      </button>
+
+      {open && !disabled ? (
+        <div
+          role="dialog"
+          aria-label="Writer table actions"
+          style={{
+            position: 'absolute',
+            top: 36,
+            left: 0,
+            zIndex: 260,
+            width: 230,
+            padding: 10,
+            borderRadius: 10,
+            border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
+            background: '#ffffff',
+            boxShadow: '0 14px 36px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.18)',
+            display: 'grid',
+            gap: 8,
+          }}
+        >
+          <div
+            style={{
+              fontSize: 11,
+              color: '#475569',
+            }}
+          >
+            Cell R{state.rowIndex + 1} / C{state.columnIndex + 1}
+            {' · '}
+            {state.rowCount}×{state.columnCount}
+          </div>
+
+          <ActionButton
+            icon={<Rows3 size={14} />}
+            label="Insert row below"
+            onClick={() => runAction('insertRowBelow')}
+          />
+          <ActionButton
+            icon={<Columns3 size={14} />}
+            label="Insert column right"
+            onClick={() => runAction('insertColumnRight')}
+          />
+          <ActionButton
+            icon={<Trash2 size={14} />}
+            label="Delete row"
+            danger
+            onClick={() => runAction('deleteRow')}
+          />
+          <ActionButton
+            icon={<Trash2 size={14} />}
+            label="Delete column"
+            danger
+            onClick={() => runAction('deleteColumn')}
+          />
+          <ActionButton
+            icon={<Trash2 size={14} />}
+            label="Delete table"
+            danger
+            onClick={() => {
+              runAction('deleteTable');
+              setOpen(false);
+            }}
+          />
+        </div>
+      ) : null}
+    </div>
+  );
+};
+
+function ActionButton({
+  icon,
+  label,
+  danger = false,
+  onClick,
+}: {
+  icon: React.ReactNode;
+  label: string;
+  danger?: boolean;
+  onClick: () => void;
+}) {
+  return (
+    <button
+      type="button"
+      onClick={onClick}
+      style={{
+        height: 28,
+        borderRadius: 6,
+        border: danger ? '1px solid #fecaca' : '1px solid #cbd5e1',
+        background: danger ? '#fef2f2' : '#ffffff',
+        color: danger ? '#b91c1c' : '#334155',
+        display: 'flex',
+        alignItems: 'center',
+        gap: 7,
+        padding: '0 8px',
+        fontSize: 11,
+        fontWeight: 700,
+        cursor: 'pointer',
+      }}
+    >
+      {icon}
+      {label}
+    </button>
+  );
+}
```

```diff
+import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import { createEditor } from 'lexical';
+import {
+  TableCellNode,
+  TableNode,
+  TableRowNode,
+} from '@lexical/table';
+import { insertTable } from './tableHelpers';
+import {
+  applyWriterTableEditAction,
+  getWriterTableSelectionState,
+} from './tableEditHelpers';
+
+function createTableEditor() {
+  return createEditor({
+    namespace: 'writer-table-edit-test',
+    nodes: [
+      TableNode,
+      TableRowNode,
+      TableCellNode,
+    ],
+    onError(error) {
+      throw error;
+    },
+  });
+}
+
+describe('tableEditHelpers', () => {
+  it('inserts row below active table cell', async () => {
+    const editor = createTableEditor();
+
+    editor.update(() => {
+      insertTable(editor, 2, 2);
+    });
+
+    await waitForEditorUpdate();
+
+    const changed = applyWriterTableEditAction(editor, 'insertRowBelow');
+
+    await waitForEditorUpdate();
+
+    const state = getWriterTableSelectionState(editor);
+
+    expect(changed).toBe(true);
+    expect(state.insideTable).toBe(true);
+    expect(state.rowCount).toBe(3);
+    expect(state.columnCount).toBe(2);
+  });
+
+  it('inserts column right active table cell', async () => {
+    const editor = createTableEditor();
+
+    editor.update(() => {
+      insertTable(editor, 2, 2);
+    });
+
+    await waitForEditorUpdate();
+
+    const changed = applyWriterTableEditAction(editor, 'insertColumnRight');
+
+    await waitForEditorUpdate();
+
+    const state = getWriterTableSelectionState(editor);
+
+    expect(changed).toBe(true);
+    expect(state.insideTable).toBe(true);
+    expect(state.rowCount).toBe(2);
+    expect(state.columnCount).toBe(3);
+  });
+
+  it('deletes table', async () => {
+    const editor = createTableEditor();
+
+    editor.update(() => {
+      insertTable(editor, 2, 2);
+    });
+
+    await waitForEditorUpdate();
+
+    const changed = applyWriterTableEditAction(editor, 'deleteTable');
+
+    await waitForEditorUpdate();
+
+    const state = getWriterTableSelectionState(editor);
+
+    expect(changed).toBe(true);
+    expect(state.insideTable).toBe(false);
+  });
+});
+
+function waitForEditorUpdate(): Promise<void> {
+  return new Promise((resolve) => {
+    setTimeout(resolve, 0);
+  });
+}
```

### If `insertTable(editor)` inside `editor.update()` causes nested-update issues

Use this alternate in tests:

```ts
insertTable(editor, 2, 2);
await waitForEditorUpdate();
```

because the patched `insertTable()` already calls `editor.focus()` and `editor.update()` internally.
