# Patch A — Add clipboard table parser

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableClipboardParser.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableClipboardParser.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableClipboardParser.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableClipboardParser.ts
@@
+export interface ParsedClipboardTable {
+  rows: string[][];
+  rowCount: number;
+  columnCount: number;
+  delimiter: 'tab' | 'comma' | 'semicolon' | 'line';
+}
+
+const MAX_ROWS = 50;
+const MAX_COLUMNS = 20;
+
+export function parseClipboardTableText(text: string): ParsedClipboardTable {
+  const normalized = text
+    .replace(/\r\n/g, '\n')
+    .replace(/\r/g, '\n')
+    .trim();
+
+  if (!normalized) {
+    return {
+      rows: [],
+      rowCount: 0,
+      columnCount: 0,
+      delimiter: 'line',
+    };
+  }
+
+  const delimiter = detectDelimiter(normalized);
+  const rows = delimiter === 'comma' || delimiter === 'semicolon'
+    ? parseDelimitedRows(normalized, delimiter === 'comma' ? ',' : ';')
+    : parseSimpleRows(normalized, delimiter === 'tab' ? '\t' : '\n');
+
+  const cleaned = normalizeRows(rows);
+  const columnCount = cleaned.length === 0
+    ? 0
+    : Math.max(...cleaned.map((row) => row.length));
+
+  return {
+    rows: cleaned,
+    rowCount: cleaned.length,
+    columnCount,
+    delimiter,
+  };
+}
+
+function detectDelimiter(text: string): ParsedClipboardTable['delimiter'] {
+  if (text.includes('\t')) return 'tab';
+
+  const firstLine = text.split('\n')[0] ?? '';
+  const commaCount = countUnquoted(firstLine, ',');
+  const semicolonCount = countUnquoted(firstLine, ';');
+
+  if (commaCount > 0 && commaCount >= semicolonCount) return 'comma';
+  if (semicolonCount > 0) return 'semicolon';
+
+  return 'line';
+}
+
+function parseSimpleRows(text: string, delimiter: '\t' | '\n'): string[][] {
+  if (delimiter === '\n') {
+    return text
+      .split('\n')
+      .map((line) => [line]);
+  }
+
+  return text
+    .split('\n')
+    .map((line) => line.split('\t'));
+}
+
+function parseDelimitedRows(text: string, delimiter: ',' | ';'): string[][] {
+  const rows: string[][] = [];
+  let currentRow: string[] = [];
+  let currentCell = '';
+  let quoted = false;
+
+  for (let index = 0; index < text.length; index += 1) {
+    const char = text[index];
+    const next = text[index + 1];
+
+    if (char === '"' && quoted && next === '"') {
+      currentCell += '"';
+      index += 1;
+      continue;
+    }
+
+    if (char === '"') {
+      quoted = !quoted;
+      continue;
+    }
+
+    if (char === delimiter && !quoted) {
+      currentRow.push(currentCell);
+      currentCell = '';
+      continue;
+    }
+
+    if (char === '\n' && !quoted) {
+      currentRow.push(currentCell);
+      rows.push(currentRow);
+      currentRow = [];
+      currentCell = '';
+      continue;
+    }
+
+    currentCell += char;
+  }
+
+  currentRow.push(currentCell);
+  rows.push(currentRow);
+
+  return rows;
+}
+
+function normalizeRows(rows: string[][]): string[][] {
+  const trimmedRows = rows
+    .map((row) =>
+      row
+        .slice(0, MAX_COLUMNS)
+        .map((cell) => cell.trim()),
+    )
+    .filter((row) => row.some((cell) => cell.length > 0))
+    .slice(0, MAX_ROWS);
+
+  const columnCount = trimmedRows.length === 0
+    ? 0
+    : Math.max(...trimmedRows.map((row) => row.length));
+
+  return trimmedRows.map((row) => {
+    const padded = [...row];
+    while (padded.length < columnCount) {
+      padded.push('');
+    }
+    return padded;
+  });
+}
+
+function countUnquoted(text: string, target: ',' | ';'): number {
+  let quoted = false;
+  let count = 0;
+
+  for (let index = 0; index < text.length; index += 1) {
+    const char = text[index];
+    const next = text[index + 1];
+
+    if (char === '"' && quoted && next === '"') {
+      index += 1;
+      continue;
+    }
+
+    if (char === '"') {
+      quoted = !quoted;
+      continue;
+    }
+
+    if (char === target && !quoted) {
+      count += 1;
+    }
+  }
+
+  return count;
+}
```

```diff
     case 'insertTable':
```

# Patch D — Add Paste Table ribbon control

## New file

`frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterPasteTableControl.tsx`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterPasteTableControl.tsx b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterPasteTableControl.tsx
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterPasteTableControl.tsx
@@
+import React from 'react';
+import { ClipboardPaste } from 'lucide-react';
+import { useActiveEditorStore } from '@/core/writer-v2/activeEditorStore';
+import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';
+import { parseClipboardTableText } from '@/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableClipboardParser';
+import { runWriterEditorCommand } from '@/core/writer-v2/writerEditorCommandBridge';
+
+export const WriterPasteTableControl: React.FC = () => {
+  const activeEditor = useActiveEditorStore((state) => state.activeEditor);
+  const sourceBytes = useSessionStore((state) => state.workingBytes ?? state.originalBytes);
+  const beginExportOperation = useSessionStore((state) => state.beginExportOperation);
+  const completeSaveOperation = useSessionStore((state) => state.completeSaveOperation);
+  const failSaveOperation = useSessionStore((state) => state.failSaveOperation);
+
+  const disabled = !sourceBytes;
+
+  const pasteTable = async () => {
+    if (!sourceBytes) {
+      failSaveOperation('Open a PDF before pasting a Writer table.');
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (!navigator.clipboard?.readText) {
+      failSaveOperation('Clipboard text access is not available in this browser.');
+      return;
+    }
+
+    try {
+      beginExportOperation('Pasting table into Writer...');
+
+      const text = await navigator.clipboard.readText();
+      const parsed = parseClipboardTableText(text);
+
+      if (parsed.rows.length === 0) {
+        failSaveOperation('Clipboard does not contain table-like text.');
+        return;
+      }
+
+      const result = await runWriterEditorCommand({
+        activeEditor,
+        commandName: 'paste table',
+        command: {
+          type: 'insertTableData',
+          data: parsed.rows,
+        },
+      });
+
+      if (result.changed) {
+        completeSaveOperation(
+          `Pasted ${parsed.rowCount}x${parsed.columnCount} table into Writer.`,
+        );
+      } else {
+        failSaveOperation(result.message);
+      }
+    } catch (error) {
+      failSaveOperation(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
+    }
+  };
+
+  return (
+    <button
+      type="button"
+      aria-label="Paste table"
+      title="Paste table from clipboard into the active Writer rich box"
+      disabled={disabled}
+      onClick={() => {
+        void pasteTable();
+      }}
+      style={{
+        height: 30,
+        borderRadius: 7,
+        border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
+        background: '#ffffff',
+        color: disabled ? '#94a3b8' : '#334155',
+        display: 'inline-flex',
+        alignItems: 'center',
+        gap: 6,
+        padding: '0 8px',
+        fontSize: 11,
+        fontWeight: 800,
+        cursor: disabled ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
+      }}
+    >
+      <ClipboardPaste size={15} />
+      Paste Table
+    </button>
+  );
+};
```

# Patch F — Parser tests

## New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableClipboardParser.test.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableClipboardParser.test.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableClipboardParser.test.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableClipboardParser.test.ts
@@
+import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import { parseClipboardTableText } from './tableClipboardParser';
+
+describe('tableClipboardParser', () => {
+  it('parses tab-separated spreadsheet data', () => {
+    const parsed = parseClipboardTableText('A\tB\n1\t2\n3\t4');
+
+    expect(parsed.delimiter).toBe('tab');
+    expect(parsed.rowCount).toBe(3);
+    expect(parsed.columnCount).toBe(2);
+    expect(parsed.rows).toEqual([
+      ['A', 'B'],
+      ['1', '2'],
+      ['3', '4'],
+    ]);
+  });
+
+  it('parses quoted CSV data', () => {
+    const parsed = parseClipboardTableText('"Name","Note"\n"Pump","A, B, C"\n"Valve","OK"');
+
+    expect(parsed.delimiter).toBe('comma');
+    expect(parsed.rows).toEqual([
+      ['Name', 'Note'],
+      ['Pump', 'A, B, C'],
+      ['Valve', 'OK'],
+    ]);
+  });
+
+  it('parses semicolon-separated data', () => {
+    const parsed = parseClipboardTableText('A;B\n1;2');
+
+    expect(parsed.delimiter).toBe('semicolon');
+    expect(parsed.rows).toEqual([
+      ['A', 'B'],
+      ['1', '2'],
+    ]);
+  });
+
+  it('treats multiline text as one-column table', () => {
+    const parsed = parseClipboardTableText('Line 1\nLine 2');
+
+    expect(parsed.delimiter).toBe('line');
+    expect(parsed.rows).toEqual([
+      ['Line 1'],
+      ['Line 2'],
+    ]);
+  });
+
+  it('clamps rows and columns', () => {
+    const row = Array.from({ length: 30 }, (_, index) => `C${index}`).join('\t');
+    const text = Array.from({ length: 80 }, () => row).join('\n');
+
+    const parsed = parseClipboardTableText(text);
+
+    expect(parsed.rowCount).toBe(50);
+    expect(parsed.columnCount).toBe(20);
+  });
+});
```

# Patch G — Component test

## New file

`frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterPasteTableControl.test.tsx`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterPasteTableControl.test.tsx b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterPasteTableControl.test.tsx
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/components/writer-v2/WriterPasteTableControl.test.tsx
@@
+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+import { fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
+import { PDFDocument } from 'pdf-lib';
+import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';
+import { useRichEditorCommandQueue } from '@/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue';
+import { useWriterV2Store } from '@/core/writer-v2/writerV2Store';
+import { WriterPasteTableControl } from './WriterPasteTableControl';
+
+async function samplePdf(): Promise<Uint8Array> {
+  const pdf = await PDFDocument.create();
+  pdf.addPage([612, 792]);
+  return pdf.save();
+}
+
+describe('WriterPasteTableControl', () => {
+  beforeEach(async () => {
+    useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().clear();
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().clearWriterV2();
+    useSessionStore.getState().openDocument({
+      documentKey: 'doc-1',
+      fileName: 'sample.pdf',
+      bytes: await samplePdf(),
+      pageCount: 1,
+    });
+
+    Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'clipboard', {
+      configurable: true,
+      value: {
+        readText: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue('A\tB\n1\t2'),
+      },
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('queues parsed clipboard table and opens rich editor', async () => {
+    render(<WriterPasteTableControl />);
+
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Paste table' }));
+
+    await waitFor(() => {
+      expect(useWriterV2Store.getState().elements).toHaveLength(1);
+    });
+
+    expect(useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().pending).toEqual([
+      {
+        type: 'insertTableData',
+        data: [
+          ['A', 'B'],
+          ['1', '2'],
+        ],
+      },
+    ]);
+  });
+
+  it('is disabled when no PDF is open', () => {
+    useSessionStore.getState().clearDocument();
+
+    render(<WriterPasteTableControl />);
+
+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Paste table' })).toBeDisabled();
+  });
+});
```

```diff
+function findParentTableCell(node: LexicalNode): TableCellNode | null {
   let current = node;
@@
 function deleteColumn(context: WriterTableContext): void {
@@
   nextSelectionCell?.selectStart();
 }
+
+function clearCell(cell: TableCellNode): void {
+  cell.clear();
+  cell.append($createParagraphNode());
+  cell.selectStart();
+}
+
+function clearTableContents(context: WriterTableContext): void {
+  for (const row of context.rows) {
+    const cells = row.getChildren().filter($isTableCellNode);
+
+    for (const cell of cells) {
+      cell.clear();
+      cell.append($createParagraphNode());
+    }
+  }
+
+  context.cellNode.selectStart();
+}
+
+function toggleHeaderRow(context: WriterTableContext): void {
+  const firstRow = context.rows[0];
+  if (!firstRow) return;
+
+  const currentlyHeader = isHeaderRow(firstRow);
+  const nextHeaderStyle = currentlyHeader
+    ? TableCellHeaderStates.NO_STATUS
+    : TableCellHeaderStates.ROW;
+
+  const cells = firstRow.getChildren().filter($isTableCellNode);
+
+  for (const cell of cells) {
+    setCellHeaderStyle(cell, nextHeaderStyle);
+  }
+
+  const firstCell = firstRow.getFirstChild();
+  if ($isTableCellNode(firstCell)) {
+    firstCell.selectStart();
+  }
+}
+
+function isHeaderRow(row: TableRowNode | undefined): boolean {
+  if (!row) return false;
+
+  const cells = row.getChildren().filter($isTableCellNode);
+  if (cells.length === 0) return false;
+
+  return cells.every((cell) => {
+    const styles = getCellHeaderStyle(cell);
+    return (styles & TableCellHeaderStates.ROW) === TableCellHeaderStates.ROW;
+  });
+}
+
+function getCellHeaderStyle(cell: TableCellNode): number {
+  const maybeCell = cell as TableCellNode & {
+    getHeaderStyles?: () => number;
+  };
+
+  return maybeCell.getHeaderStyles?.() ?? TableCellHeaderStates.NO_STATUS;
+}
+
+function setCellHeaderStyle(
+  cell: TableCellNode,
+  headerStyle: TableCellHeaderStates,
+): void {
+  const maybeCell = cell as TableCellNode & {
+    setHeaderStyles?: (style: TableCellHeaderStates) => void;
+  };
+
+  maybeCell.setHeaderStyles?.(headerStyle);
+}
@@
 function emptyTableState(): WriterTableSelectionState {
   return {
     insideTable: false,
     rowIndex: -1,
     columnIndex: -1,
     rowCount: 0,
     columnCount: 0,
+    headerRow: false,
   };
 }
```

# Patch C — Add header-row export styling

This makes header-row changes visible after flattened export. The current rich table export treats row `0` as header in the export renderer. Keep that behavior, but ensure rich renderer preserves header state later. For now, export row `0` as header is acceptable and deterministic.

If your `richTextRenderer.ts` already parses `tablecell` header state, skip this. Otherwise add `header` to parsed cell structure.

## `frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/richTextRenderer.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/richTextRenderer.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/richTextRenderer.ts
--- a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/richTextRenderer.ts
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/export/richTextRenderer.ts
@@
 export interface PdfTableCell {
   children: PdfBlock[];
+  header?: boolean;
 }
@@
 function parseTableCellNode(node: SerializedLexicalNode): PdfTableCell {
   return {
     children: parseBlockChildren(node.children ?? []),
+    header: Boolean(
+      typeof node.headerState === 'number'
+        ? node.headerState
+        : typeof node.__headerState === 'number'
+          ? node.__headerState
+          : 0,
+    ),
   };
 }
```

If your file uses a different parser shape, apply this logic where `tablecell` nodes are converted into `PdfTableCell`.

Replace the incomplete `selectFirstTableCell()` helper above with this full helper:

```ts
import { $getRoot } from 'lexical';
import {
  $isTableCellNode,
  $isTableNode,
  $isTableRowNode,
} from '@lexical/table';

function selectFirstTableCell(editor: ReturnType<typeof createTableEditor>): void {
  editor.update(() => {
    const table = $getRoot()
      .getChildren()
      .find($isTableNode);

    if (!table) return;

    const row = table
      .getChildren()
      .find($isTableRowNode);

    if (!row) return;

    const cell = row
      .getChildren()
      .find($isTableCellNode);

    cell?.selectStart();
  });
}
```

So the import block becomes:

```diff
@@
-import { createEditor } from 'lexical';
+import { $getRoot, createEditor } from 'lexical';
 import {
+  $isTableCellNode,
+  $isTableNode,
+  $isTableRowNode,
   TableCellNode,
   TableNode,
   TableRowNode,
 } from '@lexical/table';
```

## Patch 2 — Add matrix test

### New file

`frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerCommandMatrix.test.ts`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerCommandMatrix.test.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerCommandMatrix.test.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerCommandMatrix.test.ts
@@
+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import { PDFDocument } from 'pdf-lib';
+import { useSessionStore } from '@/core/session/store';
+import { useRichEditorCommandQueue } from './richEditorCommandQueue';
+import { useWriterV2Store } from './writerV2Store';
+import { runWriterEditorCommand } from './writerEditorCommandBridge';
+import { HIGH_VALUE_WRITER_COMMANDS } from './writerCommandMatrix';
+
+async function samplePdf(): Promise<Uint8Array> {
+  const pdf = await PDFDocument.create();
+  pdf.addPage([612, 792]);
+  return pdf.save();
+}
+
+describe('writer command matrix', () => {
+  beforeEach(async () => {
+    useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().clear();
+    useWriterV2Store.getState().clearWriterV2();
+    useSessionStore.getState().openDocument({
+      documentKey: 'doc-1',
+      fileName: 'sample.pdf',
+      bytes: await samplePdf(),
+      pageCount: 1,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it.each(HIGH_VALUE_WRITER_COMMANDS)(
+    'queues and opens a rich box when no editor is active: $label',
+    async ({ label, command }) => {
+      const result = await runWriterEditorCommand({
+        activeEditor: null,
+        commandName: label,
+        command,
+      });
+
+      const writerState = useWriterV2Store.getState();
+      const pending = useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().pending;
+
+      expect(result.changed).toBe(true);
+      expect(result.queued).toBe(true);
+      expect(result.editorElementId).toBeTruthy();
+      expect(writerState.elements).toHaveLength(1);
+      expect(writerState.elements[0].type).toBe('richTextBox');
+      expect(writerState.editingId).toBe(writerState.elements[0].id);
+      expect(pending).toEqual([command]);
+
+      useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().clear();
+      useWriterV2Store.getState().clearWriterV2();
+      useSessionStore.getState().openDocument({
+        documentKey: 'doc-1',
+        fileName: 'sample.pdf',
+        bytes: await samplePdf(),
+        pageCount: 1,
+      });
+    },
+  );
+
+  it.each(HIGH_VALUE_WRITER_COMMANDS)(
+    'does not silently queue when no PDF is open: $label',
+    async ({ label, command }) => {
+      useSessionStore.getState().clearDocument();
+      useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().clear();
+      useWriterV2Store.getState().clearWriterV2();
+
+      const result = await runWriterEditorCommand({
+        activeEditor: null,
+        commandName: label,
+        command,
+      });
+
+      expect(result.changed).toBe(false);
+      expect(result.queued).toBe(false);
+      expect(result.message).toMatch(/open a pdf/i);
+      expect(useRichEditorCommandQueue.getState().pending).toEqual([]);
+      expect(useWriterV2Store.getState().elements).toHaveLength(0);
+    },
+  );
+
+  it('keeps command matrix ids unique', () => {
+    const ids = HIGH_VALUE_WRITER_COMMANDS.map((item) => item.id);
+
+    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);
+  });
+});
```

```diff
+      'src/core/writer-v2/writerCommandMatrix.test.ts',
+      '--pool=forks',
+      '--maxWorkers=1',
+    ],
+  },
+];
+
+const results = [];
+let passed = true;
+
+for (const item of commands) {
+  const result = spawnSync(item.command, item.args, {
+    cwd: root,
+    encoding: 'utf8',
+    shell: process.platform === 'win32',
+  });
+
+  const ok = result.status === 0;
+
+  if (!ok && !item.optional) {
+    passed = false;
+  }
+
+  results.push({
+    label: item.label,
+    command: [item.command, ...item.args].join(' '),
+    status: result.status,
+    optional: Boolean(item.optional),
+    passed: ok,
+    stdoutTail: tail(result.stdout),
+    stderrTail: tail(result.stderr),
+  });
+}
+
+mkdirSync(reportDir, {
+  recursive: true,
+});
+
+writeFileSync(
+  reportPath,
+  JSON.stringify(
+    {
+      name: 'writer-command-matrix-certification',
+      startedAt,
+      finishedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
+      passed,
+      results,
+    },
+    null,
+    2,
+  ),
+);
+
+if (!passed) {
+  console.error(`Writer command matrix certification FAILED. Report: ${reportPath}`);
+  process.exit(1);
+}
+
+console.log(`Writer command matrix certification PASSED. Report: ${reportPath}`);
+
+function tail(value = '') {
+  return value
+    .split('\n')
+    .slice(-40)
+    .join('\n')
+    .trim();
+}
```

# Required gates

```bash
cd frontend

npm run typecheck
npm run audit:writer-no-noop
npx vitest run src/core/writer-v2/writerCommandMatrix.test.ts --pool=forks --maxWorkers=1
npm run certify:writer-command-matrix
npm run build
npm run certify:writer-overlay-v2
```

# Why this is high value

This prevents regression of the exact bug you flagged:

```txt
Table, bullets, indent, link, font, color, heading, etc.
look clickable but do nothing.
```

After this patch, those commands are certified through a single matrix:

```txt
No active editor + PDF open
→ command must queue
→ richTextBox must open/create
→ editingId must be set

No PDF open
→ command must fail explicitly
→ queue must be empty
→ no hidden object creation
```

That is higher ROI than adding more table micro-tools before the command path is certified.
--------------------------------------------------
Proceeding with only high-value work.

# WR2-HV.2 — Command coverage audit: no un-certified Writer commands

The previous slice certified the command matrix. This slice prevents future regression:

```txt
Any high-value Writer command used by the ribbon/toolbar
must be present in the command matrix.
```

This is high value because it stops the same class of bug from returning when new buttons are added.

Repo-grounding:

* `package.json` already has CI-safe Vitest via `test:ci`: `vitest run --pool=forks --maxWorkers=1`. 
* `package.json` already has Writer V2 certification scripts. 
* The current `WriterRibbon.tsx` still has many direct command paths and active-editor branches in the fetched source, so this audit is necessary until all ribbon commands are migrated.

```diff
+export const WRITER_COMMAND_IDS = [
+  'insert-table',
+  'paste-table-data',
+  'bullet-list',
+  'numbered-list',
+  'checklist',
+  'remove-list',
+  'indent',
+  'outdent',
+  'bold',
+  'italic',
+  'underline',
+  'strikethrough',
+  'superscript',

+  'paragraph-spacing',
+  'link',
+  'remove-link',
+  'horizontal-rule',
+  'quote-block',
+  'code-block',
+  'clear-formatting',
+  'insert-image',
+] as const;
+
+export type WriterCommandId = typeof WRITER_COMMAND_IDS[number];
+
+export function isWriterCommandId(value: string): value is WriterCommandId {
+  return (WRITER_COMMAND_IDS as readonly string[]).includes(value);
+}
```

# Patch 3 — Add coverage audit script

## New file

`frontend/scripts/audit-writer-command-coverage.mjs`

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/scripts/audit-writer-command-coverage.mjs b/frontend/scripts/audit-writer-command-coverage.mjs
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/scripts/audit-writer-command-coverage.mjs
@@
+import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { resolve } from 'node:path';
+
+const root = process.cwd();
+const idsSource = readFileSync(
+  resolve(root, 'src/core/writer-v2/writerCommandIds.ts'),
+  'utf8',
+);
+const matrixSource = readFileSync(
+  resolve(root, 'src/core/writer-v2/writerCommandMatrix.ts'),
+  'utf8',
+);
+const ribbonSource = readFileSync(
+  resolve(root, 'src/components/writer-v2/WriterRibbon.tsx'),
+  'utf8',
+);
+
+const declaredIds = extractArrayStringValues(idsSource, 'WRITER_COMMAND_IDS');
+const matrixIds = extractObjectPropertyStrings(matrixSource, 'id');
+const ribbonIds = extractDataCommandIds(ribbonSource);
+
+const failures = [];
+
+for (const id of declaredIds) {
+  if (!matrixIds.has(id)) {
+    failures.push(`Declared command id missing from HIGH_VALUE_WRITER_COMMANDS: ${id}`);
+  }
+}
+
+for (const id of matrixIds) {
+  if (!declaredIds.has(id)) {
+    failures.push(`Matrix command id is not declared in WRITER_COMMAND_IDS: ${id}`);
+  }
+}
+
+for (const id of ribbonIds) {
+  if (!declaredIds.has(id)) {
+    failures.push(`Ribbon uses unknown data-writer-command-id: ${id}`);
+  }
+
+  if (!matrixIds.has(id)) {
+    failures.push(`Ribbon command id is not covered by command matrix: ${id}`);
+  }
+}
+
+if (ribbonIds.size === 0) {
+  failures.push('No data-writer-command-id attributes found in WriterRibbon.tsx.');
+}
+
+if (failures.length > 0) {
+  console.error('Writer command coverage audit failed:');
+  for (const failure of failures) {
+    console.error(`- ${failure}`);
+  }
+  process.exit(1);
+}
+
+console.log(
+  `Writer command coverage audit passed. declared=${declaredIds.size}, matrix=${matrixIds.size}, ribbon=${ribbonIds.size}`,
+);
+
+function extractArrayStringValues(source, name) {
+  const match = source.match(new RegExp(`export\\s+const\\s+${name}\\s*=\\s*\\[([\\s\\S]*?)\\]\\s+as\\s+const`));
+  if (!match) return new Set();
+
+  return new Set(
+    [...match[1].matchAll(/'([^']+)'/g)].map((item) => item[1]),
+  );
+}
+
+function extractObjectPropertyStrings(source, property) {
+  return new Set(
+    [...source.matchAll(new RegExp(`${property}\\s*:\\s*'([^']+)'`, 'g'))].map(
+      (item) => item[1],
+    ),
+  );
+}
+
+function extractDataCommandIds(source) {
+  const ids = new Set();
+
+  for (const match of source.matchAll(/data-writer-command-id="([^"]+)"/g)) {
+    ids.add(match[1]);
+  }
+
+  for (const match of source.matchAll(/data-writer-command-id=\{'([^']+)'\}/g)) {
+    ids.add(match[1]);
+  }
+
+  return ids;
+}
```

```diff
 interface RibbonButtonProps {

 }
 
 const RibbonButton: React.FC<RibbonButtonProps> = ({

   disabled = false,

 }) => (

```

# Patch 7 — Add direct test for declared IDs

## New file

```diff
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null

@@
+import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import {
+  isWriterCommandId,
+  WRITER_COMMAND_IDS,
+} from './writerCommandIds';
+
+describe('writerCommandIds', () => {
+  it('keeps command ids unique', () => {
+    expect(new Set(WRITER_COMMAND_IDS).size).toBe(WRITER_COMMAND_IDS.length);
+  });
+
+  it('recognizes valid command ids', () => {
+    expect(isWriterCommandId('insert-table')).toBe(true);
+    expect(isWriterCommandId('bullet-list')).toBe(true);
+    expect(isWriterCommandId('not-a-command')).toBe(false);
+  });
+});
```

```txt
Before:
A developer can add a new WriterRibbon command button without adding it to certification.

After:
Every visible high-value WriterRibbon command must have:
- data-writer-command-id
- declared command ID
- matrix coverage
- no silent no-op audit pass

This is a release-quality regression guard, not UI polish.
```

# WR2-HV.3 — CI release gate for Writer V2 rich authoring

This makes Writer V2 failures visible in GitHub, not only local runs.

Current repo already has `typecheck`, `test:ci`, and Writer V2 certification scripts in `frontend/package.json`.  

This patch adds one release-grade aggregate certification command and one GitHub Actions workflow.

---

## Patch 1 — Add aggregate Writer rich authoring certifier

### New file

```diff
diff --git a/frontend/scripts/certify-writer-rich-authoring.mjs b/frontend/scripts/certify-writer-rich-authoring.mjs
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/scripts/certify-writer-rich-authoring.mjs
@@
+import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { resolve } from 'node:path';
+import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
+
+const root = process.cwd();
+const reportDir = resolve(root, 'reports');
+const reportPath = resolve(reportDir, 'writer-rich-authoring-certification.json');
+
+const startedAt = new Date().toISOString();
+
+const commands = [
+  {
+    label: 'Typecheck',
+    command: 'npm',
+    args: ['run', 'typecheck'],
+  },
+  {
+    label: 'Mojibake audit',
+    command: 'npm',
+    args: ['run', 'audit:no-mojibake'],
+  },
+  {
+    label: 'Writer no silent no-op audit',
+    command: 'npm',
+    args: ['run', 'audit:writer-no-noop'],
+  },
+  {
+    label: 'Writer command coverage audit',
+    command: 'npm',
+    args: ['run', 'audit:writer-command-coverage'],
+  },
+  {
+    label: 'Writer rich command and table tests',
+    command: 'npx',
+    args: [
+      'vitest',
+      'run',
+      'src/core/writer-v2/writerCommandIds.test.ts',
+      'src/core/writer-v2/writerCommandMatrix.test.ts',
+      'src/core/writer-v2/richEditorCommandQueue.test.ts',
+      'src/core/writer-v2/writerEditorCommandBridge.test.ts',
+      'src/core/writer-v2/writerEditorCommandApplier.test.ts',
+      'src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/listCommandHelpers.test.ts',
+      'src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableHelpers.test.ts',
+      'src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableClipboardParser.test.ts',
+      'src/core/writer-v2/lexical-nodes/tableEditHelpers.test.ts',
+      'src/components/writer-v2/WriterPasteTableControl.test.tsx',
+      'src/components/writer-v2/WriterTableActionsControl.test.tsx',
+      '--pool=forks',
+      '--maxWorkers=1',
+    ],
+  },
+  {
+    label: 'Writer command matrix certification',
+    command: 'npm',
+    args: ['run', 'certify:writer-command-matrix'],
+  },
+  {
+    label: 'Writer overlay V2 certification',
+    command: 'npm',
+    args: ['run', 'certify:writer-overlay-v2'],
+  },
+  {
+    label: 'Production build',
+    command: 'npm',
+    args: ['run', 'build'],
+  },
+];
+
+const results = [];
+let passed = true;
+
+for (const item of commands) {
+  const result = spawnSync(item.command, item.args, {
+    cwd: root,
+    encoding: 'utf8',
+    shell: process.platform === 'win32',
+  });
+
+  const ok = result.status === 0;
+  if (!ok) {
+    passed = false;
+  }
+
+  results.push({
+    label: item.label,
+    command: [item.command, ...item.args].join(' '),
+    status: result.status,
+    passed: ok,
+    stdoutTail: tail(result.stdout),
+    stderrTail: tail(result.stderr),
+  });
+
+  if (!ok) {
+    break;
+  }
+}
+
+mkdirSync(reportDir, {
+  recursive: true,
+});
+
+writeFileSync(
+  reportPath,
+  JSON.stringify(
+    {
+      name: 'writer-rich-authoring-certification',
+      startedAt,
+      finishedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
+      passed,
+      results,
+    },
+    null,
+    2,
+  ),
+);
+
+if (!passed) {
+  console.error(`Writer rich authoring certification FAILED. Report: ${reportPath}`);
+  process.exit(1);
+}
+
+console.log(`Writer rich authoring certification PASSED. Report: ${reportPath}`);
+
+function tail(value = '') {
+  return value
+    .split('\n')
+    .slice(-60)
+    .join('\n')
+    .trim();
+}
```

# Expected result

```txt
Before:
Writer V2 command reliability can regress unless someone manually runs the right tests.

After:
GitHub PR/push blocks on:
- no silent no-op audit
- command coverage audit
- command matrix tests
- table/list/paste tests
- Writer overlay certification
- production build
```

This is high-value because it converts the Writer V2 fixes into an enforceable release gate.

```diff
+export interface WriterRibbonMoreProps {
+  id: string;
+  label: string;
+  children: React.ReactNode;
+}
+
+export const WriterRibbonMore: React.FC<WriterRibbonMoreProps> = ({
+  id,
+  label,
+  children,
+}) => {
+  const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false);
+
+  React.useEffect(() => {
+    if (!open) return;
+
+    const close = (event: MouseEvent) => {
+      const target = event.target as HTMLElement | null;
+      if (target?.closest(`[data-writer-ribbon-more="${id}"]`)) return;
+      setOpen(false);
+    };
+
+    window.addEventListener('mousedown', close);
+    return () => window.removeEventListener('mousedown', close);
+  }, [id, open]);
+
+  return (
+    <div
+      data-writer-ribbon-more={id}
+      style={{
+        position: 'relative',
+        display: 'inline-flex',
+      }}
+    >
+      <button
+        type="button"
+        aria-label={label}
+        title={label}
+        onClick={() => setOpen((value) => !value)}
+        style={{
+          height: 30,
+          borderRadius: 7,
+          border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
+          background: open ? '#eff6ff' : '#ffffff',
+          color: open ? '#1d4ed8' : '#334155',
+          display: 'inline-flex',
+          alignItems: 'center',
+          gap: 5,
+          padding: '0 8px',
+          fontSize: 11,
+          fontWeight: 800,
+          cursor: 'pointer',
+        }}
+      >
+        {label}
+        <ChevronDown size={13} />
+      </button>
+
+      {open ? (
+        <div
+          role="dialog"
+          aria-label={`${label} menu`}
+          style={{
+            position: 'absolute',
+            top: 34,
+            left: 0,
+            zIndex: 280,
+            minWidth: 240,
+            maxWidth: 320,
+            padding: 10,
+            borderRadius: 10,
+            border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
+            background: '#ffffff',
+            boxShadow: '0 14px 36px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.18)',
+            display: 'grid',
+            gap: 8,
+          }}
+        >
+          {children}
+        </div>
+      ) : null}
+    </div>
+  );
+};
+

+  label: string;
+  children: React.ReactNode;

+      color: '#475569',
+    }}
+  >
+    <span style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>{label}</span>
+    {children}
+  </label>
+);


diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRibbonDensity.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRibbonDensity.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRibbonDensity.ts
@@
+export const WRITER_RIBBON_DENSITY_LIMITS = {
+  maxGroups: 5,
+  maxAlwaysVisibleCommandIds: 24,
+  requiredGroups: ['writer', 'text', 'paragraph', 'insert', 'context'] as const,
+};
+
+export type WriterRibbonGroupId =
+  typeof WRITER_RIBBON_DENSITY_LIMITS.requiredGroups[number];
+
+export function uniqueValues(values: string[]): string[] {
+  return [...new Set(values)];
+}
+
+export function validateWriterRibbonDensity(input: {
+  groups: string[];
+  visibleCommandIds: string[];
+}): {
+  ok: boolean;
+  issues: string[];
+} {
+  const issues: string[] = [];
+  const groups = uniqueValues(input.groups);
+  const commandIds = uniqueValues(input.visibleCommandIds);
+
+  for (const required of WRITER_RIBBON_DENSITY_LIMITS.requiredGroups) {
+    if (!groups.includes(required)) {
+      issues.push(`Missing required ribbon group: ${required}`);
+    }
+  }
+
+  if (groups.length > WRITER_RIBBON_DENSITY_LIMITS.maxGroups) {
+    issues.push(
+      `Too many always-visible ribbon groups: ${groups.length} > ${WRITER_RIBBON_DENSITY_LIMITS.maxGroups}`,
+    );
+  }
+
+  if (commandIds.length > WRITER_RIBBON_DENSITY_LIMITS.maxAlwaysVisibleCommandIds) {
+    issues.push(
+      `Too many always-visible Writer commands: ${commandIds.length} > ${WRITER_RIBBON_DENSITY_LIMITS.maxAlwaysVisibleCommandIds}`,
+    );
+  }
+
+  return {
+    ok: issues.length === 0,
+    issues,
+  };
+}


diff --git a/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRibbonDensity.test.ts b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRibbonDensity.test.ts
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/core/writer-v2/writerRibbonDensity.test.ts
@@
+import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import {
+  validateWriterRibbonDensity,
+  WRITER_RIBBON_DENSITY_LIMITS,
+} from './writerRibbonDensity';
+
+describe('writerRibbonDensity', () => {
+  it('accepts the intended five-group ribbon layout', () => {
+    const result = validateWriterRibbonDensity({
+      groups: [...WRITER_RIBBON_DENSITY_LIMITS.requiredGroups],
+      visibleCommandIds: [
+        'insert-table',
+        'paste-table-data',
+        'bullet-list',
+        'numbered-list',
+        'checklist',
+        'indent',
+        'outdent',
+        'bold',
+        'italic',
+        'underline',
+        'font-size',
+        'text-color',
+        'highlight',
+        'heading-1',
+        'heading-2',
+        'heading-3',
+        'align-left',
+        'align-center',
+        'align-right',
+        'link',
+        'insert-image',
+        'clear-formatting',
+      ],
+    });
+
+    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
+    expect(result.issues).toEqual([]);
+  });
+
+  it('rejects missing groups', () => {
+    const result = validateWriterRibbonDensity({
+      groups: ['writer', 'text'],
+      visibleCommandIds: [],
+    });
+
+    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
+    expect(result.issues).toContain('Missing required ribbon group: paragraph');
+  });
+
+  it('rejects too many visible commands', () => {
+    const result = validateWriterRibbonDensity({
+      groups: [...WRITER_RIBBON_DENSITY_LIMITS.requiredGroups],
+      visibleCommandIds: Array.from({ length: 25 }, (_, index) => `cmd-${index}`),
+    });
+
+    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
+    expect(result.issues[0]).toMatch(/Too many always-visible Writer commands/);
+  });
+});


diff --git a/frontend/scripts/audit-writer-ribbon-density.mjs b/frontend/scripts/audit-writer-ribbon-density.mjs
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/scripts/audit-writer-ribbon-density.mjs
@@
+import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { resolve } from 'node:path';
+
+const sourcePath = resolve(process.cwd(), 'src/components/writer-v2/WriterRibbon.tsx');
+const source = readFileSync(sourcePath, 'utf8');
+
+const requiredGroups = ['writer', 'text', 'paragraph', 'insert', 'context'];
+const maxGroups = 5;
+const maxAlwaysVisibleCommands = 24;
+
+const groupIds = unique([...source.matchAll(/data-writer-ribbon-group="([^"]+)"/g)].map((item) => item[1]));
+const commandIds = unique([...source.matchAll(/data-writer-command-id="([^"]+)"/g)].map((item) => item[1]));
+
+const issues = [];
+
+for (const group of requiredGroups) {
+  if (!groupIds.includes(group)) {
+    issues.push(`Missing required WriterRibbon group: ${group}`);
+  }
+}
+
+if (groupIds.length > maxGroups) {
+  issues.push(`Too many WriterRibbon groups: ${groupIds.length} > ${maxGroups}`);
+}
+
+if (commandIds.length > maxAlwaysVisibleCommands) {
+  issues.push(`Too many always-visible WriterRibbon command IDs: ${commandIds.length} > ${maxAlwaysVisibleCommands}`);
+}
+
+if (/data-writer-ribbon-group="(?:tools|misc|other|legacy|temp)"/i.test(source)) {
+  issues.push('Generic ribbon groups such as tools/misc/other/legacy/temp are not allowed.');
+}
+
+if (issues.length > 0) {
+  console.error('Writer ribbon density audit failed:');
+  for (const issue of issues) {
+    console.error(`- ${issue}`);
+  }
+  console.error(`groups=${JSON.stringify(groupIds)}`);
+  console.error(`commands=${commandIds.length}`);
+  process.exit(1);
+}
+
+console.log(`Writer ribbon density audit passed. groups=${groupIds.length}, visibleCommands=${commandIds.length}`);
+
+function unique(values) {
+  return [...new Set(values)];
+}
```
